<h2>Verb Details</h2>

### 1. damage <a id="1-damage"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *damagier* ("to damage, injure"), from *dam* ("damage"), from Latin *damnum* ("loss, harm, injury").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *damagier*（"损害，伤害"），源自 *dam*（"损害"），源自拉丁语 *damnum*（"损失，伤害，损伤"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause physical harm to something; to have a negative effect on something
- CN: 损坏；损害；对……产生负面影响

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with concrete and abstract objects: damage a building / one's reputation — 常与具体和抽象宾语连用：damage a building / one's reputation
- "Damage" as a verb is stronger than "harm" in the physical sense — 作动词时在物理意义上比 "harm" 语气更强
- Not used with "to" as a verb (e.g. NOT "damage to something" — that is the noun form) — 作动词时不与 "to" 连用（例如不说 "damage to something"——那是名词用法）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- damage the environment
- damage one's reputation
- damage relations
- damage property
- cause damage
- serious/severe damage
- damage beyond repair
- damage by fire
- damage from floods
- permanently damage

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 破坏环境
- 损害某人的声誉
- 破坏关系
- 损坏财产
- 造成损害
- 严重损害
- 损坏到无法修复
- 因火灾而损坏
- 因洪水而受损
- 永久性损害
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- damage to property
- damage by fire
- damage from floods
- damage in an accident
- damage through neglect

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对财产的损害
- 因火灾造成的损害
- 洪水造成的损害
- 在事故中受损
- 因疏忽而损坏
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The earthquake severely damaged many buildings in the city. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The scandal seriously damaged the politician's reputation. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Prolonged exposure to sunlight can damage the skin. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The virus damages the immune system, making patients vulnerable to infections. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Be careful not to damage the furniture when you move it.
- 介词搭配: The storm caused extensive damage to property along the coast.
- 介词搭配: Several paintings were damaged by fire during the museum blaze.
- 介词搭配: The crops suffered damage from floods after weeks of heavy rain.
- 介词搭配: The car was badly damaged in a collision on the highway.
- 介词搭配: The old building was damaged through years of neglect and lack of maintenance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 地震严重损坏了该市的许多建筑物。
- 丑闻严重损害了这位政治家的声誉。
- 长时间暴露在阳光下会损害皮肤。
- 该病毒损害免疫系统，使患者容易受到感染。
- 搬家具时小心别碰坏了。
- 暴风雨对沿海地区的财产造成了广泛破坏。
- 博物馆火灾中有几幅画被烧毁。
- 数周暴雨之后，庄稼遭受洪水破坏。
- 这辆车在高速公路上的一次碰撞中严重受损。
- 这栋旧建筑因多年疏忽和缺乏维护而损坏。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-1-damage)

### 2. damn <a id="2-damn"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *damner* ("to condemn, damn"), from Latin *damnare* ("to condemn, adjudge guilty"), from *damnum* ("loss, injury").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *damner*（"谴责，诅咒"），源自拉丁语 *damnare*（"谴责，判决有罪"），源自 *damnum*（"损失，伤害"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to criticize strongly; to condemn; (informal) to express anger or frustration
- CN: 严厉批评；谴责；（非正式用语）该死，诅咒

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used as an exclamation: "Damn!" — 常用作感叹词："Damn!"（该死！）
- "I'll be damned" expresses surprise — "I'll be damned" 表示惊讶
- "Damn sb with faint praise" = to praise in a way that actually criticizes — "Damn sb with faint praise" = 明褒实贬
- In formal writing, avoid using as an exclamation — 在正式写作中，避免用作感叹词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- damn it
- damn well
- I'll be damned
- damn sb with faint praise
- damn near
- damn sb for

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 该死
- 肯定地；毫无疑问地
- 真想不到；我绝不会
- 明褒实贬
- 几乎；差不多
- 因……而指责某人
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- damn sb for sth
- damn sb as sth
- damn with faint praise
- be damned by sb

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某事而指责某人
- 把某人指责为……
- 明褒实贬
- 被某人指责/否定
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The critics damned the film for its shallow plot. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The proposal was damned by environmental groups as harmful to wildlife. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The reviewer damned the novel with faint praise, calling it "adequate." (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The study was damned by peers for its flawed methodology. (Nature 2020)
- Common: I'll be damned if I let them cheat me again.
- 介词搭配: The public damned the company for its environmental violations.
- 介词搭配: The government's policy was damned as a betrayal of the working class.
- 介词搭配: Her performance was damned with faint praise by the critics.
- 介词搭配: The committee's report was damned by industry leaders as biased.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 评论家因情节肤浅而严厉批评了这部电影。
- 环保组织谴责该提案对野生动物有害。
- 评论者以明褒实贬的方式评价这部小说，称之为"尚可"。
- 这项研究因其方法论存在缺陷而受到同行的谴责。
- 我绝不会让他们再次欺骗我。
- 公众因该公司破坏环境而谴责它。
- 政府的政策被谴责为对工人阶级的背叛。
- 她的表演受到评论家明褒实贬的评价。
- 该委员会的报告被行业领袖批评为有失公允。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-2-damn)

### 3. dampen <a id="3-dampen"></a>

**Etymology:** From *damp* (from Middle Low German *damp* — "vapor, steam") + *-en* (verbal suffix).

**词源：** 源自 *damp*（源自中古低地德语 *damp* — "蒸汽"）+ *-en*（动词后缀）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something slightly wet; to reduce or lessen something (enthusiasm, sound, etc.)
- CN: 使潮湿；抑制，减弱（热情、声音等）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both literal (make wet) and figurative (lessen enthusiasm) meanings — 既有字面意义（弄湿）也有比喻意义（减弱热情）
- "Dampen" is more common in figurative use than "damp" — "Dampen" 在比喻用法中比 "damp" 更常见
- Often used with "enthusiasm," "spirits," "demand" in economic contexts — 常与 "enthusiasm"、"spirits"、"demand" 在经济语境中连用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dampen enthusiasm/spirits
- dampen a sound
- dampen demand
- dampen growth
- dampen the impact of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 抑制热情/挫伤士气
- 减弱声音
- 抑制需求
- 抑制增长
- 减轻影响
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dampen with a cloth/water
- dampen by bad news
- dampen through lack of support

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用布/水弄湿
- 因坏消息而受挫
- 因缺乏支持而减弱
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The rain did little to dampen the children's enthusiasm for the outing. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: Rising interest rates are expected to dampen consumer demand. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The thick curtains helped dampen the noise from the street. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: Economic sanctions have dampened the country's GDP growth. (The Economist 2021)
- Common: A towel can be used to dampen the fabric before ironing.
- 介词搭配: She dampened a cloth with cold water to clean the wound.
- 介词搭配: The team's spirits were dampened by their unexpected defeat.
- 介词搭配: The project's progress was dampened through a lack of funding and resources.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 下雨几乎没有削弱孩子们对郊游的热情。
- 预计加息将抑制消费者的需求。
- 厚窗帘有助于减弱街上的噪音。
- 经济制裁抑制了该国的GDP增长。
- 熨烫前可以用毛巾把布料弄湿。
- 她用一块蘸了冷水的布清洗伤口。
- 队员们的士气因意外的失败而受挫。
- 该项目的进展因缺乏资金和资源而受阻。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-3-dampen)

### 4. dance <a id="4-dance"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *dancier* ("to dance"), of West Germanic origin; related to Old High German *dansōn* ("to stretch, draw").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *dancier*（"跳舞"），源自西日耳曼语；与古高地德语 *dansōn*（"伸展，拉"）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move rhythmically to music; to perform a series of rhythmic movements
- CN: 跳舞；舞蹈

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Most commonly used as intransitive (dance with someone) — 最常用作不及物动词（和某人跳舞）
- As a transitive verb, can take a cognate object: dance a waltz — 作为及物动词，可接同源宾语：dance a waltz
- "Dance to the music" — dance跟随音乐，介词用 "to" — "Dance to the music" — 跳舞时跟随音乐，介词用 "to"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dance with sb
- dance to the music
- dance a dance (e.g., a waltz, a tango)
- dance the night away
- dance for joy
- dance around

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人跳舞
- 随音乐起舞
- 跳一种舞（如华尔兹、探戈）
- 跳了一整夜舞
- 高兴得手舞足蹈
- 在周围舞动/回避
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dance with sb
- dance to the music
- dance at a party
- dance on stage
- dance around the room
- dance in the street
- dance through the night
- dance into the morning

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人跳舞
- 随着音乐起舞
- 在聚会上跳舞
- 在舞台上跳舞
- 在房间里跳舞
- 在街上跳舞
- 跳舞一整夜
- 一直跳到早上
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The couple danced gracefully across the ballroom floor. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Children danced for joy when they heard the news. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The traditional dancers performed a ritual dance to celebrate the harvest. (TOEFL TPO 35)
- Academic: The study examined how honey bees dance to communicate the location of food sources. (Science 2020)
- Common: She loves to dance to Latin music at parties.
- 介词搭配: He danced with his daughter at her wedding.
- 介词搭配: The crowd danced to the rhythm of the drums.
- 介词搭配: They danced at the festival until midnight.
- 介词搭配: The ballet dancer danced on stage with extraordinary grace.
- 介词搭配: The children danced around the Christmas tree, singing happily.
- 介词搭配: People danced in the street to celebrate the victory.
- 介词搭配: The couple danced through the night without feeling tired.
- 介词搭配: The party was so lively that everyone danced into the early morning.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这对夫妇优雅地舞过舞厅地板。
- 孩子们听到这个消息后高兴得跳起舞来。
- 传统舞者表演了一种仪式性舞蹈来庆祝丰收。
- 该研究考察了蜜蜂如何通过舞蹈来交流食物来源的位置。
- 她喜欢在聚会上随着拉丁音乐跳舞。
- 他在女儿的婚礼上与女儿跳舞。
- 人群随着鼓点节奏起舞。
- 他们在节日庆典上跳舞直到午夜。
- 芭蕾舞演员以非凡的优雅在舞台上翩翩起舞。
- 孩子们围着圣诞树跳舞，欢快地唱歌。
- 人们在街上跳舞庆祝胜利。
- 这对夫妇跳了一整夜舞而不觉得累。
- 派对气氛热烈，大家都跳舞一直跳到清晨。
</details>

### 5. dare <a id="5-dare"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *durran* ("to be bold, venture"), from Proto-Germanic *durzan* ("to dare"), from PIE *dʰers-* ("to be bold").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *durran*（"勇敢，敢于"），源自原始日耳曼语 *durzan*（"敢于"），源自原始印欧语 *dʰers-*（"勇敢"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have enough courage or confidence to do something; to challenge someone to do something
- CN: 敢于；胆敢；向……挑战，激将

**Part of Speech:** vt. & modal v. (transitive & modal verb)

**Usage Notes:**

- As a modal verb (mainly in UK): "I dare not go" without "to" — 作情态动词时（主要在英式英语中）："I dare not go" 不用带 "to"
- As a main verb: "He doesn't dare to go" — 作实义动词时："He doesn't dare to go"
- "How dare you!" expresses strong anger — "How dare you!" 表示强烈的愤怒
- "I dare say" = I think it is likely — "I dare say" = 我认为很可能

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dare to do sth
- I dare say
- how dare you
- dare sb to do sth
- don't you dare
- dare to dream
- dare to speak

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 敢于做某事
- 我敢说；我想
- 你竟敢
- 激某人做某事
- 你敢（不许）
- 敢于梦想
- 敢于发言
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dare sb into doing sth
- dare sb to do sth
- dare to challenge

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 激某人做某事
- 向某人挑战去做某事
- 敢于挑战
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She dared to challenge the traditional views of the committee. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Few scientists dared to question the established theory at that time. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: I dare say the experiment will yield interesting results. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The researchers dared to propose a radically new interpretation of the data. (Nature 2019)
- Common: Don't you dare touch my phone without asking!
- 介词搭配: His friends dared him into jumping off the bridge into the river.
- 介词搭配: I dare you to try the spicy noodles — they are extremely hot.
- 介词搭配: Only a few brave activists dared to challenge the government's decision.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她敢于挑战委员会的传统观点。
- 当时很少有科学家敢于质疑已有的理论。
- 我敢说这个实验将会产生有趣的结果。
- 研究人员大胆提出了一种对这些数据的全新解释。
- 未经允许你胆敢碰我的手机！
- 他的朋友们激他跳下桥去，跳进河里。
- 我挑战你尝尝那个辣面条——非常辣。
- 只有少数勇敢的积极分子敢于质疑政府的决定。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-5-dare)

### 6. darken <a id="6-darken"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *deorcian* ("to become dark"), from *deorc* ("dark") + *-ian* (verbal suffix). Cognate with Old High German *tarchanjan* ("to darken").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *deorcian*（"变暗"），源自 *deorc*（"黑暗的"）+ *-ian*（动词后缀）。与古高地德语 *tarchanjan*（"使变暗"）同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become or make something dark or darker; to make someone's mood gloomy
- CN: 使变暗；变黑；使阴沉，使忧郁

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be both transitive (darken a room) and intransitive (the sky darkened) — 既可作及物动词（darken a room）也可作不及物动词（the sky darkened）
- Figurative: "His mood darkened" = he became sad or angry — 比喻用法："His mood darkened" = 他变得悲伤或愤怒
- "Never darken my door again" = an old-fashioned expression meaning never come back — "Never darken my door again" = 古语表达，意为再也不要回来

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- darken the sky
- darken one's mood
- darken a room
- darken the door of
- darken the horizon
- darken the future

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使天空变暗
- 使情绪低落
- 使房间变暗
- 登门造访（常用于否定）
- 使前景暗淡
- 使前途黯淡
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- darken with anger
- darken by clouds
- darken into night

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因愤怒而阴沉
- 被乌云遮蔽
- 渐入黑夜
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The sky darkened as the storm approached the coastal city. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: His expression darkened when he heard the tragic news. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The room was darkened to allow the slides to be projected clearly. (TOEFL TPO 37)
- Academic: The researchers observed that the leaves darkened significantly after exposure to the chemical. (Nature Plants 2021)
- Common: Close the curtains to darken the room for the movie.
- 介词搭配: His face darkened with anger when he learned of the betrayal.
- 介词搭配: The afternoon sky was darkened by thick smoke from the nearby wildfire.
- 介词搭配: The evening sky gradually darkened into a starry night.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 听到这个悲惨的消息，他的表情阴沉了下来。
- 房间被遮暗，以便清晰投影幻灯片。
- 研究人员观察到，叶子在接触化学物质后明显变暗。
- 拉上窗帘，让房间暗下来看电影。
- 得知背叛之事，他的脸因愤怒而阴沉下来。
- 下午的天空被附近野火产生的浓烟遮蔽。
- 傍晚的天空逐渐暗下来，变成一个繁星之夜。
</details>

### 7. dart <a id="7-dart"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *dart* ("a dart"), from Frankish *daroth* ("spear, javelin"), from Proto-Germanic *darōþuz* ("spear").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *dart*（"飞镖"），源自法兰克语 *daroth*（"矛，标枪"），源自原始日耳曼语 *darōþuz*（"矛"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move suddenly and quickly in a particular direction; to look at someone or something quickly
- CN: 猛冲，飞奔；投掷；瞥一眼，急扫

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- As intransitive: dart away/off/forward/past — 作不及物动词：dart away/off/forward/past
- As transitive with "glance/look": She darted a look at him — 作及物动词与 glance/look 连用：She darted a look at him
- Describes sudden, swift movement — 描述突然、迅速的动作

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dart a glance/look
- dart away/off
- dart forward/back
- dart into/out of
- dart across the room
- dart between

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 瞥一眼
- 迅速离开
- 猛冲向前/向后
- 冲进/冲出
- 冲过房间
- 穿梭于之间
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dart into the room
- dart out of the building
- dart across the street
- dart through the crowd
- dart toward the exit
- dart from one place to another
- dart between cars
- dart at sb/sth

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 冲进房间
- 冲出大楼
- 冲过街道
- 穿过人群
- 冲向出口
- 从一个地方赶到另一个地方
- 在车辆间穿梭
- 向某人/某物冲去
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The cat darted across the garden and disappeared into the bushes. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: She darted a quick glance at her watch, anxious about the time. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The hummingbird darted from flower to flower, collecting nectar. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The researchers observed the fish darting between coral branches to evade predators. (Marine Biology 2020)
- Common: The child darted across the playground to join his friends.
- 介词搭配: A figure darted into the room just before the door closed.
- 介词搭配: The spy darted out of the building through a side exit.
- 介词搭配: He darted across the street without looking both ways.
- 介词搭配: The rabbit darted through the dense undergrowth.
- 介词搭配: The goalkeeper darted toward the ball to make the save.
- 介词搭配: The children darted from one exhibit to another at the museum.
- 介词搭配: The taxi darted between lanes of traffic.
- 介词搭配: The snake darted at its prey with lightning speed.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 猫飞奔过花园，消失在灌木丛中。
- 她迅速瞥了一眼手表，担心时间不够。
- 蜂鸟在花丛间飞来飞去，采集花蜜。
- 研究人员观察到这种鱼在珊瑚枝之间快速穿梭以躲避捕食者。
- 那个孩子飞奔过操场去加入他的朋友们。
- 就在门关上的那一刻，一个人影冲进了房间。
- 间谍从一个侧门冲出了大楼。
- 他没看两边就冲过了街道。
- 兔子穿过茂密的灌木丛。
- 守门员冲向球去扑救。
- 孩子们在博物馆里从一个展区跑到另一个展区。
- 出租车在车流中穿梭。
- 蛇以闪电般的速度扑向猎物。
</details>

### 8. dash <a id="8-dash"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English *daschen* ("to strike, smash"), from Old Danish *daske* ("to strike, beat"), probably imitative.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *daschen*（"撞击，打碎"），源自古丹麦语 *daske*（"击打"），可能为拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to run or move quickly and suddenly; to destroy or ruin something (one's hopes)
- CN: 猛冲，急跑；打碎，使（希望）破灭

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: dash across/down/through somewhere — 字面义：dash across/down/through 某处
- Figurative: "dash one's hopes/dreams" — 比喻义："dash one's hopes/dreams" 使希望破灭
- "Dash off" = to write or leave quickly — "Dash off" = 匆忙写就或迅速离开
- "Dashed" in UK English can be a mild expletive — 在英式英语中 "dashed" 可作为温和的感叹词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dash one's hopes
- dash out
- dash away
- dash against
- dash across
- dash through
- dash off
- dash sb's dreams

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人的希望破灭
- 冲出去
- 匆忙离开
- 撞击在……上
- 冲过
- 匆忙穿过
- 匆忙离开/匆忙写就
- 使某人的梦想破灭
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dash into the room
- dash out of the house
- dash across the road
- dash through the rain
- dash against the rocks
- dash toward the finish line
- dash from the scene
- dash with great speed
- dash for cover

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 冲进房间
- 冲出房子
- 冲过马路
- 冒雨冲过
- 撞击在岩石上
- 冲向终点线
- 逃离现场
- 快速冲去
- 冲向掩体
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She dashed out of the office to catch the last train. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The failure dashed his hopes of becoming a professional athlete. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The tourists dashed for shelter when the sudden storm hit. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The waves dashed against the cliffs with tremendous force. (Geology Review 2021)
- Common: I need to dash off a few emails before the meeting.
- 介词搭配: He dashed into the classroom just as the bell was ringing.
- 介词搭配: The children dashed out of the school gate when the final bell rang.
- 介词搭配: She dashed across the road without checking for traffic.
- 介词搭配: The runner dashed through the rain to finish the marathon.
- 介词搭配: The ship was dashed against the rocks by the powerful storm.
- 介词搭配: The competitor dashed toward the finish line with all his remaining strength.
- 介词搭配: The thief dashed from the scene before anyone could react.
- 介词搭配: The soldier dashed for cover when the enemy opened fire.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她冲出办公室去赶最后一班火车。
- 这次失败使他成为职业运动员的希望破灭了。
- 突如其来的暴风雨来临时，游客们冲向避雨处。
- 巨浪以巨大的力量撞击在悬崖上。
- 我需要在开会前匆匆写几封邮件。
- 就在铃声响起时，他冲进了教室。
- 最后一节课的铃声响起，孩子们冲出了校门。
- 她没有注意来往车辆就冲过了马路。
- 那名选手冒雨奔跑，完成了马拉松比赛。
- 那艘船在猛烈的暴风雨中被撞到岩石上。
- 那名参赛者用尽全力冲向终点线。
- 小偷在任何人反应过来之前就逃离了现场。
- 敌人开火时，那名士兵冲向掩体。
</details>

### 9. date <a id="9-date"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *dater* ("to date"), from *date* ("date"), from Late Latin *data* (from *data Romae* — "given at Rome"), from *dare* ("to give").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *dater*（"注明日期"），源自 *date*（"日期"），源自后期拉丁语 *data*（源自 *data Romae* — "颁发于罗马"），源自 *dare*（"给予"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to mark or record the date of something; to determine the age of something; to go out with someone romantically
- CN: 注明日期；确定年代；与……约会

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Date back to" / "date from" = to have existed since a particular time — "Date back to" / "date from" = 可追溯到某个时期
- "Date sb" = to have a romantic relationship — "Date sb" = 与某人恋爱
- "Out of date" (adj. phrase) = no longer valid — "Out of date" (形容词短语) = 过时的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- date back to
- date from
- date sb
- date a document
- out of date
- up to date
- date a letter
- date to the period

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 追溯到
- 始于（某时期）
- 与某人约会
- 给文件注明日期
- 过时
- 最新
- 在信上写明日期
- 可追溯到某个时期
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- date from the 18th century
- date back to ancient times
- date to a specific year
- date at around 300 BC
- date by carbon dating
- be dated with a stamp

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 始于18世纪
- 可追溯到古代
- 可追溯到某一年
- 年代约为公元前300年
- 通过碳定年法确定年代
- 盖邮戳注明日期
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The fossils date back to the Jurassic period, according to the researchers. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The manuscript dates from the 14th century and is extremely valuable. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Archaeologists dated the pottery to around 2000 BC using radiocarbon techniques. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The rock samples were dated at approximately 4.5 billion years old. (Science 2021)
- Common: Remember to date your check before handing it in.
- 介词搭配: This tradition dates from the 18th century when the town was first established.
- 介词搭配: The oldest known map of the region dates back to ancient Roman times.
- 介词搭配: The artwork has been dated to the early Renaissance period.
- 介词搭配: The skeleton was dated at around 3,000 years old by forensic experts.
- 介词搭配: The age of the artifact was dated by carbon dating methods.
- 介词搭配: The official letter was dated with the company's stamp.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 研究人员称，这些化石可追溯到侏罗纪时期。
- 这份手稿始于14世纪，极为珍贵。
- 考古学家利用放射性碳技术测定这些陶器的年代约为公元前2000年。
- 这些岩石样本的年代测定约为45亿年。
- 交支票前记得注明日期。
- 这一传统始于18世纪该镇初建之时。
- 该地区已知最古老的地图可追溯到古罗马时期。
- 这件艺术品被鉴定为文艺复兴早期作品。
- 法医专家鉴定该骨骼距今约3000年。
- 该文物的年代是通过碳定年法测定的。
- 正式信函上盖有公司的日期印章。
</details>

### 10. dawn <a id="10-dawn"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *dagian* ("to become day"), from Proto-Germanic *dagāną* ("to become day, dawn"), from *dagaz* ("day").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *dagian*（"破晓"），源自原始日耳曼语 *dagāną*（"天亮，破晓"），源自 *dagaz*（"天，日"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (of a day) to begin; to become light in the morning; to become clear or obvious to someone
- CN: 破晓，天亮；开始出现；变得明白，醒悟

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Dawn on/upon sb" = sb suddenly realizes something — "Dawn on/upon sb" = 某人突然意识到
- Used impersonally: "It dawned" = the day began — 用于无人称句："It dawned" = 天亮了
- Not used with a direct object — 不与直接宾语连用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dawn on/upon sb
- dawn breaks
- dawn rises
- at the crack of dawn
- from dawn to dusk
- a new day dawns
- the truth dawns
- it dawns that

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 某人突然明白
- 破晓
- 黎明来临
- 在破晓时分
- 从早到晚
- 新的一天开始
- 真相浮现
- 突然意识到
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dawn on sb that
- dawn in the east
- dawn over the city
- dawn upon sb

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 某人突然意识到
- 东方破晓
- 城市上空天亮了
- 某人恍然大悟
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It suddenly dawned on her that she had left the keys in the office. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: As the morning dawned, the soldiers prepared for the final assault. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: It dawned on the researchers that their hypothesis might have been fundamentally flawed. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The realization dawned that climate change was accelerating faster than previously predicted. (Nature Climate Change 2020)
- Common: A new era of digital communication was dawning.
- 介词搭配: It dawned on him that he had been deceived by his closest friend.
- 介词搭配: The sun dawned in the east, painting the sky in shades of orange.
- 介词搭配: A beautiful morning dawned over the peaceful valley.
- 介词搭配: Gradually it dawned upon the committee that the project was no longer viable.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她突然意识到把钥匙落在了办公室。
- 清晨来临时，士兵们准备最后的进攻。
- 研究人员突然意识到他们的假设可能从根本上有缺陷。
- 人们开始意识到气候变化比之前预测的加速得更快。
- 数字通信的新时代正在开启。
- 他突然意识到自己被他最亲密的朋友欺骗了。
- 太阳从东方升起，将天空染成橙色的色调。
- 一个美丽的清晨降临在宁静的山谷之上。
- 委员会逐渐意识到这个项目不再可行。
</details>

### 11. dazzle <a id="11-dazzle"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English *dasen* ("to daze, confuse"), frequentative form *dazlen*, from Old Norse *dasask* ("to become exhausted").

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *dasen*（"使茫然，使迷惑"），重复形式 *dazlen*，源自古诺尔斯语 *dasask*（"变得疲惫"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to blind or confuse someone with a bright light; to impress someone greatly with beauty, skill, or intelligence
- CN: 使眼花，使目眩；使赞叹，使倾倒

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: a bright light dazzles — 字面义：强光使人目眩
- Figurative: a performance/speech/beauty dazzles — 比喻义：表演/演讲/美貌令人赞叹
- Often used in passive: "be dazzled by" — 常用被动语态："be dazzled by"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dazzle the audience
- dazzle with
- be dazzled by
- dazzling light/smile
- dazzle the crowd
- dazzling performance

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 令观众赞叹
- 用……使人倾倒
- 被……迷住/目眩
- 耀眼的光芒/灿烂的笑容
- 令人群赞叹
- 令人赞叹的表演
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dazzle sb with beauty/skill
- be dazzled by the lights
- dazzle in a performance
- be dazzled at the sight

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 以美貌/技艺令某人倾倒
- 被灯光照得目眩
- 在表演中光彩夺目
- 被景象所震慑
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The bright headlights of the oncoming car dazzled the driver. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The young pianist dazzled the audience with her incredible technique. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Visitors are often dazzled by the magnificent architecture of the cathedral. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The telescope's mirrors were designed to collect light without being dazzled by bright stars. (Astronomy Journal 2022)
- Common: His dazzling smile won everyone over immediately.
- 介词搭配: The magician dazzled the children with his amazing tricks.
- 介词搭配: I was completely dazzled by the brilliant display of fireworks.
- 介词搭配: She absolutely dazzled in her first Broadway performance.
- 介词搭配: The explorers were dazzled at the sight of the hidden treasure.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 迎面开来车辆刺眼的车前灯使那位司机目眩。
- 这位年轻的钢琴家以令人难以置信的技巧令观众赞叹。
- 游客常常被大教堂宏伟的建筑所倾倒。
- 该望远镜的镜面被设计成能够收集光线而不被明亮的恒星干扰。
- 他那灿烂的笑容立刻赢得了所有人的好感。
- 魔术师以他神奇的戏法让孩子们惊叹不已。
- 我被绚丽的烟花表演完全迷住了。
- 她在百老汇的首场演出中大放异彩。
- 探险者看到隐藏的宝藏时惊得目瞪口呆。
</details>

### 12. deal <a id="12-deal"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *dǣlan* ("to divide, distribute, share"), from Proto-Germanic *dailijaną* ("to divide"), from PIE *dʰail-* ("to divide").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *dǣlan*（"分配，分发，分享"），源自原始日耳曼语 *dailijaną*（"分割"），源自原始印欧语 *dʰail-*（"分割"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take action to solve a problem; to be about something; to buy and sell goods or services; to distribute cards
- CN: 处理，应对；涉及，论述；经营，买卖；分发（牌）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deal with" = to handle a problem or situation — "Deal with" = 处理问题或情况
- "Deal in" = to buy and sell goods — "Deal in" = 从事……买卖
- "Deal a blow to" = to harm or shock someone — "Deal a blow to" = 打击
- "Deal out" = to distribute — "Deal out" = 分发

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deal with a problem
- deal in goods
- deal a blow
- deal out cards
- deal with customers
- deal fairly
- deal effectively
- deal with stress
- deal in facts

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理问题
- 经营商品
- 给予打击
- 发牌
- 与客户打交道
- 公正地对待
- 有效处理
- 应对压力
- 以事实为依据
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deal with sb/sth
- deal in antique furniture
- deal out punishment
- deal a blow to the economy
- deal at an auction
- deal by a certain policy

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理某人/某事/与某人打交道
- 经营古董家具
- 施以惩罚
- 对经济给予打击
- 在拍卖中交易
- 按照某种政策处理
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government must deal effectively with the rising unemployment rate. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The new regulation deals with issues related to data privacy. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The company deals in high-end electronic equipment. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: This research paper deals with the long-term effects of climate change on marine ecosystems. (Science 2021)
- Common: How do you usually deal with stress at work?
- 介词搭配: The manager knows how to deal with difficult customers.
- 介词搭配: This antique shop deals in rare books and manuscripts.
- 介词搭配: The judge dealt out harsh punishments to the offenders.
- 介词搭配: The sudden increase in oil prices dealt a severe blow to the global economy.
- 介词搭配: Rare coins are often dealt at specialized auction houses.
- 介词搭配: The complaints were dealt by a special committee.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府必须有效应对不断上升的失业率。
- 新规定涉及与数据隐私相关的问题。
- 这家公司经营高端电子设备。
- 这篇研究论文论述了气候变化对海洋生态系统的长期影响。
- 你通常如何应对工作压力？
- 经理知道如何与难缠的客户打交道。
- 这家古董店经营珍本图书和手稿。
- 法官对违法者施以严厉的惩罚。
- 油价的突然上涨对全球经济造成了严重打击。
- 稀有硬币常在专门的拍卖行进行交易。
- 投诉由一个特别委员会处理。
</details>

### 13. debate <a id="13-debate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *debatre* ("to fight, contend, debate"), from Latin *dis-* ("apart") + *battuere* ("to beat, fight").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *debatre*（"斗争，争论，辩论"），源自拉丁语 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *battuere*（"打，斗"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to discuss a subject formally in a public meeting or in Parliament; to consider a matter carefully
- CN: 辩论，争论；仔细考虑，斟酌

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be followed by a wh-clause: debate whether/what/how — 可接 wh-从句：debate whether/what/how
- "Hotly debated" = causing much discussion — "Hotly debated" = 引起激烈讨论的
- More formal than "discuss", often involves opposing views — 比 "discuss" 更正式，常涉及对立观点

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- debate an issue
- debate whether/wh
- debate with sb
- debate about/over
- hotly/widely debated
- debate a proposal
- debate the merits of
- debate in parliament

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 辩论问题
- 辩论是否……
- 与某人辩论
- 就……辩论
- 激烈/广泛讨论的
- 辩论提案
- 讨论利弊
- 在议会中辩论
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- debate about the issue
- debate with opponents
- debate over the policy
- debate in the Senate
- debate on a topic
- debate against a proposition
- debate as to whether

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 就这个问题辩论
- 与对手辩论
- 就政策问题辩论
- 在参议院辩论
- 就某个话题辩论
- 辩驳某个命题
- 就是否……进行辩论
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The committee debated the proposal for hours before reaching a decision. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Scholars have long debated whether the manuscript is authentic. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The candidates debated the merits of the healthcare reform. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The scientific community continues to debate the implications of gene editing technology. (Nature 2022)
- Common: We debated whether to go on vacation or save the money.
- 介词搭配: The students debated about the ethics of artificial intelligence.
- 介词搭配: The politician debated with her opponent on live television.
- 介词搭配: Parliament debated over the proposed tax reforms for several days.
- 介词搭配: The issue was debated in the Senate last week.
- 介词搭配: The panel will debate on the topic of climate change.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer debated against the prosecution's argument.
- 介词搭配: There is much debate as to whether the policy will be effective.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 委员会就这项提案辩论了几个小时才做出决定。
- 学者们长期以来一直在争论这份手稿是否真实。
- 候选人讨论了医疗改革的利弊。
- 科学界继续就基因编辑技术的意义进行辩论。
- 我们讨论是去度假还是把钱存起来。
- 学生们就人工智能的伦理问题进行了辩论。
- 这位政治家在电视直播中与对手辩论。
- 议会就拟议的税收改革辩论了数天。
- 上周参议院对这个议题进行了辩论。
- 专家组将就气候变化这一主题进行辩论。
- 这位律师驳斥了控方的论点。
- 关于这项政策是否有效还存在很多争议。
</details>

### 14. decay <a id="14-decay"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *decair* ("to fall, decay"), from Latin *dēcidere* ("to fall down, fall off"), from *dē-* ("down") + *cadere* ("to fall").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *decair*（"衰落，腐烂"），源自拉丁语 *dēcidere*（"倒下，掉落"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *cadere*（"落下"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be gradually destroyed through natural processes; to become gradually worse in quality or strength
- CN: 腐烂，腐朽；衰退，衰败

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used for organic matter: food/wood/teeth decay — 用于有机物质：食物/木头/牙齿腐烂
- Used for abstract concepts: moral/social/urban decay — 用于抽象概念：道德/社会/城市衰退
- Unlike "rot", "decay" can be a slow, natural process — 与 "rot" 不同，"decay" 可以是缓慢的自然过程
- Not typically used as a transitive verb — 通常不用于及物动词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decay over time
- decay rapidly
- tooth decay
- moral decay
- urban decay
- decay naturally
- decay with age
- prevent decay

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 随时间腐烂
- 迅速腐烂/衰退
- 蛀牙
- 道德败坏
- 城市衰败
- 自然腐烂
- 随年龄增长而衰退
- 防止腐烂
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decay into dust
- decay from within
- decay with age
- decay in the soil
- decay to a powder

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 腐烂成尘土
- 从内部开始腐烂
- 随着年龄增长而衰退
- 在土壤中腐烂
- 腐烂成粉末
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The abandoned wooden house began to decay after years of neglect. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: If left untreated, the tooth will continue to decay and may cause serious pain. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Organic matter decays more quickly in warm, moist environments. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The researchers studied how plant material decays in different soil types. (Ecology 2021)
- Common: Eating too much sugar can cause your teeth to decay.
- 介词搭配: The fallen leaves decayed into rich organic matter over the winter.
- 介词搭配: The old building was decaying from within due to moisture and pests.
- 介词搭配: The ancient monument decayed with age and exposure to the elements.
- 介词搭配: Buried wood decays slowly in the dry desert soil.
- 介词搭配: The organic material gradually decays to a fine powder.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 那座废弃的木屋因多年无人照管而开始腐朽。
- 如果不加处理，牙齿会继续蛀坏，可能导致剧烈疼痛。
- 有机物质在温暖潮湿的环境中腐烂得更快。
- 研究人员研究了植物物质在不同土壤类型中的腐烂情况。
- 吃太多糖会导致蛀牙。
- 落叶在整个冬天腐烂成了肥沃的有机物。
- 由于潮湿和虫害，老建筑从内部开始腐朽。
- 古纪念碑因年代久远和风吹日晒而渐渐风化。
- 埋在干涸沙漠土壤中的木头腐烂得很慢。
- 有机物逐渐腐烂成细粉。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-14-decay)

### 15. deceive <a id="15-deceive"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deceivre* ("to deceive"), from Latin *dēcipere* ("to ensnare, trap, deceive"), from *dē-* ("from") + *capere* ("to take").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deceivre*（"欺骗"），源自拉丁语 *dēcipere*（"诱捕，欺骗"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *capere*（"拿取"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone believe something that is not true, especially in order to get something
- CN: 欺骗，蒙骗，误导

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deceive sb into doing sth" = to trick someone into doing something — "Deceive sb into doing sth" = 诱骗某人做某事
- "Deceive oneself" = to refuse to admit the truth — "Deceive oneself" = 自欺欺人
- Stronger than "mislead"; implies deliberate intent — 比 "mislead" 语气更强；暗示有意为之
- Not used with "to" ("deceive to" is incorrect) — 不与 "to" 连用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deceive sb
- deceive oneself
- deceive the public
- deliberately deceive
- deceive sb into doing
- be deceived by appearances
- deceive by lying

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 欺骗某人
- 自欺欺人
- 欺骗公众
- 故意欺骗
- 诱骗某人做某事
- 被表象所欺骗
- 用谎言欺骗
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deceive sb into believing sth
- deceive sb about sth
- be deceived by appearances
- deceive through false promises

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 诱骗某人相信某事
- 就某事欺骗某人
- 被表象所欺骗
- 通过虚假承诺欺骗
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company deceived customers into signing unfair contracts. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: He deceived himself into thinking that everything would be fine. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Appearances can deceive; what looks like a simple plant may have complex properties. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The study found that participants were easily deceived by misleading statistics. (Psychological Science 2021)
- Common: Don't be deceived by the low price — the quality is poor.
- 介词搭配: The salesman deceived the elderly couple into buying a worthless insurance policy.
- 介词搭配: She deceived her parents about where she had been that night.
- 介词搭配: Many investors were deceived by the company's impressive but false financial reports.
- 介词搭配: The fraudster deceived his victims through elaborate fake documents.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司欺骗客户签署不公平的合同。
- 他自欺欺人地认为一切都会好起来。
- 外表可能具有欺骗性；看起来很简单的植物可能有复杂的特性。
- 研究发现，参与者很容易被误导性的统计数据所欺骗。
- 不要被低价所欺骗——质量很差。
- 推销员诱骗这对老夫妇购买了一份毫无价值的保险单。
- 她向父母隐瞒了那天晚上去了哪里。
- 许多投资者被该公司令人印象深刻但虚假的财务报告所欺骗。
- 该诈骗犯通过精心制作的假文件欺骗受害者。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-15-deceive)

### 16. decide <a id="16-decide"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *decider* ("to decide, settle"), from Latin *dēcīdere* ("to cut off, determine"), from *dē-* ("off") + *caedere* ("to cut").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *decider*（"决定，确定"），源自拉丁语 *dēcīdere*（"切断，决定"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *caedere*（"切割"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a choice or judgment about something after considering it; to determine something
- CN: 决定；判决；确定

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Followed by to-infinitive: decide to do sth — 后接不定式：decide to do sth
- Followed by wh-clause: decide what/whether/when — 后接 wh-从句：decide what/whether/when
- "Decide on/upon" = to choose from possibilities — "Decide on/upon" = 选定
- "Decide against" = to choose not to do — "Decide against" = 决定不做

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decide to do sth
- decide on/upon
- decide that
- decide between
- decide against
- decide in favor of
- decide by vote
- decide for oneself
- decide the outcome
- decide a case

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 决定做某事
- 选定
- 决定……
- 在……之间做决定
- 决定反对
- 决定支持
- 通过投票决定
- 自己做决定
- 决定结果
- 裁决案件
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decide on a date/location
- decide between two options
- decide against a proposal
- decide for the plaintiff
- decide by majority vote
- decide in favor of reform
- decide upon a course of action

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 选定日期/地点
- 在两个选项中做决定
- 决定反对一项提案
- 做出有利于原告的判决
- 以多数票决定
- 决定支持改革
- 决定行动方案
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: After much discussion, the committee decided to postpone the event. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: It is difficult to decide which approach is more effective without sufficient data. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The court will decide whether the evidence is admissible. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The researchers decided on a randomized controlled trial design. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: Have you decided what to major in at university?
- 介词搭配: We need to decide on a venue for the conference by next week.
- 介词搭配: She couldn't decide between studying abroad or taking the job offer.
- 介词搭配: The majority of members decided against the proposed changes.
- 介词搭配: The jury decided for the defendant after a lengthy deliberation.
- 介词搭配: The final decision was decided by a unanimous vote.
- 介词搭配: The judge decided in favor of the plaintiff.
- 介词搭配: The board decided upon a new marketing strategy.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经过大量讨论，委员会决定推迟该活动。
- 没有足够的数据，很难确定哪种方法更有效。
- 法院将裁决该证据是否可被采纳。
- 研究人员决定采用随机对照试验设计。
- 你决定好大学主修什么了吗？
- 我们需要在下周之前选定会议场地。
- 她在出国留学和接受这份工作之间难以抉择。
- 大多数成员决定反对提议的变更。
- 经过长时间的商议，陪审团做出了有利于被告的裁决。
- 最终决定由一致投票做出。
- 法官做出了有利于原告的裁决。
- 董事会决定采取新的营销策略。
</details>

### 17. declare <a id="17-declare"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēclārāre* ("to make clear, declare"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *clārāre* ("to make clear"), from *clārus* ("clear").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēclārāre*（"阐明，宣告"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *clārāre*（"使清晰"），源自 *clārus*（"清晰的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to state something formally or officially; to announce something clearly
- CN: 宣布，宣告；声明，申报

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal/official context: declare war, declare independence — 正式/官方语境：宣战，宣布独立
- "Declare sth open/closed" — 宣布某物开始/结束
- "Declare for/against" = to state support/opposition — "Declare for/against" = 表明支持/反对
- In customs: "declare goods" = to report for tax — 在海关语境中："declare goods" = 申报纳税

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- declare war
- declare bankruptcy
- declare independence
- declare one's intention
- declare the result
- declare openly/publicly
- declare sb dead
- declare an emergency
- declare an interest

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 宣战
- 宣布破产
- 宣布独立
- 声明某人的意图
- 宣布结果
- 公开声明
- 宣布某人死亡
- 宣布紧急状态
- 申报利益
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- declare for a candidate
- declare against a policy
- declare to the press
- declare as income
- declare in court
- declare on oath

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 公开支持某候选人
- 公开反对某项政策
- 向媒体声明
- 申报为收入
- 在法庭上声明
- 宣誓声明
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The country declared independence after decades of struggle. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The government declared a state of emergency following the earthquake. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: All passengers must declare any goods they are carrying that exceed the duty-free limit. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The WHO declared the outbreak a public health emergency of international concern. (The Lancet 2020)
- Common: He declared his intention to run for president.
- 介词搭配: Several senators declared for the new healthcare bill.
- 介词搭配: Many citizens declared against the proposed tax increase.
- 介词搭配: The spokesperson declared to the media that no decision had been made.
- 介词搭配: You must declare any gifts you receive as income on your tax return.
- 介词搭配: The witness declared in court that she had seen the defendant at the scene.
- 介词搭配: The defendant declared on oath that he was innocent.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经过几十年的斗争，该国宣布独立。
- 地震发生后，政府宣布进入紧急状态。
- 所有乘客必须申报携带的任何超出免税限额的物品。
- 世界卫生组织宣布该疫情为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。
- 他宣布了参选总统的意图。
- 几位参议员公开支持新的医疗法案。
- 许多公民公开反对拟议的增税方案。
- 发言人向媒体声明尚未做出任何决定。
- 你必须在纳税申报中将收到的任何礼物申报为收入。
- 证人在法庭上声明她曾在现场看到被告。
- 被告宣誓声明自己是无辜的。
</details>

### 18. decline <a id="18-decline"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *decliner* ("to sink, decline"), from Latin *dēclīnāre* ("to bend down, turn aside, avoid"), from *dē-* ("down") + *clīnāre* ("to bend").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *decliner*（"下沉，衰落"），源自拉丁语 *dēclīnāre*（"弯曲，转向，避免"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *clīnāre*（"弯曲"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become smaller, weaker, or less important; to politely refuse to accept or do something
- CN: 下降，衰退；婉拒，谢绝

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- As intransitive (most common): population/economy/health declines — 作不及物动词（最常见）：人口/经济/健康衰退
- As transitive: politely refuse an offer/invitation — 作及物动词：婉拒提议/邀请
- More polite than "refuse" — 比 "refuse" 更有礼貌
- "In decline" (noun phrase) = declining — "In decline"（名词短语）= 在衰退

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decline an offer/invitation
- decline in value
- decline rapidly/steadily
- decline an invitation
- decline to comment
- decline a proposal
- decline sharply
- population decline

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 婉拒提议/邀请
- 价值下降
- 迅速/稳步下降
- 谢绝邀请
- 拒绝评论
- 拒绝提案
- 急剧下降
- 人口减少
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decline by 10%
- decline from 100 to 50
- decline in quality/population
- decline to a certain level
- decline toward extinction

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 下降了10%
- 从100降到50
- 在质量/数量上下降
- 下降到某个水平
- 趋于灭绝
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The birth rate has declined significantly over the past decade. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: She politely declined the invitation, citing prior commitments. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The manufacturing sector declined by 5% due to reduced exports. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The population of the species has declined to fewer than 1,000 individuals in the wild. (Conservation Biology 2022)
- Common: He declined to answer any further questions.
- 介词搭配: The company's profits declined by 15% last quarter.
- 介词搭配: The number of applicants declined from 5,000 to just over 3,000.
- 介词搭配: There has been a sharp decline in the quality of the products recently.
- 介词搭配: The standard of living has declined to levels not seen in decades.
- 介词搭配: The once-thriving industry is declining toward extinction.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 过去十年出生率显著下降。
- 她以已有安排为由，礼貌地谢绝了邀请。
- 由于出口减少，制造业下降了5%。
- 该物种的野外种群数量已下降到不足1,000只。
- 他拒绝回答更多问题。
- 公司利润上个季度下降了15%。
- 申请人数从5,000人下降到仅3,000多人。
- 最近产品质量急剧下降。
- 生活水平已下降到几十年未见的水平。
- 这个曾经繁荣的产业正趋于消亡。
</details>

### 19. decorate <a id="19-decorate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *decoratus* ("adorned, embellished"), past participle of *decorare* ("to adorn, beautify"), from *decus* ("ornament, honor").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *decoratus*（"装饰的"），*decorare*（"装饰，美化"）的过去分词，源自 *decus*（"装饰，荣誉"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something look more attractive by adding things to it; to apply paint or wallpaper to a room
- CN: 装饰，装潢；粉刷，油漆

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Decorate with" = using materials — "Decorate with" = 使用材料
- "Decorate for" = for an occasion — "Decorate for" = 为某个场合
- In UK: "decorate" often means paint/wallpaper a room — 在英式英语中："decorate" 常指粉刷/贴墙纸
- "Decorate a cake" = adding icing — "Decorate a cake" = 在蛋糕上裱花

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decorate a room/house
- decorate with flowers
- decorate for Christmas
- decorate a cake
- decorate a street
- decorate elaborately
- decorate beautifully
- freshly decorated

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 装饰房间/房子
- 用鲜花装饰
- 为圣诞节装饰
- 装饰蛋糕
- 装饰街道
- 精心装饰
- 装饰得漂亮
- 新装修的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decorate with ribbons and balloons
- decorate for the wedding
- decorate in red and gold
- decorate by hand
- decorate throughout the house

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用丝带和气球装饰
- 为婚礼装饰
- 以红色和金色装饰
- 手工装饰
- 在整个房子里装饰
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The students decorated the classroom with colorful paper for the New Year's party. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The streets were decorated with lanterns and banners during the festival. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The traditional cakes are decorated with intricate patterns made from colored icing. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The temple walls are decorated with exquisite frescoes dating from the 12th century. (Art History Review 2021)
- Common: We spent the whole weekend decorating the living room.
- 介词搭配: The Christmas tree was decorated with lights and ornaments.
- 介词搭配: The hall was beautifully decorated for the annual awards ceremony.
- 介词搭配: The room was decorated in soft pastel colors.
- 介词搭配: The intricate patterns on the vase were decorated by skilled craftsmen.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学生们用彩纸装饰了教室，为新年晚会做准备。
- 节日期间，街上装饰着灯笼和彩旗。
- 传统蛋糕用彩色糖霜装饰出精美的图案。
- 寺庙的墙壁上装饰着可追溯到12世纪的精美壁画。
- 我们花了整个周末装饰客厅。
- 圣诞树上装饰着彩灯和饰品。
- 大厅为年度颁奖典礼装饰得非常漂亮。
- 房间以柔和的粉色系装饰。
- 花瓶上精美的图案是由熟练工匠手工制作的。
</details>

### 20. decrease <a id="20-decrease"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *decroistre* ("to decrease"), from Latin *dēcrēscere* ("to grow less, diminish"), from *dē-* ("down from") + *crēscere* ("to grow").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *decroistre*（"减少"），源自拉丁语 *dēcrēscere*（"减少，缩小"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *crēscere*（"生长"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become smaller or fewer in size, amount, or strength
- CN: 减少，降低，减小

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive (decrease costs) and intransitive (costs decrease) — 既可及物（decrease costs）也可不及物（costs decrease）
- Opposite of "increase" — 是 "increase" 的反义词
- More formal than "go down" or "drop" — 比 "go down" 或 "drop" 更正式
- "Decrease by" shows amount of change; "decrease to" shows final amount — "Decrease by" 表示变化量；"decrease to" 表示最终数量

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decrease the risk
- decrease steadily/significantly
- decrease the number of
- decrease the cost
- decrease in size
- decrease dramatically
- gradually decrease
- decrease the likelihood

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 降低风险
- 稳步/显著下降
- 减少数量
- 降低成本
- 尺寸缩小
- 急剧减少
- 逐渐减少
- 降低可能性
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decrease by 20%
- decrease from 500 to 300
- decrease in value
- decrease to a minimum
- decrease with age
- decrease over time

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 减少了20%
- 从500减少到300
- 价值下降
- 减少到最低限度
- 随着年龄增长而减少
- 随时间减少
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The number of students choosing to study abroad has decreased this year. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: Regular exercise can significantly decrease the risk of heart disease. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The population of the species decreased dramatically due to habitat loss. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The researchers observed that cell activity decreases with age. (Cell 2021)
- Common: Please decrease the volume of the music — it's too loud.
- 介词搭配: The company's workforce decreased by 10% after the restructuring.
- 介词搭配: Temperatures decreased from 35°C to 20°C overnight.
- 介词搭配: There has been a noticeable decrease in crime rates this year.
- 介词搭配: The water level decreased to its lowest point in fifty years.
- 介词搭配: Bone density naturally decreases with age.
- 介词搭配: The effectiveness of the drug decreases over time.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 今年选择出国留学的学生人数减少了。
- 定期锻炼可以显著降低患心脏病的风险。
- 由于栖息地丧失，该物种的数量急剧减少。
- 研究人员观察到细胞活动随年龄增长而减少。
- 请把音乐音量调小——太吵了。
- 重组后公司员工减少了10%。
- 温度在一夜之间从35摄氏度降到了20摄氏度。
- 今年的犯罪率有明显下降。
- 水位降到了五十年来的最低点。
- 骨密度随年龄增长而自然降低。
- 这种药物的效果会随着时间的推移而减弱。
</details>

### 21. decree <a id="21-decree"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *decret* ("decree"), from Latin *dēcrētum* ("decree, decision"), neuter past participle of *dēcernere* ("to decide, determine").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *decret*（"法令"），源自拉丁语 *dēcrētum*（"法令，裁决"），*dēcernere*（"决定，确定"）的中性过去分词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make an official order or decision, especially by a ruler or government
- CN: 颁布法令；裁定，判决

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal/legal context — 正式/法律语境
- Can be followed by a that-clause: decree that... — 可接 that-从句
- Often used in passive: it was decreed that... — 常用被动语态：it was decreed that...
- Typically associated with government, royal, or judicial authority — 通常与政府、皇室或司法权威相关

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- decree that
- decree a law
- decree an amnesty
- decree by the government
- issue a decree
- royal decree
- presidential decree
- decree an end to

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 颁布法令规定……
- 颁布法律
- 颁布大赦
- 由政府颁布
- 发布法令
- 皇家法令
- 总统令
- 下令结束
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- decree by the king
- decree under the constitution
- decree as law
- decree in writing

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 由国王颁布
- 根据宪法颁布
- 作为法律颁布
- 以书面形式颁布
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government decreed that all schools must remain closed until further notice. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The emperor decreed a general amnesty for political prisoners. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The court decreed that the land be returned to its original owners. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The new regulations were decreed to protect endangered marine species. (Nature 2020)
- Common: The principal decreed that students must wear uniforms at all times.
- 介词搭配: The new law was decreed by the parliament after lengthy debate.
- 介词搭配: The president acted under the powers decreed under the constitution.
- 介词搭配: The reforms were decreed as official policy last month.
- 介词搭配: The terms of the settlement were decreed in writing by the judge.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府下令所有学校继续关闭，直至另行通知。
- 皇帝下令大赦政治犯。
- 法院裁定土地应归还原主。
- 颁布新法规以保护濒危海洋物种。
- 校长规定学生必须始终穿着校服。
- 议会在长时间的辩论后颁布了这项新法律。
- 总统根据宪法赋予的权力行事。
- 改革措施上月被颁布为官方政策。
- 和解条款由法官以书面形式裁定。
</details>

### 22. dedicate <a id="22-dedicate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēdicātus*, past participle of *dēdicāre* ("to proclaim, dedicate"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *dicāre* ("to proclaim, set apart").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēdicātus*，*dēdicāre*（"宣告，奉献"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *dicāre*（"宣告，奉献"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give all of your energy, time, or attention to something; to say that a book, song, etc. is for someone
- CN: 致力于，献身于；把（作品）题献给某人

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Dedicate oneself/one's life to" = to commit entirely — "Dedicate oneself/one's life to" = 全身心投入
- "Dedicate A to B" = to commit resources or time — "Dedicate A to B" = 将资源或时间用于
- Also used for artistic works: "This book is dedicated to..." — 也用于艺术作品："This book is dedicated to..."
- Stronger commitment than "devote" in some contexts — 在某些语境中比 "devote" 的承诺更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dedicate oneself to
- dedicate a book to
- dedicate time/energy to
- dedicate one's life to
- dedicate a monument
- dedicate efforts to
- dedicate resources to
- fully dedicate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 致力于
- 将书题献给
- 把时间/精力用于
- 奉献一生
- 为纪念碑揭幕
- 把努力用于
- 将资源用于
- 全身心投入
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dedicate to a cause
- dedicate in memory of sb
- dedicate for a purpose
- dedicate as a memorial

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 致力于某项事业
- 为纪念某人而奉献
- 为某个目的而投入
- 作为纪念而奉献
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She dedicated her entire career to improving public health. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The scientist dedicated his life to finding a cure for cancer. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The author dedicated the book to her parents, who had always supported her writing. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The research team dedicated significant resources to analyzing the genomic data. (Nature 2021)
- Common: He dedicated his victory to his late grandfather.
- 介词搭配: The organization is dedicated to the cause of environmental protection.
- 介词搭配: The monument was dedicated in memory of the soldiers who died in the war.
- 介词搭配: A portion of the budget was dedicated for educational purposes.
- 介词搭配: The garden was dedicated as a memorial to the victims of the tragedy.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她把整个职业生涯致力于改善公共健康。
- 这位科学家毕生致力于寻找癌症的治疗方法。
- 作者将这本书献给一直支持她写作的父母。
- 研究团队投入了大量资源来分析基因组数据。
- 他把胜利献给了已故的祖父。
- 该组织致力于环境保护事业。
- 该纪念碑是为了纪念战争中阵亡的士兵而设立的。
- 一部分预算被拨用于教育目的。
- 花园被辟为纪念地，以纪念这场悲剧的受害者。
</details>

### 23. deduce <a id="23-deduce"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēdūcere* ("to lead down, derive"), from *dē-* ("down") + *dūcere* ("to lead").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēdūcere*（"引导向下，推导"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *dūcere*（"引导"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to reach a conclusion by reasoning from given facts or general principles
- CN: 推论，推断，演绎

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal word, more technical than "conclude" — 正式词汇，比 "conclude" 更具技术性
- Often used in scientific and logical reasoning contexts — 常用于科学和逻辑推理语境
- "Deduce from" is the most common preposition pattern — "Deduce from" 是最常见的介词搭配
- Opposite direction of "induce" (inductive reasoning) — 与 "induce"（归纳推理）方向相反

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deduce from
- deduce that
- deduce a conclusion
- deduce by reasoning
- deduce logically
- deduce from evidence
- deduce the truth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……推断出
- 推断出……
- 推出结论
- 通过推理推断
- 逻辑推断
- 从证据中推断
- 推断出真相
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deduce from the evidence
- deduce by a process of elimination
- deduce through careful analysis

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从证据中推断
- 通过排除法推断
- 通过仔细分析推断
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: From the clues left at the scene, the detective deduced what had happened. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The researchers deduced from the data that the drug was effective in most cases. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Based on the fossils found, scientists deduced that the area was once underwater. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: By comparing DNA sequences, the team deduced the evolutionary relationships among the species. (Science 2020)
- Common: I deduced from his silence that he was not happy with the decision.
- 介词搭配: The investigator deduced the truth from the contradictory statements of the witnesses.
- 介词搭配: The solution was deduced by a process of logical elimination.
- 介词搭配: The cause of the accident was deduced through careful examination of the wreckage.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 从现场留下的线索，侦探推断出了发生的事情。
- 研究人员从数据中推断该药物在大多数情况下是有效的。
- 根据发现的化石，科学家推断该地区曾经在水下。
- 通过比较DNA序列，研究团队推断出了这些物种之间的进化关系。
- 我从他的沉默推断出他对这个决定不满意。
- 调查人员从证人相互矛盾的陈述中推断出了真相。
- 通过逻辑排除法推断出了解决方案。
- 通过对残骸的仔细检查推断出了事故原因。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-23-deduce)

### 24. deduct <a id="24-deduct"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēductus*, past participle of *dēdūcere* ("to lead away, withdraw"), from *dē-* ("away") + *dūcere* ("to lead").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēductus*，*dēdūcere*（"引开，扣除"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *dūcere*（"引导"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take away an amount or part from a total; to subtract
- CN: 扣除，减去

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used in financial contexts: deduct tax from salary — 常用于财务语境：从工资中扣税
- "Deduct from" is the standard preposition pattern — "Deduct from" 是标准介词搭配
- "Deductible" (adj.) = can be deducted for tax purposes — "Deductible" (形容词) = 可扣除的（用于税务）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deduct from
- deduct an amount
- deduct expenses
- deduct taxes
- deduct points
- deduct from salary
- automatically deduct
- deduct costs

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……中扣除
- 扣除金额
- 扣除费用
- 扣除税款
- 扣分
- 从工资中扣除
- 自动扣除
- 扣除成本
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deduct from one's salary
- deduct for damages
- deduct by the employer
- deduct at source

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从工资中扣除
- 因损坏而扣款
- 由雇主扣除
- 从源头扣除（代扣）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The employer deducted income tax directly from the employee's wages. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Business expenses can be deducted from taxable income. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The bank deducts a monthly service fee from your account. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The researchers deducted the background noise from the signal to obtain accurate measurements. (Physics Review 2021)
- Common: Points will be deducted for late submissions.
- 介词搭配: The cost of the damaged equipment was deducted from his final paycheck.
- 介词搭配: A fee was deducted for the late return of the library books.
- 介词搭配: The tax is deducted by the employer before the salary is paid.
- 介词搭配: Income tax is deducted at source in most countries.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 业务费用可以从应税收入中扣除。
- 银行每月从你的账户中扣除服务费。
- 研究人员从信号中扣除背景噪音以获得精确的测量数据。
- 逾期提交将被扣分。
- 损坏设备的费用从他的最后一张工资支票中扣除了。
- 因图书归还逾期而被收取了费用。
- 税款在发放工资前由雇主代扣。
- 大多数国家在源头代扣所得税。
</details>

### 25. deem <a id="25-deem"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *dēman* ("to judge, declare, deem"), from Proto-Germanic *dōmijaną* ("to judge"), from *dōmaz* ("judgment").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *dēman*（"判断，宣布，认为"），源自原始日耳曼语 *dōmijaną*（"判断"），源自 *dōmaz*（"判断"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to consider or judge something in a particular way; to have a particular opinion about something
- CN: 认为，视为；相信

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal word, common in legal and official contexts — 正式词汇，常用于法律和官方语境
- Often used in passive: "be deemed" — 常用被动语态："be deemed"
- "Deem it necessary/appropriate to do sth" — 认为做某事有必要/合适
- Can be followed by object complement: deem sb/sth + adj/noun — 可接宾语补足语：deem sb/sth + 形容词/名词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deem necessary/appropriate
- deem it necessary to
- be deemed as
- deem sb/sth adj
- deem that
- deem sufficient
- deem acceptable

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 认为有必要/合适
- 认为有必要做
- 被视为
- 认为某人/某物……
- 认为……
- 视为足够
- 认为可接受
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deem as essential
- deem by the authorities
- deem of great importance

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 视为必不可少
- 被当局认为
- 被认为非常重要
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The court deemed the evidence insufficient to support the charges. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The government deemed it necessary to impose stricter regulations. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Any behavior deemed inappropriate by the committee may result in disqualification. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The drug was deemed safe for human use after extensive clinical trials. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: She deemed it wise to keep her opinions to herself.
- 介词搭配: The proposal was deemed as impractical by the board members.
- 介词搭配: The contract was deemed valid by the legal authorities.
- 介词搭配: The research was deemed of great importance to the scientific community.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 法院认为证据不足以支持这些指控。
- 政府认为有必要实施更严格的规定。
- 任何被委员会认为不恰当的行为可能导致取消资格。
- 经过广泛的临床试验，该药物被认为对人体使用是安全的。
- 她认为明智的做法是保持沉默，不发表意见。
- 该提案被董事会成员视为不切实际。
- 该合同被法律机构认定为有效。
- 这项研究被认为对科学界非常重要。
</details>

### 26. default <a id="26-default"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *default* ("fault, deficiency, default"), from *default* ("to fail, lack"), from Latin *dēfallere* ("to fail, be lacking"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *fallere* ("to deceive, disappoint").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *default*（"过错，缺失，违约"），源自 *default*（"失败，缺乏"），源自拉丁语 *dēfallere*（"未能，缺乏"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *fallere*（"欺骗，使失望"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to fail to do something that you are legally required to do, especially to pay a debt; (computing) to revert to a standard setting
- CN: 违约，不履行义务；（计算机）恢复到默认设置

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Most commonly used in financial contexts: default on a loan — 最常用于财务语境：拖欠贷款
- "In default of" (formal) = because of lack of — "In default of"（正式）= 因为缺乏
- As a noun: "by default" = because of no alternative — 作名词时："by default" = 默认情况下
- In computing: "default to" a setting — 在计算机中：默认采用某种设置

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- default on a loan
- default on a payment
- default on a mortgage
- default on a debt
- default by
- default in

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拖欠贷款
- 逾期未付款
- 拖欠按揭
- 拖欠债务
- 由……违约
- 在……方面未履行
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- default on one's obligations
- default in payment
- default to a safe mode

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 未履行义务
- 付款违约
- 默认恢复到安全模式
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many homeowners defaulted on their mortgages during the financial crisis. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: If the borrower defaults on the loan, the bank has the right to seize the property. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The company defaulted on its debt payments and was forced into bankruptcy. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study examined factors that increase the likelihood of small businesses defaulting on loans. (Journal of Economics 2021)
- Common: The software defaults to the standard settings if no configuration file is found.
- 介词搭配: The country defaulted on its international debt obligations last year.
- 介词搭配: The tenant defaulted in payment of rent for three consecutive months.
- 介词搭配: The system will default to basic mode if the advanced settings are corrupted.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 金融危机期间，许多房主拖欠了按揭贷款。
- 如果借款人拖欠贷款，银行有权没收财产。
- 该公司拖欠债务，被迫破产。
- 这项研究考察了增加小企业贷款违约可能性的因素。
- 如果没有找到配置文件，该软件默认使用标准设置。
- 该国去年拖欠了其国际债务。
- 房客连续三个月未付房租。
- 如果高级设置损坏，系统将默认恢复到基本模式。
</details>

### 27. defeat <a id="27-defeat"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *desfait* ("undone, destroyed"), past participle of *desfaire* ("to undo, destroy"), from Latin *dis-* ("apart") + *facere* ("to do, make").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *desfait*（"未做的，毁坏的"），*desfaire*（"取消，毁灭"）的过去分词，源自拉丁语 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *facere*（"做，制造"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to win against someone in a war, competition, or election; to prevent something from succeeding
- CN: 击败，战胜；使失败，挫败

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used in military, sports, and political contexts — 用于军事、体育和政治语境
- "Defeat the purpose" = make the purpose meaningless — "Defeat the purpose" = 使目的失去意义
- "Admit defeat" = accept that you have lost — "Admit defeat" = 承认失败
- Stronger than "beat" in formal contexts — 在正式语境中比 "beat" 语气更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- defeat an enemy
- defeat a proposal
- defeat the purpose
- defeat a team
- decisively defeat
- admit defeat
- suffer defeat
- narrowly defeat
- defeat an opponent

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 击败敌人
- 否决提案
- 使目的失去意义
- 击败一支队伍
- 决定性击败
- 承认失败
- 遭受失败
- 险胜
- 击败对手
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- defeat by a narrow margin
- defeat in the election
- defeat through superior strategy
- defeat on a technicality

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 以微弱差距落败
- 在选举中落败
- 因更优的策略而被击败
- 因技术细节而失败
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The home team defeated the visitors by three goals to one. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The opposition's attempt to block the bill was defeated in Parliament. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The candidate narrowly defeated the incumbent to win the election. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The immune system can defeat most common viruses without medical intervention. (Nature Immunology 2021)
- Common: If you let fear stop you, you have already defeated yourself.
- 介词搭配: The champion was defeated by a newcomer in an upset match.
- 介词搭配: The government was defeated in the general election after a decade in power.
- 介词搭配: The rebel forces were defeated through a combination of air strikes and ground operations.
- 介词搭配: The case was defeated on a technicality in court.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 主队以三比一击败了客队。
- 反对派阻止该法案的企图在议会被挫败。
- 该候选人以微弱优势击败了在任者，赢得了选举。
- 免疫系统可以在没有医疗干预的情况下击败大多数常见病毒。
- 如果你让恐惧阻止你，你就已经打败了自己。
- 冠军在一场爆冷的比赛中被一名新手击败。
- 执政十年后，该政府在大选中落败。
- 叛军通过空袭和地面行动的联合攻势被击败。
- 该案件因法庭上的技术细节而败诉。
</details>

### 28. defect <a id="28-defect"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēfectus* ("failure, lack"), past participle of *dēficere* ("to desert, fail, be lacking"), from *dē-* ("down, away") + *facere* ("to do, make").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēfectus*（"失败，缺乏"），*dēficere*（"叛离，失败，缺乏"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"向下，离开"）+ *facere*（"做，制造"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to leave a country, political party, or organization to join an opposing one
- CN: 叛离，背叛，投奔敌方

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used primarily in political and military contexts — 主要用于政治和军事语境
- "Defect from X to Y" = leave X to join Y — "Defect from X to Y" = 离开X加入Y
- The noun "defect" (with different pronunciation) means a flaw — 名词 "defect"（发音不同）意为缺陷
- Strong negative connotation in the original context, but can be neutral — 在原始语境中有强烈的负面含义，但也可能是中性的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- defect from
- defect to
- defect from a party
- defect to the enemy
- defect from a country
- defect to the opposition

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……叛离
- 投奔……
- 脱离政党
- 投敌
- 叛离国家
- 投靠反对派
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- defect from the army
- defect to the other side
- defect in favor of the rebels

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从军队叛离
- 投靠另一方
- 倒向叛军一方
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Several high-ranking officials defected to the opposition party. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The scientist defected from his home country to seek political asylum abroad. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: During the war, a number of soldiers defected to the enemy side. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The study analyzed why some members choose to defect from political organizations. (Political Science Quarterly 2020)
- Common: He defected from the company to join a competitor.
- 介词搭配: The general defected from the national army and joined the rebel forces.
- 介词搭配: Several key witnesses defected to the prosecution's side during the trial.
- 介词搭配: A faction of the party defected in favor of the rival candidate.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 几位高级官员倒向了反对党。
- 这位科学家叛离祖国，到国外寻求政治庇护。
- 战争期间，一些士兵投向了敌方。
- 该研究分析了为什么有些成员选择脱离政治组织。
- 他离开公司，加入了竞争对手。
- 这位将军脱离了国家军队，加入了叛军。
- 审判期间，几名关键证人倒向了控方一方。
- 该党派的一个派系倒向了竞争候选人一边。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-28-defect)

### 29. defend <a id="29-defend"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *defendre* ("to defend, protect"), from Latin *dēfendere* ("to ward off, protect"), from *dē-* ("away") + *fendere* ("to strike, thrust").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *defendre*（"防御，保护"），源自拉丁语 *dēfendere*（"挡开，保护"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *fendere*（"打击，刺"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to protect someone or something from attack or harm; to support an idea or argument against criticism
- CN: 防御，保护；辩护，辩解

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both physical (defend a country) and figurative (defend an argument) — 既有字面义（保卫国家）也有比喻义（为论点辩护）
- "Defend against/from" = protect — "Defend against/from" = 保护免受
- "Defend oneself" = resist attack or argue one's case — "Defend oneself" = 自卫/为自己辩护
- In sports: "defend a title" = try to remain champion — 在体育中："defend a title" = 卫冕

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- defend oneself
- defend against
- defend from
- defend a title
- defend a position
- defend a case
- defend sb's rights
- defend vigorously
- defend strongly
- defend the interests of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 自卫/为自己辩护
- 防御
- 保护免受
- 卫冕
- 捍卫立场
- 为案件辩护
- 捍卫某人的权利
- 极力辩护
- 坚决辩护
- 捍卫……的利益
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- defend against attack
- defend from harm
- defend with evidence
- defend by arguing
- defend on the grounds of
- defend before the court

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 防御攻击
- 保护免受伤害
- 用证据辩护
- 通过辩论辩护
- 以……为由辩护
- 在法庭上辩护
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The soldiers fought bravely to defend their country against invasion. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The lawyer defended her client's interests throughout the trial. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Plants have evolved various mechanisms to defend themselves from herbivores. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The immune system defends the body against pathogens and foreign substances. (Nature 2021)
- Common: He always defends his opinions strongly in discussions.
- 介词搭配: The castle was built to defend against enemy invasions.
- 介词搭配: A helmet can help defend your head from serious injury.
- 介词搭配: She defended her thesis with extensive data and analysis.
- 介词搭配: The company defended its actions by citing industry standards.
- 介词搭配: The policy was defended on the grounds of national security.
- 介词搭配: The accused will defend himself before the court next week.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 士兵们英勇作战，保卫国家免受侵略。
- 律师在整个审判过程中维护了委托人的利益。
- 植物进化出了各种机制来防御食草动物。
- 免疫系统保护身体免受病原体和外来物质的侵害。
- 他在讨论中总是坚定地捍卫自己的观点。
- 这座城堡是为了防御敌人的入侵而建造的。
- 头盔有助于保护头部免受严重伤害。
- 她用大量的数据和分析来为自己的论文辩护。
- 该公司以行业标准为由为其行为辩护。
- 该政策以国家安全为由进行了辩护。
- 被告下周将在法庭上为自己辩护。
</details>

### 30. defer <a id="30-defer"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *differer* ("to delay, defer"), from Latin *differre* ("to carry apart, postpone, differ"), from *dis-* ("apart") + *ferre* ("to carry, bear").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *differer*（"延迟，推迟"），源自拉丁语 *differre*（"分开携带，推迟，不同"），源自 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *ferre*（"携带，承受"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to delay something until a later time; to accept someone else's opinion or authority
- CN: 推迟，延期；遵从，听从

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Defer to" (intransitive) = to yield to another's opinion/authority — "Defer to"（不及物）= 遵从他人的意见/权威
- "Defer a decision/plan" (transitive) = to postpone — "Defer a decision/plan"（及物）= 推迟决定/计划
- "Deferred payment" = payment later — "Deferred payment" = 延期付款
- More formal than "postpone" — 比 "postpone" 更正式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- defer to
- defer a decision
- defer payment
- defer a plan
- defer to sb's opinion
- defer to experience
- defer judgment
- defer action

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 遵从
- 推迟决定
- 延期付款
- 推迟计划
- 听从某人的意见
- 遵从经验
- 推迟判断
- 推迟行动
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- defer to the experts
- defer for a week
- defer until next month
- defer on a matter
- defer in favor of sb

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 听从专家意见
- 推迟一周
- 推迟到下个月
- 在某事上推迟决定
- 让位于某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The committee decided to defer the final decision until more information is available. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: Students can defer their enrollment for a year if they choose to travel or work. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In such technical matters, I defer to the judgment of the specialists. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The publication of the research was deferred pending further peer review. (Science 2021)
- Common: Let's defer this discussion to the next meeting.
- 介词搭配: On legal questions, the court defers to the expertise of the attorney general.
- 介词搭配: The project was deferred for six months due to budget constraints.
- 介词搭配: The payment deadline was deferred until the end of the quarter.
- 介词搭配: The committee deferred on the controversial issue to avoid conflict.
- 介词搭配: The younger members deferred in favor of the senior professor's recommendation.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 委员会决定推迟最终决定，直到获得更多信息。
- 学生可以选择旅行或工作，将入学时间推迟一年。
- 在这样的技术问题上，我听从专家的判断。
- 这项研究的发表被推迟，等待进一步的同行评议。
- 我们把这次讨论留到下个会议吧。
- 在法律问题上，法院遵从司法部长的专业意见。
- 由于预算限制，该项目被推迟了六个月。
- 付款截止日期被推迟到季度末。
- 委员会在争议性问题上推迟做出决定以避免冲突。
- 年轻成员们让位，支持资深教授的建议。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-30-defer)
