### 31. define <a id="31-define"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēfīnīre* ("to limit, determine, define"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *fīnīre* ("to limit, bound"), from *fīnis* ("boundary, limit").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēfīnīre*（"限定，确定，定义"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *fīnīre*（"限定"），源自 *fīnis*（"边界，界限"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to state or describe the exact meaning of a word or concept; to mark out the limits of something
- CN: 定义，解释；限定，界定

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Clearly/precisely define a term — 清晰地/精确地定义术语
- "Define A as B" — 将A定义为B
- Often used in academic and technical writing — 常用于学术和技术写作
- Can be used with wh-clauses: define what/how — 可与wh-从句连用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- define a term
- define a concept
- define clearly/precisely
- define as
- define the boundaries of
- define the scope of
- define one's role
- define success
- define by law
- define in terms of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 定义术语
- 定义概念
- 清晰地定义
- 定义为
- 界定边界
- 界定范围
- 界定某人的角色
- 定义成功
- 由法律界定
- 用……来定义
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- define X as Y
- define by legislation
- define in a contract
- define through precedent
- define according to
- define for the purpose of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将X定义为Y
- 由立法界定
- 在合同中界定
- 通过先例界定
- 根据……定义
- 为……目的而定义
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is important to clearly define the terms used in the research. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The constitution defines the powers of each branch of government. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In biology, a species is defined as a group of organisms capable of interbreeding. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study defines emotional intelligence as the ability to perceive and manage emotions. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Can you define what you mean by "success"?
- 介词搭配: The term "sustainable development" was defined by the UN as meeting present needs without compromising future generations.
- 介词搭配: The duties of the position are defined in the employee handbook.
- 介词搭配: Acceptable behavior is defined by law and social norms.
- 介词搭配: The concept was defined through decades of philosophical debate.
- 介词搭配: The criteria were defined according to internationally recognized standards.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 清晰地定义研究中使用的术语非常重要。
- 宪法界定了政府各部门的权力。
- 在生物学中，物种被定义为能够杂交繁殖的一组生物体。
- 该研究将情商定义为感知和管理情绪的能力。
- 你能解释一下你说的"成功"是什么意思吗？
- "可持续发展"这个术语被联合国定义为在不损害后代利益的前提下满足当前需求。
- 该职位的职责在员工手册中有界定。
- 可接受的行为由法律和社会规范界定。
- 这个概念通过几十年的哲学辩论得以界定。
- 这些标准是根据国际公认的标准定义的。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-31-define)

### 32. defy <a id="32-defy"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *desfier* ("to challenge, defy"), from Latin *dis-* ("apart") + *fīdus* ("faithful"), literally "to renounce one's faith or allegiance."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *desfier*（"挑战，蔑视"），源自拉丁语 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *fīdus*（"忠诚的"），字面意为"放弃信仰或效忠"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to refuse to obey or show respect for someone in authority, a law, or a rule; to successfully resist something
- CN: 违抗，反抗；蔑视；使……不可能，无法（描述/解释）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "resist" — implies open opposition — 比 "resist" 语气更强，暗示公开对抗
- "Defy belief/description/imagination" = to be almost impossible to believe/describe — "Defy belief/description/imagination" = 难以置信/难以描述
- Often used figuratively: behavior defies explanation — 常用于比喻：行为难以解释

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- defy authority
- defy the law
- defy an order
- defy convention
- defy expectations
- defy belief
- defy description
- defy explanation
- defy gravity
- defy the odds

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 违抗权威
- 藐视法律
- 违抗命令
- 违反惯例
- 出乎意料
- 令人难以置信
- 难以描述
- 难以解释
- 克服重力
- 克服困难
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- defy sb to do sth
- defy by continuing
- defy with courage
- defy in the face of danger
- defy despite the risks

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 激某人做某事
- 以坚持来对抗
- 以勇气对抗
- 在危险面前抗争
- 不顾风险
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The acrobat defied gravity with his incredible performance. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The student defied the school's dress code by wearing casual clothes. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The complexity of the ecosystem defies simple description. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The results defy conventional wisdom about the relationship between diet and health. (Nature 2022)
- Common: She defied all expectations and became a successful entrepreneur.
- 介词搭配: I defy you to find a better example of modern architecture in this city.
- 介词搭配: The protesters defied the government by continuing their demonstrations despite the ban.
- 介词搭配: He defied his illness with remarkable courage and determination.
- 介词搭配: The soldier defied the enemy in the face of certain death.
- 介词搭配: They defied the harsh conditions despite the lack of proper equipment.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 那位杂技演员以令人难以置信的表演克服了重力。
- 那名学生穿便装，违反了学校的着装规定。
- 这个生态系统的复杂性难以简单描述。
- 这些结果挑战了关于饮食与健康关系的传统观点。
- 她出乎所有人的意料，成为了一名成功的企业家。
- 我激你在本市找出一个更好的现代建筑范例。
- 抗议者无视禁令，继续示威，与政府对抗。
- 他以非凡的勇气和决心对抗疾病。
- 这名士兵面对必死的局面，依然英勇抗敌。
- 尽管缺乏适当装备，他们还是克服了恶劣条件。
</details>

### 33. degenerate <a id="33-degenerate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēgenerāre* ("to degenerate, decline from ancestral quality"), from *dē-* ("down from") + *genus* ("race, kind"); literally "to depart from one's kind."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēgenerāre*（"退化，堕落"），源自 *dē-*（"从……下来"）+ *genus*（"种族，种类"）；字面意为"脱离自己的种类"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become worse in quality, moral character, or strength; to decline from a higher to a lower state
- CN: 退化，堕落，恶化

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with "into": degenerate into chaos/violence — 常与 "into" 连用：degenerate into chaos/violence
- Stronger negative connotation than "deteriorate" — 比 "deteriorate" 负面含义更强
- Used in biology, sociology, and moral contexts — 用于生物学、社会学和道德语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- degenerate into
- degenerate over time
- degenerate rapidly/gradually
- moral degeneration
- degenerate from
- degenerate to the point of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 退化为，堕落为
- 随时间退化
- 迅速/逐渐退化
- 道德堕落
- 从……退化
- 退化到……地步
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- degenerate into anarchy
- degenerate from a debate to a quarrel
- degenerate over generations
- degenerate under pressure
- degenerate to a lower form

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 退化为无政府状态
- 从辩论恶化为争吵
- 经过几代人退化
- 在压力下退化
- 退化为更低级的形式
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The peaceful protest degenerated into violent clashes with the police. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The conversation degenerated into a bitter argument about politics. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The quality of the ecosystem degenerated rapidly after the introduction of invasive species. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The researchers found that the cells degenerated when exposed to prolonged oxidative stress. (Cell Biology 2021)
- Common: Without proper maintenance, the building's condition will degenerate over time.
- 介词搭配: The political situation degenerated into chaos after the election results were announced.
- 介词搭配: What started as a friendly discussion degenerated from a polite exchange into a heated argument.
- 介词搭配: The species' genetic diversity degenerated over generations due to inbreeding.
- 介词搭配: The tissue degenerated under the combined effects of disease and poor nutrition.
- 介词搭配: The once-thriving civilization degenerated to a primitive state after the collapse of its central government.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 和平抗议演变成了与警方的暴力冲突。
- 对话恶化成了关于政治的激烈争吵。
- 引入入侵物种后，该生态系统的质量迅速退化。
- 研究人员发现，细胞在长时间氧化应激下会退化。
- 没有适当的维护，建筑物的状况会随时间恶化。
- 选举结果公布后，政治局势陷入混乱。
- 开始的友好讨论从礼貌交流变成了激烈争吵。
- 由于近亲繁殖，该物种的遗传多样性经过几代后有所退化。
- 在疾病和营养不良的共同作用下，组织退化了。
- 中央政府崩溃后，曾经繁荣的文明退化到了原始状态。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-33-degenerate)

### 34. degrade <a id="34-degrade"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *degrader* ("to degrade, deprive of office"), from Latin *dēgradārī* ("to degrade, reduce in rank"), from *dē-* ("down") + *gradus* ("step, grade").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *degrader*（"降级，剥夺职务"），源自拉丁语 *dēgradārī*（"降级，降低等级"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *gradus*（"台阶，等级"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to treat someone as if they are worthless or inferior; to reduce the quality or value of something; (chemistry) to break down
- CN: 贬低，降低身份；降低质量；降解

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used in both social and environmental contexts — 用于社会和环保语境
- "Degrade oneself" = to lower one's own dignity — "Degrade oneself" = 降低自己的尊严
- In environmental science: "biodegradable" = capable of being broken down — 在环境科学中："biodegradable" = 可生物降解的
- Stronger than "insult" in terms of dignity — 在尊严方面比 "insult" 更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- degrade sb
- feel degraded
- degrade the environment
- degrade the quality of
- degrade oneself
- degrade the value of
- degrade the status of
- chemically degrade
- degrade slowly/quickly

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 贬低某人
- 感到被贬低
- 破坏环境
- 降低质量
- 贬低自己
- 降低价值
- 降低地位
- 化学降解
- 缓慢/快速降解
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- degrade by treating unfairly
- degrade from a position
- degrade into a lower state
- degrade to a lower level
- degrade through neglect

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过不公平对待来贬低
- 从某个职位降级
- 退化为更低的状态
- 降低到更低的水平
- 因疏忽而退化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The manager's constant criticism degraded the employees and lowered their morale. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The quality of the product has degraded significantly due to the use of cheaper materials. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Plastic waste takes hundreds of years to degrade in the natural environment. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The study found that soil quality degrades rapidly when forests are cleared for agriculture. (Environmental Science 2021)
- Common: Making fun of others in public is a sure way to degrade yourself.
- 介词搭配: The prisoners were degraded by being forced to work in humiliating conditions.
- 介词搭配: The officer was degraded from captain to lieutenant for misconduct.
- 介词搭配: The argument quickly degraded into a personal attack.
- 介词搭配: The fabric has degraded to a point where it can no longer be used.
- 介词搭配: The historic building degraded through years of exposure to weather and pollution.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经理不断的批评贬低了员工，降低了他们的士气。
- 由于使用更便宜的材料，产品质量显著下降。
- 塑料垃圾在自然环境中需要数百年才能降解。
- 研究发现，森林被砍伐用于农业后，土壤质量迅速退化。
- 当众取笑别人肯定是在贬低自己。
- 囚犯被迫在屈辱的条件下工作，受尽了侮辱。
- 该军官因行为不当从上尉降为中尉。
- 争论很快沦为个人攻击。
- 这块布料已经坏到不能用了。
- 由于常年风吹日晒和污染，这座历史建筑逐渐破败。
</details>

### 35. delay <a id="35-delay"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *delaier* ("to put off, delay"), from Latin *dīlātāre* ("to extend, put off"), frequentative of *differre* ("to postpone").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *delaier*（"推迟，延迟"），源自拉丁语 *dīlātāre*（"延长，推迟"），*differre*（"推迟"）的反复形式。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something happen at a later time than originally planned; to cause someone to be late
- CN: 推迟，延误；使迟到

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive (delay a decision) and intransitive (don't delay) — 既可及物也可不及物
- "Delay doing sth" (American English: "delay in doing") — 后接动名词
- "Without delay" = immediately (adverb phrase) — "Without delay" = 立即
- Often used in transportation and project management — 常用于交通和项目管理

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- delay a decision
- delay the start of
- delay payment
- delay the release of
- delay the implementation of
- delay until/till
- delay for a week/month
- delay due to weather
- flight/train delayed
- without delay

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 推迟决定
- 推迟开始
- 延迟付款
- 推迟发布
- 推迟实施
- 推迟到
- 推迟一周/一个月
- 因天气而延误
- 航班/火车延误
- 毫不拖延地
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- delay by two hours
- delay for a period of time
- delay until further notice
- delay on the grounds of
- delay in doing sth
- delay due to circumstances
- delay out of caution

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 延迟两个小时
- 推迟一段时间
- 推迟到另行通知
- 以……为由推迟
- 在……方面拖延
- 因情况而延迟
- 出于谨慎而推迟
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The flight was delayed by three hours due to heavy fog. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The government decided to delay the implementation of the new tax policy. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The construction project was delayed for six months because of funding issues. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: Early diagnosis and treatment can delay the onset of the disease. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Don't delay sending in your application — the deadline is Friday.
- 介词搭配: The meeting was delayed by 30 minutes because the presenter was stuck in traffic.
- 介词搭配: The launch of the new product has been delayed for several months.
- 介词搭配: The court case was delayed until next year due to lack of evidence.
- 介词搭配: The company delayed making a decision on the grounds that more data was needed.
- 介词搭配: The train was delayed in arriving because of signal problems.
- 介词搭配: The surgery was delayed due to the patient's unstable condition.
- 介词搭配: The announcement was delayed out of respect for the victims' families.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 航班因大雾延误了三个小时。
- 政府决定推迟实施新的税收政策。
- 由于资金问题，这个建设项目被推迟了六个月。
- 早期诊断和治疗可以延缓疾病的发作。
- 不要拖延提交申请——截止日期是周五。
- 会议因主讲人堵车而延迟了30分钟。
- 新产品的发布已被推迟了数月。
- 由于缺乏证据，该案件被推迟到明年审理。
- 该公司以需要更多数据为由推迟了决定。
- 由于信号故障，火车延迟到站。
- 由于病人情况不稳定，手术被推迟了。
- 出于对受害者家属的尊重，公告被推迟发布。
</details>

### 36. delegate <a id="36-delegate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēlēgātus* ("sent, delegated"), past participle of *dēlēgāre* ("to send, assign, delegate"), from *dē-* ("from") + *lēgāre* ("to send with a commission").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēlēgātus*（"派遣的，委派的"），*dēlēgāre*（"派遣，分配，委派"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"从"）+ *lēgāre*（"派遣"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give part of your work, power, or authority to someone in a lower position; to choose someone to represent a group
- CN: 授权，委派；委托

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Crucial management skill — delegate tasks/responsibilities — 重要的管理技能——委派任务/职责
- "Delegate A to B" = assign A to B — "Delegate A to B" = 将A委派给B
- As a noun: same spelling, different pronunciation — 作名词时拼写相同，发音不同
- Not used for delegating personal matters — 不用于委托个人事务

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- delegate work/tasks
- delegate responsibility
- delegate authority/power
- delegate decisions
- delegate to a subordinate
- delegate effectively
- delegate the job to sb

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 委派工作/任务
- 委派职责
- 授予权力
- 授权决策
- 委派给下属
- 有效授权
- 将工作委托给某人
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- delegate to a committee
- delegate by written authority
- delegate with full power
- delegate on behalf of
- delegate for a specific purpose

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 委托给委员会
- 通过书面授权委托
- 授权全权处理
- 代表……委托
- 为特定目的而委托
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: The project manager delegated the research phase to a specialized consulting firm. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The principal investigator delegated data analysis responsibilities to postdoctoral researchers. (Nature 2021)
- Common: You can't do everything yourself — you need to learn to delegate.
- 介词搭配: The task was delegated to a junior staff member for completion.
- 介词搭配: The ambassador was delegated by the president to negotiate the trade agreement.
- 介词搭配: The committee was delegated with full authority to make the final decision.
- 介词搭配: The vice president delegated on behalf of the CEO at the international conference.
- 介词搭配: A special representative was delegated for the purpose of mediating the dispute.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 项目经理将研究阶段委托给了一家专业咨询公司。
- 课题负责人将数据分析职责委派给了博士后研究人员。
- 你不可能什么事都自己做——你需要学会授权。
- 任务被委派给一名初级员工去完成。
- 大使由总统委派去谈判贸易协定。
- 委员会被授予全权做出最终决定。
- 副总裁代表首席执行官出席了国际会议。
- 为调解争端的目的委派了一名特别代表。
</details>

### 37. delete <a id="37-delete"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēlētus*, past participle of *dēlēre* ("to destroy, wipe out, efface"), origin uncertain; possibly from *dē-* ("off") + *linere* ("to smear, rub out").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēlētus*，*dēlēre*（"毁灭，擦掉，抹去"）的过去分词；词源不确定，可能源自 *dē-*（"去掉"）+ *linere*（"涂抹，擦掉"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to remove or erase something (especially text, data, or files) that has been written, recorded, or stored
- CN: 删除，删去；消除

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily used in computing and editing contexts — 主要用于计算和编辑语境
- "Delete from" = remove from a place — "Delete from" = 从某处删除
- "Delete" is stronger than "remove" in digital contexts — 在数字语境中比 "remove" 语气更强
- Irreversible in many contexts — 在许多语境中是不可逆的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- delete a file
- delete text/data
- delete an email
- delete an account
- delete the message
- delete permanently
- delete completely
- delete from the record
- delete accidentally
- delete by mistake

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 删除文件
- 删除文本/数据
- 删除邮件
- 删除账户
- 删除消息
- 永久删除
- 完全删除
- 从记录中删除
- 意外删除
- 误删
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- delete from the list
- delete by selecting and pressing
- delete without a trace
- delete in error
- delete on request

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从列表中删除
- 通过选择并按键删除
- 不留痕迹地删除
- 错误删除
- 按要求删除
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I accidentally deleted the important file and couldn't recover it. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The editor deleted several paragraphs from the article to meet the word limit. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The software allows you to delete unnecessary data to free up storage space. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: Outliers were deleted from the dataset to improve statistical significance. (Science 2021)
- Common: Please delete my name from the mailing list.
- 介词搭配: The virus deleted all the files from the hard drive.
- 介词搭配: Unwanted programs can be deleted by using the control panel.
- 介词搭配: The spy managed to delete the evidence without a trace.
- 介词搭配: Several records were deleted in error during the system migration.
- 介词搭配: Personal data will be deleted on request in accordance with privacy regulations.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我不小心删除了重要文件，无法恢复。
- 编辑从文章中删除了几段，以满足字数限制。
- 该软件允许你删除不必要的数据以释放存储空间。
- 异常值被从数据集中删除，以提高统计显著性。
- 请把我的名字从邮件列表中删除。
- 病毒删除了硬盘上的所有文件。
- 不需要的程序可以通过控制面板来删除。
- 间谍设法不留痕迹地删除了证据。
- 系统迁移期间有几条记录被错误删除。
- 根据隐私规定，个人数据可应要求删除。
</details>

### 38. deliberate <a id="38-deliberate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēlīberātus*, past participle of *dēlīberāre* ("to weigh, consider, deliberate"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *lībra* ("balance, scale").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēlīberātus*，*dēlīberāre*（"权衡，考虑，商议"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *lībra*（"天平，秤"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to think carefully about something, especially before making a decision; to discuss something formally
- CN: 仔细考虑；商议

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive (deliberate what to do) and intransitive (deliberate about/on/over) — 既可及物也可不及物
- More formal than "think about" — 比 "think about" 更正式
- As an adjective, "deliberate" (adj.) means intentional — 作形容词时意为故意的
- Often used in legal, political, and formal decision-making contexts — 常用于法律、政治和正式决策语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deliberate about/on/over
- deliberate carefully/seriously
- deliberate whether
- deliberate the question
- deliberate the matter
- deliberate at length
- deliberate in private
- deliberate as a group

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 仔细考虑
- 认真/仔细审议
- 考虑是否
- 审议问题
- 审议事项
- 长时间商议
- 私下商议
- 集体商议
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deliberate on a proposal
- deliberate about the future
- deliberate over the details
- deliberate among themselves
- deliberate behind closed doors
- deliberate with counsel

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 就提案进行商议
- 考虑未来
- 斟酌细节
- 内部商议
- 闭门商议
- 与法律顾问商议
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The jury deliberated for several hours before reaching a verdict. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The committee deliberated on whether to approve the funding for the research project. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The board deliberated over the proposed changes to the curriculum. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The panel deliberated the ethical implications of the new genetic technology. (Nature 2022)
- Common: We need to deliberate before making such a big decision.
- 介词搭配: The senators deliberated on the proposed legislation for weeks.
- 介词搭配: The management deliberated about the best strategy for market expansion.
- 介词搭配: They deliberated over every detail of the contract before signing it.
- 介词搭配: The members deliberated among themselves before presenting their conclusions.
- 介词搭配: The negotiations were deliberated behind closed doors.
- 介词搭配: The defendant deliberated with his legal counsel before entering a plea.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 陪审团商议了几个小时才做出裁决。
- 委员会审议了是否批准该研究项目的资金。
- 董事会就课程改革的提议进行了商议。
- 专家组讨论了新基因技术的伦理影响。
- 在做出如此重大的决定之前，我们需要仔细考虑。
- 参议员们就拟议的立法商议了数周。
- 管理层考虑了市场扩张的最佳策略。
- 他们在签署合同之前斟酌了每一个细节。
- 成员们在提交结论之前进行了内部商议。
- 谈判是闭门进行的。
- 被告与律师商议后才进行抗辩。
</details>

### 39. delight <a id="39-delight"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *delitier* ("to please, charm"), from Latin *dēlectāre* ("to delight, charm, please"), frequentative of *dēlicere* ("to entice, allure"), from *dē-* ("away") + *lacere* ("to entice").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *delitier*（"使高兴，使愉悦"），源自拉丁语 *dēlectāre*（"使愉悦，使陶醉"），*dēlicere*（"引诱，吸引"）的反复形式，源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *lacere*（"引诱"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give someone great pleasure or joy; to be very pleased by or to enjoy something greatly
- CN: 使高兴，使欣喜；非常喜欢，以……为乐

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Delight in" = to take great pleasure in — "Delight in" = 以……为乐
- Often used in passive: "be delighted (at/by/with)" — 常用被动语态
- "Be delighted to do" = very willing to do — "Be delighted to do" = 很乐意做
- Stronger than "please" — 比 "please" 语气更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- delight in doing sth
- delight the audience
- delight sb with
- be delighted at/by/with
- be delighted to do
- delight the senses
- delight the eye
- great delight
- sheer delight

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 以做某事为乐
- 使观众喜悦
- 以……使某人高兴
- 对……感到欣喜
- 很高兴做某事
- 愉悦感官
- 赏心悦目
- 极大的快乐
- 纯粹的快乐
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- delight in teasing others
- delight at the news
- delight with a surprise
- delight by performing
- delight of the audience

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 以取笑他人为乐
- 对消息感到欣喜
- 以惊喜使某人高兴
- 通过表演使人愉悦
- 观众的喜悦
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The children delighted the audience with their lively performance. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: She delighted in reading classic literature and spent hours in the library. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The intricate designs of the temple delighted visitors from around the world. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The discovery delighted the scientific community, as it opened new avenues for research. (Science 2021)
- Common: We were delighted to hear about your engagement!
- 介词搭配: The toddler delighted in pulling the dog's tail, much to his mother's dismay.
- 介词搭配: The team was delighted at the news of their promotion.
- 介词搭配: The magician delighted the children with his clever tricks.
- 介词搭配: The audience was delighted by the pianist's stunning performance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 孩子们以活泼的表演使观众感到愉悦。
- 她以阅读经典文学为乐，在图书馆一待就是几个小时。
- 寺庙精妙的设计令来自世界各地的游客赏心悦目。
- 这一发现令科学界欣喜，因为它开辟了新的研究途径。
- 听说你订婚了我们非常高兴！
- 那个学步的小孩以拉狗的尾巴为乐，这让他的母亲很无奈。
- 团队对晋升的消息感到欣喜。
- 魔术师用巧妙的手法让孩子们高兴不已。
- 观众被钢琴家精彩的表演深深吸引。
</details>

### 40. deliver <a id="40-deliver"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *delivrer* ("to set free, deliver"), from Late Latin *dēlīberāre* ("to set free"), from Latin *dē-* ("from") + *līberāre* ("to free"), from *līber* ("free").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *delivrer*（"释放，交付"），源自后期拉丁语 *dēlīberāre*（"释放"），源自拉丁语 *dē-*（"从"）+ *līberāre*（"使自由"），源自 *līber*（"自由的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take goods, letters, etc. to a particular place; to give a speech or lecture; to help a baby be born; to fulfill a promise
- CN: 递送，交付；发表（演讲）；接生；履行（承诺）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Multiple distinct meanings across contexts — 在多种语境中有不同含义
- "Deliver a speech/lecture/talk" = to give formally — "Deliver a speech/lecture/talk" = 发表正式演讲
- "Deliver on" = to fulfill a promise/expectation — "Deliver on" = 履行承诺/达到期望
- "Deliver a baby" = to assist at childbirth — "Deliver a baby" = 接生

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deliver a speech
- deliver a package
- deliver a service
- deliver results
- deliver on a promise
- deliver a baby
- deliver goods
- deliver a verdict
- deliver an ultimatum
- deliver a message

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 发表演讲
- 递送包裹
- 提供服务
- 交付成果
- 履行承诺
- 接生婴儿
- 交付货物
- 宣布裁决
- 发出最后通牒
- 传达信息
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deliver to sb's doorstep
- deliver by courier
- deliver on time
- deliver in person
- deliver under the terms of
- deliver via email
- deliver against expectations
- deliver through cooperation

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 送到某人家门口
- 由快递送达
- 按时交付
- 亲自送达
- 按条款交付
- 通过电子邮件发送
- 不负期望
- 通过合作完成
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: The company promises to deliver our orders within 24 hours. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The doctor delivered over a thousand babies during her career. (The Lancet 2021)

- 介词搭配: The package was delivered to the wrong address by mistake.
- 介词搭配: The documents will be delivered by express courier tomorrow morning.
- 介词搭配: The contractor failed to deliver on time, causing significant delays.
- 介词搭配: The verdict was delivered in person by the judge in court.
- 介词搭配: Goods must be delivered under the terms specified in the contract.
- 介词搭配: The training will be delivered via an online platform.
- 介词搭配: The new CEO delivered against all expectations and turned the company around.
- 介词搭配: The aid was delivered through a network of local charities.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

### 41. delude <a id="41-delude"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēlūdere* ("to play false, mock, deceive"), from *dē-* ("down, away") + *lūdere* ("to play").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēlūdere*（"欺骗，嘲弄"），源自 *dē-*（"向下，离开"）+ *lūdere*（"玩耍"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone believe something that is not true, especially something that is to their own disadvantage
- CN: 欺骗，哄骗；误导

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Delude oneself" = to refuse to accept the truth — "Delude oneself" = 自欺欺人
- "Delude sb into doing" — 骗某人做某事
- Similar to "deceive" but often implies self-deception — 与 "deceive" 类似，但常指自欺欺人

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- delude oneself
- delude sb into thinking
- be deluded by
- delude the public
- delude with false promises
- delude about the reality

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 自欺欺人
- 欺骗某人以为
- 被……欺骗
- 欺骗公众
- 用虚假承诺欺骗
- 对现实有错误认知
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- delude into believing
- delude about one's abilities
- delude by appearances
- delude with flattery

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 欺骗……相信
- 对自己的能力有错误认知
- 被外表所欺骗
- 用奉承话欺骗
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Some people delude themselves into thinking they can pass the exam without studying. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The public should not be deluded into believing that the problem is solved. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many investors were deluded by the promise of quick profits. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study warns against deluding ourselves that technology alone can solve climate change. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Don't delude yourself — he's not going to change.
- 介词搭配: She deluded herself into believing that everything would be fine.
- 介词搭配: He is deluded about his own talents as a singer.
- 介词搭配: They were deluded by the attractive appearance of the product.
- 介词搭配: The manager deluded his staff with empty promises of promotion.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 有些人自欺欺人地认为不学习也能通过考试。
- 不应欺骗公众，让他们以为问题已经解决。
- 许多投资者被快速获利的承诺所欺骗。
- 该研究警告我们不要自欺欺人地认为仅靠技术就能解决气候变化。
- 别自欺欺人了——他不会改变的。
- 她自欺欺人地认为一切都会好起来。
- 他对自己作为歌手的才能有不切实际的认识。
- 他们被产品漂亮的外观所欺骗。
- 经理用空洞的晋升承诺欺骗员工。
</details>

### 42. demand <a id="42-demand"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *demander* ("to ask, request"), from Latin *dēmandāre* ("to entrust, charge"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *mandāre* ("to entrust, order").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *demander*（"询问，请求"），源自拉丁语 *dēmandāre*（"委托，命令"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *mandāre*（"委托，命令"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to ask for something forcefully and firmly; to require something
- CN: 强烈要求；需要

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "ask" or "request" — implies authority or urgency — 比 "ask" 或 "request" 语气更强，暗示权威或紧迫
- Can be followed by that-clause (usually with subjunctive): demand that he be — 可接that-从句（常用虚拟语气）
- "Demand to do sth" — 要求做某事
- "Demand sth of/from sb" = to require someone to give — "Demand sth of/from sb" = 要求某人提供某物

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- demand an answer
- demand an explanation
- demand action
- demand justice
- demand a refund
- demand that
- demand to know
- demand attention
- demand respect
- demand the right to
- demand sth from/of sb

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 要求解释
- 要求采取行动
- 要求公正
- 要求退款
- 要求……
- 要求知道
- 需要关注
- 需要尊重
- 要求……的权利
- 向某人要求某物
</details>

- demand from the government
- demand of the employees
- demand by the public
- demand under threat
- demand on the basis of
- demand in writing

- 基于……的要求
- 书面要求

- Academic: The growing population demands more sustainable approaches to urban planning. (Science 2021)
- Common: I demand to speak to the manager right now!
- 介词搭配: The protesters demanded justice from the government after the incident.
- 介词搭配: The job demands a high level of skill and dedication of its employees.
- 介词搭配: A full investigation was demanded by the victim's family.
- 介词搭配: He demanded payment under threat of legal action.
- 介词搭配: The refund was demanded on the basis of the product warranty.
- 介词搭配: All demands must be submitted in writing to the committee.
</details>

- 不断增长的人口要求更加可持续的城市规划方法。

- 抗议者要求政府就该事件主持公道。
- 这份工作要求员工有高超的技能和奉献精神。
- 受害者家属要求进行全面调查。
- 他以提起法律诉讼相威胁，要求付款。
- 根据产品保修条款要求退款。
- 所有要求必须书面提交给委员会。
</details>

### 43. demonstrate <a id="43-demonstrate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēmōnstrātus*, past participle of *dēmōnstrāre* ("to show, point out, demonstrate"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *mōnstrāre* ("to show"), from *mōnstrum* ("sign, portent").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēmōnstrātus*，*dēmōnstrāre*（"展示，指出，证明"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *mōnstrāre*（"展示"），源自 *mōnstrum*（"标志，预兆"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to show clearly that something is true or exists; to show and explain how something works; to take part in a public protest
- CN: 证明，示范；展示，演示；游行示威

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Three main uses: proving a point, showing how to do, and public protesting — 三种主要用法：证明观点、示范做法、游行抗议
- "Demonstrate against" = to protest — "Demonstrate against" = 示威抗议
- More formal than "show" — 比 "show" 更正式
- "Demonstrate how" + infinitive — 后接 how + 不定式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- demonstrate that
- demonstrate the effectiveness of
- demonstrate how to
- demonstrate a skill
- demonstrate a product
- demonstrate against
- demonstrate support for
- clearly demonstrate
- conclusively demonstrate
- demonstrate experimentally

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 证明……
- 证明……的有效性
- 演示如何做
- 展示技能
- 展示产品
- 抗议……
- 表示支持
- 清楚证明
- 确凿证明
- 通过实验证明
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- demonstrate by example
- demonstrate through research
- demonstrate in public
- demonstrate to the audience
- demonstrate with data
- demonstrate before a committee
- demonstrate against the policy
- demonstrate in favor of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过实例证明
- 通过研究证明
- 公开展示
- 向观众展示
- 用数据证明
- 在委员会面前展示
- 抗议政策
- 示威支持
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The experiment clearly demonstrates the principle of gravity. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The study demonstrates that early intervention can significantly improve outcomes. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The instructor demonstrated how to use the laboratory equipment safely. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The research conclusively demonstrates a link between air pollution and cognitive decline. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Thousands of people demonstrated against the new law in the city square.
- 介词搭配: The teacher demonstrated the chemical reaction by mixing the two compounds.
- 介词搭配: The theory was demonstrated through a series of controlled experiments.
- 介词搭配: The artist demonstrated her technique to an audience of aspiring painters.
- 介词搭配: The effectiveness of the drug was demonstrated with clinical trial data.
- 介词搭配: The proposal was demonstrated before the review committee.
- 介词搭配: Citizens demonstrated against the proposed tax increase.
- 介词搭配: The crowd demonstrated in favor of greater environmental protections.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这个实验清楚地证明了重力原理。
- 该研究表明早期干预可以显著改善结果。
- 指导老师演示了如何安全使用实验室设备。
- 研究确凿地证明了空气污染与认知能力下降之间存在联系。
- 数千人在城市广场上抗议新法律。
- 老师通过混合两种化合物来演示化学反应。
- 通过一系列对照实验证明了该理论。
- 艺术家向一群有抱负的画家示范了她的技法。
- 用临床试验数据证明了该药物的有效性。
- 该提案在评审委员会面前进行了展示。
- 市民抗议拟议的增税方案。
- 人群游行支持加强环境保护。
</details>

### 44. denote <a id="44-denote"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēnotāre* ("to mark out, signify"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *notāre* ("to mark, note"), from *nota* ("a mark, sign").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēnotāre*（"标示，表示"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *notāre*（"标记，注意"），源自 *nota*（"记号，符号"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be a sign of something; to mean or represent something
- CN: 表示，代表；意指

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- More technical and formal than "mean" — 比 "mean" 更具技术性和正式性
- Used in linguistics, mathematics, logic, and semiotics — 用于语言学、数学、逻辑学和符号学
- "Denote" refers to literal/direct meaning; "connote" refers to associated meaning — 字面含义 vs 联想含义
- Often used in academic and technical writing — 常用于学术和技术写作

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- denote a difference
- denote a change
- denote the presence of
- denote by a symbol
- denote the meaning of
- denote the beginning of
- denote the end of
- denote primarily

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 表示差异
- 表示变化
- 表示……的存在
- 用符号表示
- 表示……的意思
- 表示……的开始
- 表示……的结束
- 主要表示
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- denote by the letter X
- denote in mathematical terms
- denote with an asterisk
- denote as a function of
- denote through notation

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用字母X表示
- 用数学术语表示
- 用星号表示
- 表示为……的函数
- 通过符号表示
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: In mathematics, the symbol π denotes the ratio of a circle's circumference to its diameter. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The term "globalization" denotes the increasing interconnectedness of world economies. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In chemical equations, the arrow denotes the direction of the reaction. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The color red in this diagram denotes areas of high population density. (Geography Review 2021)
- Common: A red traffic light denotes that you must stop.
- 介词搭配: Variables are denoted by letters such as x, y, and z in algebraic expressions.
- 介词搭配: The significance level is denoted in statistical reports as p < 0.05.
- 介词搭配: Footnotes are denoted with an asterisk at the end of the sentence.
- 介词搭配: Stress can be denoted as a function of force per unit area.
- 介词搭配: The different categories are denoted through the use of color-coding.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在数学中，符号π表示圆的周长与直径之比。
- "全球化"一词表示世界经济日益紧密的相互联系。
- 在化学方程式中，箭头表示反应方向。
- 该图表中的红色表示人口密度高的区域。
- 红色交通灯表示你必须停车。
- 在代数表达式中，变量用字母如x、y、z表示。
- 统计报告中用p < 0.05表示显著性水平。
- 脚注在句末用星号表示。
- 应力可以表示为每单位面积受力的函数。
- 不同的类别通过颜色编码来表示。
</details>

### 45. denounce <a id="45-denounce"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *denoncier* ("to proclaim, denounce"), from Latin *dēnuntiāre* ("to announce, proclaim, denounce"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *nuntiāre* ("to announce, report"), from *nuntius* ("messenger").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *denoncier*（"宣布，谴责"），源自拉丁语 *dēnuntiāre*（"宣布，声明，谴责"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *nuntiāre*（"宣布，报告"），源自 *nuntius*（"信使"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to publicly criticize someone or something, or to accuse someone of something; to inform against someone
- CN: 公然谴责；指责，揭发

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Strong public condemnation — 强烈的公开谴责
- "Denounce sb as" = to call someone something publicly — "Denounce sb as" = 公开指责某人为……
- Stronger than "criticize" — implies formal/official disapproval — 比 "criticize" 语气更强，暗示正式/官方的反对
- Often used in political and moral contexts — 常用于政治和道德语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- denounce sb/sth
- denounce violence
- denounce corruption
- denounce injustice
- denounce as false
- denounce as unacceptable
- denounce publicly
- denounce strongly
- denounce in strong terms

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 谴责某人/某物
- 谴责暴力
- 谴责腐败
- 谴责不公
- 谴责为虚假的
- 谴责为不可接受的
- 公开谴责
- 强烈谴责
- 以强硬措辞谴责
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- denounce as a fraud
- denounce for wrongdoing
- denounce in the press
- denounce before the court
- denounce to the authorities
- denounce on moral grounds

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 谴责为欺诈
- 因不法行为而谴责
- 在媒体上谴责
- 在法庭上指控
- 向当局告发
- 基于道德理由谴责
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Human rights organizations denounced the government's treatment of political prisoners. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The opposition party denounced the new policy as harmful to the economy. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The report denounces the use of child labor in the manufacturing industry. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The scientific community denounced the fraudulent research as a serious breach of ethics. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Many celebrities used social media to denounce the injustice.
- 介词搭配: The diplomat was denounced as a spy by the host country's government.
- 介词搭配: The CEO was denounced for his role in the accounting scandal.
- 介词搭配: The organization was denounced in the press for its unethical practices.
- 介词搭配: The witness denounced the accused before the court.
- 介词搭配: The corrupt official was denounced to the anti-corruption agency.
- 介词搭配: The policy was denounced on moral grounds by religious leaders.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 人权组织谴责政府对待政治犯的方式。
- 反对党谴责新政策对经济有害。
- 该报告谴责制造业使用童工。
- 科学界谴责这项欺诈性研究严重违反道德。
- 许多名人在社交媒体上谴责这一不公正行为。
- 该外交官被所在国政府指控为间谍。
- 这位CEO因在会计丑闻中的角色而受到谴责。
- 该组织因不道德的做法在媒体上受到谴责。
- 证人在法庭上指控了被告。
- 该腐败官员被举报到了反腐败机构。
- 宗教领袖基于道德理由谴责了该政策。
</details>

### 46. deny <a id="46-deny"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deniier* ("to deny, refuse"), from Latin *dēnegāre* ("to deny, refuse"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *negāre* ("to say no, deny"), from *nec* ("not").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deniier*（"否认，拒绝"），源自拉丁语 *dēnegāre*（"否认，拒绝"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *negāre*（"说"不"，否认"），源自 *nec*（"不"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to say that something is not true; to refuse to allow someone to have something
- CN: 否认；拒绝（给予）；拒绝承认

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deny doing sth" = claim not to have done — "Deny doing sth" = 否认做过某事
- "Deny sb sth" / "Deny sth to sb" = refuse to give — "Deny sb sth" / "Deny sth to sb" = 拒绝给予某人某物
- "There is no denying that..." = it is obvious that — "There is no denying that..." = 不可否认的是……
- Opposite of "admit" or "confirm" — 与 "admit" 或 "confirm" 相反

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deny the accusation
- deny the allegation
- deny a charge
- deny responsibility
- deny doing sth
- deny that
- deny sb access to
- deny sb the right to
- deny sb the opportunity
- deny entry/visa
- deny the truth
- flatly/strongly deny

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 否认指控
- 否认指称
- 否认控罪
- 否认责任
- 否认做过某事
- 否认……
- 拒绝某人进入
- 剥夺某人的权利
- 剥夺某人的机会
- 拒绝入境/签证
- 否认事实
- 断然否认
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deny to the press
- deny under oath
- deny in court
- deny on the grounds of
- deny by the authorities
- deny for security reasons

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向媒体否认
- 在宣誓下否认
- 在法庭上否认
- 以……为由拒绝
- 被当局拒绝
- 基于安全原因拒绝
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The accused man denied all the charges brought against him. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: She denied having any involvement in the scandal. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The company denies responsibility for the environmental damage caused by its factory. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The researchers deny that their findings support the controversial hypothesis. (Science 2021)
- Common: There is no denying that climate change is a serious global issue.
- 介词搭配: The politician denied the allegations to the press in a televised interview.
- 介词搭配: The witness denied under oath that she had seen the defendant at the scene.
- 介词搭配: The defendant denied all charges in court.
- 介词搭配: The visa was denied on the grounds of insufficient financial resources.
- 介词搭配: The request for an extension was denied by the committee.
- 介词搭配: Access to the confidential documents was denied for security reasons.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 研究人员否认他们的发现支持了有争议的假设。
- 不可否认，气候变化是一个严重的全球性问题。

- 证人宣誓否认她在现场看到过被告。
- 被告在法庭上否认所有指控。
- 因经济来源不足为由拒绝了签证。
- 延期请求被委员会拒绝了。
- 出于安全原因拒绝访问机密文件。
</details>

### 47. depart <a id="47-depart"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *departir* ("to divide, separate, depart"), from Latin *dispertīre* ("to divide, distribute"), from *dis-* ("apart") + *partīre* ("to share, part").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *departir*（"分开，分离，离开"），源自拉丁语 *dispertīre*（"分开，分配"），源自 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *partīre*（"分享，分开"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to leave a place, especially to start a journey; to deviate from a usual or accepted way of doing something
- CN: 离开，出发；背离，违反

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal equivalent of "leave" — 比 "leave" 更正式
- "Depart from" = to leave a place OR to deviate from a standard — "Depart from" = 离开某地或偏离标准
- "Depart for" = to leave for a destination — "Depart for" = 出发前往某地
- The noun "departure" is more common in everyday use — 名词 "departure" 在日常使用中更常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- depart from
- depart for
- depart at (time)
- depart on (journey)
- depart the country
- depart the premises
- depart from tradition
- depart from the norm
- depart from the subject

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……离开
- 出发前往
- 在（时间）出发
- 启程（旅行）
- 离境
- 离开场所
- 背离传统
- 偏离常规
- 偏离主题
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- depart from the airport
- depart for London
- depart on a journey
- depart at noon
- depart by plane
- depart without notice
- depart in haste

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从机场出发
- 出发前往伦敦
- 启程旅行
- 中午出发
- 乘飞机出发
- 不告而别
- 匆匆离开
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The train departs from platform 3 at 9:15 AM. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The new policy departs significantly from the traditional approach. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The flight departs for Tokyo at 6:30 PM from Gate 15. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study's methodology departs from previous research in several important ways. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Please arrive early as the bus departs at 8 AM sharp.
- 介词搭配: The delegation departed from the capital on a diplomatic mission.
- 介词搭配: She departed for the United States to pursue her graduate studies.
- 介词搭配: The expedition departed on a journey to explore the Arctic region.
- 介词搭配: The ceremony departed at the scheduled time without any delay.
- 介词搭配: The team departed by helicopter for the remote research station.
- 介词搭配: He departed without notice, leaving no forwarding address.
- 介词搭配: The ambassador departed in haste when the political crisis erupted.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 火车于上午9:15从3号站台出发。
- 新政策与传统做法有很大不同。
- 飞往东京的航班于下午6:30从15号登机口起飞。
- 该研究的方法论在几个重要方面与先前的研究不同。
- 请提前到达，巴士在早上8点准时出发。
- 代表团从首都出发，执行外交任务。
- 她出发前往美国攻读研究生。
- 考察队启程去探索北极地区。
- 仪式按预定时间开始，没有任何延迟。
- 团队乘直升机前往偏远的研究站。
- 他不告而别，没有留下转寄地址。
- 政治危机爆发时，大使匆忙离开。
</details>

### 48. depend <a id="48-depend"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *dependre* ("to hang from, depend"), from Latin *dēpendēre* ("to hang down, be dependent on"), from *dē-* ("down") + *pendēre* ("to hang, be suspended").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *dependre*（"悬挂，依赖"），源自拉丁语 *dēpendēre*（"悬挂，依靠"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *pendēre*（"悬挂"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be determined, affected, or controlled by something; to rely on someone or something for support
- CN: 取决于，视……而定；依赖，依靠

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Always used with "on" or "upon" — 总是与 "on" 或 "upon" 连用
- "It depends" is a common standalone expression — "It depends" 是常用的独立表达
- "Depend on/upon sb to do sth" — 依靠某人做某事
- "Depend on/upon sb for sth" — 在某事上依赖某人

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- depend on/upon
- depend heavily on
- depend entirely on
- depend largely on
- depend on sb for sth
- depend on sb to do
- depend on the outcome of
- depend on the weather
- depend on circumstances
- it (all) depends

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 取决于
- 严重依赖
- 完全取决于
- 很大程度上取决于
- 依靠某人提供某物
- 依靠某人做某事
- 取决于……的结果
- 取决于天气
- 视情况而定
- 看情况
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- depend on factors
- depend upon the context
- depend for survival on
- depend to a large extent on
- depend in part on
- depend critically on

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 取决于因素
- 取决于语境
- 依靠……生存
- 在很大程度上取决于
- 部分取决于
- 关键取决于
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Whether we go camping depends on the weather forecast. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The success of the project depends largely on effective teamwork. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The survival of many species depends on the health of their natural habitats. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The rate of chemical reaction depends critically on the temperature and pressure conditions. (Science 2021)
- Common: "Can I borrow your car?" — "It depends. When do you need it?"
- 介词搭配: The outcome of the trial depends on several key factors, including the credibility of witnesses.
- 介词搭配: The meaning of this word depends upon the context in which it is used.
- 介词搭配: Many marine organisms depend for survival on the health of coral reefs.
- 介词搭配: The success of the treatment depends to a large extent on early diagnosis.
- 介词搭配: The cost will depend in part on the materials you choose.
- 介词搭配: Whether the species can adapt depends critically on the speed of climate change.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我们是否去露营取决于天气预报。
- 项目的成功在很大程度上取决于有效的团队合作。
- 许多物种的生存取决于其自然栖息地的健康状况。
- 化学反应的速度关键取决于温度和压力条件。
- "我能借你的车吗？"——"看情况。你什么时候需要？"
- 审判的结果取决于几个关键因素，包括证人的可信度。
- 这个词的含义取决于它所使用的语境。
- 许多海洋生物依靠珊瑚礁的健康而生存。
- 治疗的成功在很大程度上取决于早期诊断。
- 费用将部分取决于你选择的材料。
- 该物种能否适应关键取决于气候变化的速度。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-48-depend)

### 49. depict <a id="49-depict"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēpictus*, past participle of *dēpingere* ("to paint, depict, portray"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *pingere* ("to paint, draw").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēpictus*，*dēpingere*（"描绘，描述"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *pingere*（"绘画"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to represent or show something in a picture, story, or description; to describe something in words
- CN: 描绘，描画；描述

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used in visual arts (paintings, films) as well as written descriptions — 用于视觉艺术（绘画、电影）和书面描述
- "Depict sb/sth as" = to show in a particular way — "Depict sb/sth as" = 把某人/某物描绘成
- Similar to "portray" but slightly more neutral — 与 "portray" 类似，但稍微更中性
- Common in literary, art, and media analysis — 常用于文学、艺术和媒体分析

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- depict a scene
- depict the life of
- depict accurately
- depict graphically
- depict as
- depict in detail
- depict the relationship between
- depict the struggle of
- depict the reality of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 描绘场景
- 描绘……的生活
- 准确描绘
- 生动描绘
- 描绘成
- 详细描绘
- 描绘……之间的关系
- 描绘……的斗争
- 描绘……的现实
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- depict in a painting
- depict as a hero
- depict from memory
- depict with great accuracy
- depict through symbolism
- depict on canvas

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在画中描绘
- 描绘成英雄
- 凭记忆描绘
- 非常准确地描绘
- 通过象征手法描绘
- 在画布上描绘
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The painting depicts a beautiful landscape of the countryside. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The novel vividly depicts the hardships of life during the war. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary depicts the daily lives of penguins in Antarctica. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The model depicts the complex interactions between climate and ocean currents. (Science 2022)
- Common: The artist depicts ordinary people in extraordinary situations.
- 介词搭配: The historical event was depicted in a series of oil paintings.
- 介词搭配: In the film, the main character is depicted as a courageous leader.
- 介词搭配: The witness depicted the accident scene from memory for the police sketch artist.
- 介词搭配: The artist depicted the city skyline with great accuracy and attention to detail.
- 介词搭配: The theme of hope is depicted through the use of light and color.
- 介词搭配: The scene was depicted on a large canvas that dominated the gallery wall.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这幅画描绘了乡村美丽的风景。
- 这部小说生动地描绘了战争时期生活的艰难。
- 这部纪录片描绘了南极企鹅的日常生活。
- 该模型描绘了气候与洋流之间的复杂互动。
- 这位艺术家描绘了非同寻常情境中的普通人。
- 这一历史事件被描绘在一系列油画中。
- 在影片中，主角被描绘成一位勇敢的领导者。
- 证人凭记忆为警方的素描师描绘了事故现场。
- 艺术家非常准确地描绘了城市天际线，注重细节。
- 通过运用光线和色彩来表现希望的主题。
- 整个场景被画在一块大画布上，占据了画廊的整面墙。
</details>

### 50. deplete <a id="50-deplete"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēplētus*, past participle of *dēplēre* ("to empty out, deplete"), from *dē-* ("out, away") + *plēre* ("to fill").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēplētus*，*dēplēre*（"倒空，耗尽"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"出去，离开"）+ *plēre*（"装满"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to reduce the amount or supply of something, especially over time; to use up resources
- CN: 耗尽，用尽；使枯竭

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with natural resources, energy, and supplies — 常与自然资源、能源和供应品连用
- "Depleted" = reduced in amount — "Depleted" = 耗尽的，枯竭的
- "Ozone-depleting substances" = chemicals that destroy the ozone layer — "Ozone-depleting substances" = 消耗臭氧层的物质
- Implies gradual reduction rather than sudden exhaustion — 暗示逐渐减少而非突然耗尽

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deplete resources
- deplete energy
- deplete the supply of
- deplete the ozone layer
- deplete savings
- deplete the soil of nutrients
- deplete the stock
- deplete the body of
- severely deplete
- rapidly deplete

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 耗尽资源
- 耗尽精力
- 耗尽……的供应
- 消耗臭氧层
- 耗尽积蓄
- 耗尽土壤养分
- 耗尽库存
- 使身体缺乏
- 严重耗尽
- 迅速耗尽
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deplete of resources
- deplete by overuse
- deplete through overfishing
- deplete to dangerously low levels
- deplete from the body
- deplete as a result of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 耗尽资源
- 因过度使用而耗尽
- 因过度捕捞而枯竭
- 减少到危险的低水平
- 从体内流失
- 由于……而耗尽
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The prolonged drought has severely depleted the region's water reserves. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: Overfishing has depleted the fish stocks in many parts of the ocean. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The body's energy reserves are depleted during prolonged physical exercise. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The study examines how industrial agriculture depletes the soil of essential nutrients. (Nature 2021)
- Common: After climbing the mountain, our energy was completely depleted.
- 介词搭配: The region's forests have been depleted of valuable timber by decades of logging.
- 介词搭配: The groundwater supply was depleted by excessive agricultural irrigation.
- 介词搭配: Fish populations have been depleted through decades of commercial overfishing.
- 介词搭配: The battery charge depleted to dangerously low levels during the long flight.
- 介词搭配: Stress can deplete the body of essential vitamins and minerals.
- 介词搭配: The country's foreign currency reserves were depleted as a result of the economic crisis.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 长期干旱已严重耗尽了该地区的水储备。
- 过度捕捞使海洋许多地区的鱼类资源枯竭。
- 长时间体育锻炼会耗尽身体的能量储备。
- 该研究考察了工业化农业如何耗尽了土壤中的必需养分。
- 爬上那座山之后，我们的精力完全耗尽了。
- 该地区的森林因几十年的伐木而失去了珍贵的木材。
- 地下水供应因农业过度灌溉而枯竭。
- 由于几十年的商业性过度捕捞，鱼类种群已枯竭。
- 在长途飞行中，电池电量降到了危险的低水平。
- 压力会耗尽体内的必需维生素和矿物质。
- 由于经济危机，该国的外汇储备枯竭了。
</details>

### 51. deplore <a id="51-deplore"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēplōrāre* ("to weep bitterly, lament, deplore"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *plōrāre* ("to cry out, wail, lament").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēplōrāre*（"痛哭，悲叹，谴责"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *plōrāre*（"哭喊，哀号"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to feel or express strong disapproval of something; to regret deeply
- CN: 强烈反对，谴责；深感遗憾

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal word, expressing strong moral condemnation — 正式词汇，表达强烈的道德谴责
- Stronger than "disapprove of" or "regret" — 比 "disapprove of" 或 "regret" 语气更强
- Often used in formal statements, editorials, and speeches — 常用于正式声明、社论和演讲
- Not used in casual conversation — 不用于日常对话

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deplore the fact that
- deplore violence
- deplore the treatment of
- deplore the loss of
- deplore the decision
- deplore the situation
- deeply deplore
- publicly deplore

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……事实感到遗憾
- 谴责暴力
- 对……的对待方式感到愤慨
- 对……的损失感到痛心
- 反对/谴责决定
- 对局势深感忧虑
- 深感遗憾
- 公开谴责
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deplore by all parties
- deplore in the strongest terms
- deplore on humanitarian grounds
- deplore as unacceptable

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 受到各方谴责
- 以最强烈的措辞谴责
- 基于人道主义理由谴责
- 谴责为不可接受
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The United Nations deplored the use of chemical weapons in the conflict. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Many educators deplore the decline in reading standards among young people. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Environmental groups deplore the destruction of rainforests for commercial gain. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The scientific community deplores the manipulation of data to support predetermined conclusions. (Nature 2021)
- Common: We deplore any form of discrimination in the workplace.
- 介词搭配: The attack was deplored by all political parties in the country.
- 介词搭配: The government deplored the act of vandalism in the strongest terms.
- 介词搭配: The treatment of prisoners of war was deplored on humanitarian grounds.
- 介词搭配: The proposal was deplored as unacceptable by environmental activists.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 联合国谴责在冲突中使用化学武器。
- 许多教育工作者对年轻人阅读水平下降感到痛心。
- 环保组织谴责为了商业利益而破坏雨林。
- 科学界谴责为了支持预设结论而操纵数据的行为。
- 我们谴责工作场所任何形式的歧视。
- 这次袭击受到该国所有政党的谴责。
- 政府以最强烈的措辞谴责了故意破坏公物的行为。
- 对战俘的待遇基于人道主义理由受到谴责。
- 该提议被环保活动家谴责为不可接受。
</details>

### 52. deploy <a id="52-deploy"></a>

**Etymology:** From French *déployer* ("to unfold, deploy"), from Latin *displicāre* ("to unfold, scatter"), from *dis-* ("apart") + *plicāre* ("to fold").

**词源：** 源自法语 *déployer*（"展开，部署"），源自拉丁语 *displicāre*（"展开，散开"），源自 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *plicāre*（"折叠"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to arrange soldiers, equipment, or resources in a position ready for action; to use something effectively
- CN: 部署，调度；有效运用

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Originally military, now widely used in business and technology — 原为军事用语，现广泛用于商业和技术领域
- "Deploy resources/talent" = to use effectively — "Deploy resources/talent" = 有效运用资源/人才
- In software: "deploy an application" = to make it available — 在软件中："deploy an application" = 部署应用
- More formal/strategic than "use" — 比 "use" 更具正式/战略性

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deploy troops
- deploy forces
- deploy resources
- deploy technology
- deploy a strategy
- deploy a system
- deploy software
- deploy personnel
- deploy assets
- deploy weapons

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 部署部队
- 部署兵力
- 调配资源
- 应用技术
- 实施策略
- 部署系统
- 部署软件
- 调配人员
- 配置资产
- 部署武器
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deploy to the region
- deploy along the border
- deploy in response to
- deploy across the network
- deploy as part of
- deploy under the command of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 部署到该地区
- 沿边境部署
- 为应对……而部署
- 在整个网络中部署
- 作为……的一部分部署
- 在……的指挥下部署
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government deployed additional police officers to maintain order during the protest. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The company plans to deploy new software to improve efficiency across all departments. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The military deployed thousands of troops to assist with disaster relief efforts. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The researchers deployed advanced sensors to monitor seismic activity in the region. (Science 2022)
- Common: We need to deploy our best people to solve this crisis.
- 介词搭配: Peacekeeping forces were deployed to the conflict zone under a UN mandate.
- 介词搭配: Surveillance cameras were deployed along the border to prevent illegal crossings.
- 介词搭配: Emergency services were deployed in response to the natural disaster.
- 介词搭配: The new security system will be deployed across the entire corporate network.
- 介词搭配: Drones were deployed as part of the search and rescue operation.
- 介词搭配: The naval fleet was deployed under the command of an experienced admiral.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府增派了警力以在抗议期间维持秩序。
- 该公司计划部署新软件以提高所有部门的效率。
- 军方部署了数千名士兵协助救灾工作。
- 研究人员部署了先进的传感器来监测该地区的地震活动。
- 我们需要调动最优秀的人才来解决这场危机。
- 维和部队根据联合国授权部署到冲突地区。
- 沿边境部署了监控摄像头以防止非法越境。
- 为应对自然灾害，紧急服务部门被部署到位。
- 新的安全系统将在整个企业网络中部署。
- 无人机被部署作为搜救行动的一部分。
- 海军舰队由一位经验丰富的海军上将指挥部署。
</details>

### 53. deport <a id="53-deport"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēportāre* ("to carry away, banish"), from *dē-* ("away") + *portāre* ("to carry").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēportāre*（"带走，放逐"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *portāre*（"携带"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to force a person who is not a citizen to leave a country, especially for legal reasons
- CN: 驱逐出境，遣返

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Legal/immigration context — 法律/移民语境
- "Deport from" = remove from a country — "Deport from" = 从某国驱逐
- "Deport to" = send to a specific country — "Deport to" = 遣送到某国
- Related noun: "deportation" — 相关名词："deportation"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deport an immigrant
- deport a foreign national
- deport a criminal
- deport from a country
- deport to one's home country
- deport by force
- face deportation
- deport illegal immigrants

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 驱逐移民
- 驱逐外国公民
- 驱逐罪犯
- 从某国驱逐
- 遣送回原籍国
- 强制驱逐
- 面临驱逐出境
- 驱逐非法移民
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deport from the country
- deport to the country of origin
- deport under immigration law
- deport on security grounds
- deport for overstaying a visa

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 驱逐出境
- 遣送回原籍国
- 根据移民法驱逐
- 基于安全原因驱逐
- 因签证逾期而驱逐
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government deported the foreign national for violating immigration laws. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Illegal immigrants who commit serious crimes may be deported. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The convicted spy was deported from the country after serving his prison sentence. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study examines the social and economic consequences of mass deportations. (Migration Studies 2021)
- Common: He was deported to his home country after his visa expired.
- 介词搭配: The undocumented worker was deported from the United States to Mexico.
- 介词搭配: The terrorist suspect was deported to his country of origin for prosecution.
- 介词搭配: The individual was deported under the provisions of the Immigration Act.
- 介词搭配: Several foreign nationals were deported on national security grounds.
- 介词搭配: He was deported for overstaying his student visa by more than two years.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府因该外国公民违反移民法而将其驱逐出境。
- 犯有严重罪行的非法移民可能会被驱逐出境。
- 这名被判有罪的间谍在服刑后被驱逐出境。
- 该研究考察了大规模驱逐出境的社会和经济后果。
- 他的签证到期后被遣返回了原籍国。
- 这名无证工人被从美国驱逐回墨西哥。
- 恐怖主义嫌疑人被遣送回原籍国接受起诉。
- 根据《移民法》的条款，该人被驱逐出境。
- 几名外国公民基于国家安全原因被驱逐出境。
- 他因学生签证逾期超过两年而被驱逐出境。
</details>

### 54. deposit <a id="54-deposit"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēpositus*, past participle of *dēpōnere* ("to put down, lay aside"), from *dē-* ("down") + *pōnere* ("to put, place").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēpositus*，*dēpōnere*（"放下，存放"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *pōnere*（"放置"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to put money into a bank account; to put something valuable in a safe place; to leave a layer of a substance on a surface
- CN: 存入（银行）；存放；使沉积

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Financial: deposit money/check/funds — 金融：存钱/存支票/存资金
- "Deposit A in/into B" = put money into an account — "Deposit A in/into B" = 将A存入B
- "Deposit A with B" = leave something with someone for safekeeping — "Deposit A with B" = 将A交由B保管
- Also means a natural accumulation: sediment deposits — 也指自然沉积：沉积物

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deposit money
- deposit a check
- deposit funds
- deposit cash
- deposit into an account
- deposit with a bank
- deposit valuables
- deposit a layer of
- deposit sediment
- deposit minerals

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 存钱
- 存入支票
- 存入资金
- 存入现金
- 存入账户
- 存放在银行
- 存放贵重物品
- 沉积一层
- 沉积泥沙
- 沉积矿物质
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deposit in a bank account
- deposit into savings
- deposit with a financial institution
- deposit for safekeeping
- deposit on a surface
- deposit over time
- deposit by the river

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 存入银行账户
- 存入储蓄账户
- 存放在金融机构
- 为安全保管而存放
- 沉积在表面
- 随时间沉积
- 被河流冲积
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I need to deposit this check into my savings account. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: You are required to deposit a security deposit before renting the apartment. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The river deposits rich sediment on the floodplain during the rainy season. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The researchers observed that minerals deposit on the surface of the rocks over thousands of years. (Geology 2021)
- Common: Please deposit all valuables in the hotel safe before going out.
- 介词搭配: The company deposited the funds in a trust account for the project.
- 介词搭配: She deposits a portion of her salary into a retirement savings account each month.
- 介词搭配: The original documents were deposited with the National Archives for preservation.
- 介词搭配: Items can be deposited with the front desk for safekeeping during your stay.
- 介词搭配: Fine dust deposited on every surface in the room after the construction work.
- 介词搭配: Layers of silt were deposited over time by the flooding of the Nile River.
- 介词搭配: Rich alluvial soil was deposited by the river along its banks.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我需要把这张支票存入我的储蓄账户。
- 租公寓之前需要交一笔押金。
- 雨季期间，河流在洪泛平原上沉积了丰富的泥沙。
- 研究人员观察到，矿物质在数千年间沉积在岩石表面。
- 外出前请将所有贵重物品存放在酒店保险箱中。
- 公司将资金存入该项目的信托账户。
- 她每月将一部分工资存入退休储蓄账户。
- 原始文件存放在国家档案馆进行保存。
- 入住期间可将物品存放在前台代为保管。
- 施工后，细尘沉积在房间的每一个表面上。
- 随着时间推移，尼罗河泛滥沉积了层层淤泥。
- 河流沿岸沉积了肥沃的冲积土。
</details>

### 55. depreciate <a id="55-depreciate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Late Latin *dēpretiāre* ("to lower the price of, undervalue"), from Latin *dē-* ("down") + *pretium* ("price, value").

**词源：** 源自后期拉丁语 *dēpretiāre*（"降低价格，低估"），源自拉丁语 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *pretium*（"价格，价值"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to decrease in value over time, especially of assets or currency; to express disapproval of or to belittle
- CN: 贬值，跌价；贬低，轻视

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily financial: assets, currency, equipment depreciate — 主要用于财务：资产、货币、设备贬值
- "Depreciate in value" — 价值下降
- Opposite of "appreciate" (increase in value) — 与 "appreciate"（增值）相反
- Related noun: "depreciation" — 相关名词："depreciation"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- depreciate in value
- depreciate over time
- depreciate rapidly/quickly
- depreciate an asset
- currency depreciates
- depreciate against
- depreciate for tax purposes
- depreciate by a percentage
- depreciate the importance of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 贬值
- 随时间贬值
- 迅速贬值
- 计提资产折旧
- 货币贬值
- 对……贬值
- 为税务目的计算折旧
- 贬值一定百分比
- 贬低……的重要性
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- depreciate against the dollar
- depreciate by 10%
- depreciate over a period of
- depreciate in real terms
- depreciate for accounting purposes
- depreciate from the original cost

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对美元贬值
- 贬值10%
- 在……期间折旧
- 按实际价值贬值
- 为会计目的折旧
- 从原始成本折旧
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The value of the local currency has depreciated significantly against the US dollar. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: New cars depreciate rapidly in the first few years of ownership. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The equipment can be depreciated over a period of five years for tax purposes. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study analyzes how political instability causes a nation's currency to depreciate. (Journal of Economics 2021)
- Common: Never depreciate the value of hard work and dedication.
- 介词搭配: The Japanese yen depreciated against the euro by 3% this quarter.
- 介词搭配: The machinery depreciates by approximately 15% each year.
- 介词搭配: The asset will be depreciated over a useful life of ten years.
- 介词搭配: Although the nominal value remained the same, the asset depreciated in real terms due to inflation.
- 介词搭配: The company depreciates its vehicles for accounting purposes using the straight-line method.
- 介词搭配: The property's value depreciated from its original purchase price after the market crash.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 当地货币对美元大幅贬值。
- 新车在购买后的头几年贬值很快。
- 该设备可在五年内为税务目的计提折旧。
- 该研究分析了政治不稳定如何导致国家货币贬值。
- 永远不要贬低努力工作和奉献精神的价值。
- 日元本季度对欧元贬值了3%。
- 这台机器每年大约贬值15%。
- 该资产将在十年使用寿命内折旧。
- 尽管名义价值不变，但由于通货膨胀，该资产的实际价值下降了。
- 公司采用直线法为会计目的对其车辆计提折旧。
- 市场崩盘后，该房产的价值从原始购买价下跌了。
</details>

### 56. depress <a id="56-depress"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *depresser* ("to press down, lower"), from Latin *depressus*, past participle of *deprimere* ("to press down"), from *dē-* ("down") + *primere* ("to press").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *depresser*（"压下，降低"），源自拉丁语 *depressus*，*deprimere*（"压下"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *primere*（"按压"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone feel very sad and without hope; to reduce the value or amount of something
- CN: 使沮丧，使消沉；降低，抑制

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Emotional meaning is most common: sadden — 情感含义最常见：使悲伤
- Economic meaning: depress prices/wages/the economy — 经济含义：压低价格/工资/抑制经济
- Physical meaning (rare): depress a button/pedal — 字面含义（较少见）：按下按钮/踏板
- Related adjective: "depressed" (feeling sad OR economically weak) — 相关形容词："depressed"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- depress sb
- feel depressed
- get depressed about/over
- depress the economy
- depress prices
- depress wages
- depress demand
- depress the market
- depress a key/button
- depress the clutch

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人沮丧
- 感到沮丧
- 对……感到沮丧
- 抑制经济
- 压低价格
- 压低工资
- 抑制需求
- 使市场低迷
- 按下按键/按钮
- 踩离合器
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- depress about the future
- depress by bad news
- depress through overproduction
- depress to a low level
- depress during recession

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对未来感到沮丧
- 因坏消息而沮丧
- 因生产过剩而压低
- 压低到较低水平
- 在经济衰退期间抑制
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The gloomy weather tends to depress many people during the winter months. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The oversupply of oil depressed prices on the global market. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Economic factors such as inflation can depress consumer spending. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The study found that prolonged unemployment significantly depresses mental health. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: It depresses me to see so much waste in our society.
- 介词搭配: He was depressed about his poor performance on the final exam.
- 介词搭配: The whole community was depressed by the news of the factory closure.
- 介词搭配: Farm prices were depressed through overproduction and falling demand.
- 介词搭配: The economy was depressed to levels not seen since the Great Depression.
- 介词搭配: Consumer confidence is often depressed during periods of economic uncertainty.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 阴沉的天气在冬季往往使很多人的情绪低落。
- 石油供应过剩压低了全球市场的价格。
- 通货膨胀等经济因素会抑制消费者支出。
- 研究发现长期失业会显著损害心理健康。
- 看到社会上有这么多浪费让我感到沮丧。
- 他对期末考试成绩不佳感到沮丧。
- 整个社区因工厂关闭的消息而沮丧。
- 由于生产过剩和需求下降，农产品价格被压低。
- 经济被压低到了自大萧条以来从未见过的水平。
- 在经济不确定时期，消费者信心往往受到抑制。
</details>

### 57. deprive <a id="57-deprive"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *depriver* ("to deprive"), from Latin *dēprīvāre* ("to deprive, take away"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *prīvāre* ("to separate, deprive"), from *prīvus* ("individual, private").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *depriver*（"剥夺"），源自拉丁语 *dēprīvāre*（"剥夺，夺去"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *prīvāre*（"分离，剥夺"），源自 *prīvus*（"个人的，私有的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to prevent someone from having something, especially something that they need or want
- CN: 剥夺，使丧失

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deprive sb of sth" — always use "of" — "Deprive sb of sth" — 总是与 "of" 连用
- "Deprived" (adj.) = lacking basic necessities — "Deprived"（形容词）= 贫困的，缺乏基本生活条件的
- Stronger than "take away" — emphasizes the injustice/hardship — 比 "take away" 语气更强，强调不公/困苦
- Common in legal, social, and psychological contexts — 常用于法律、社会和心理学语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deprive sb of sth
- deprive of freedom
- deprive of rights
- deprive of opportunity
- deprive of sleep
- deprive of education
- deprive of food/water
- deprive of oxygen
- deprive of liberty
- deprive of basic needs

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 剥夺某人的某物
- 剥夺自由
- 剥夺权利
- 剥夺机会
- 剥夺睡眠
- 剥夺受教育权
- 剥夺食物/水
- 使缺氧
- 剥夺自由
- 剥夺基本需求
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deprive of the right to vote
- deprive by law
- deprive through discrimination
- deprive under the pretext of
- deprive on the basis of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 剥夺选举权
- 通过法律剥夺
- 通过歧视剥夺
- 以……为借口剥夺
- 基于……剥夺
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The prisoners were deprived of basic human rights. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Children from poor families should not be deprived of educational opportunities. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Sleep deprivation can significantly impair cognitive function and decision-making ability. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The study found that plants deprived of sunlight showed significantly reduced growth rates. (Botany 2021)
- Common: Working overtime every day deprives me of time with my family.
- 介词搭配: The citizens were deprived of the right to vote under the authoritarian regime.
- 介词搭配: The indigenous people were deprived of their ancestral lands by law.
- 介词搭配: Many minority groups have been deprived of economic opportunities through systemic discrimination.
- 介词搭配: The company deprived workers of their benefits under the pretext of financial difficulties.
- 介词搭配: No one should be deprived of medical care on the basis of their economic status.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 囚犯被剥夺了基本人权。
- 不应剥夺贫困家庭孩子的受教育机会。
- 睡眠不足会显著损害认知功能和决策能力。
- 研究发现，缺乏光照的植物生长速度明显减慢。
- 每天加班使我失去了与家人相处的时间。
- 在专制政权下，公民被剥夺了选举权。
- 原住民根据法律被剥夺了祖先的土地。
- 许多少数群体因系统性歧视而被剥夺了经济机会。
- 该公司以财务困难为借口剥夺了工人的福利。
- 任何人都不应因其经济状况而被剥夺医疗权利。
</details>

### 58. derail <a id="58-derail"></a>

**Etymology:** From French *dérailler* ("to derail"), from *dé-* ("off, from") + *rail* ("rail"), from English "rail."

**词源：** 源自法语 *dérailler*（"使脱轨"），源自 *dé-*（"离开"）+ *rail*（"铁轨"），源自英语 "rail"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause a train to leave the tracks; to obstruct or prevent a process from continuing as planned
- CN: 使脱轨；阻挠，破坏（计划、进程等）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: a train derails — 字面义：火车脱轨
- Figurative: derail plans/negotiations/career — 比喻义：破坏计划/谈判/事业
- Common in news and business contexts — 常用于新闻和商业语境
- Related noun: "derailment" — 相关名词："derailment"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- derail a train
- derail plans
- derail negotiations
- derail a project
- derail a career
- derail peace talks
- derail the peace process
- derail economic reform
- nearly derail
- threaten to derail

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使火车脱轨
- 破坏计划
- 破坏谈判
- 破坏项目
- 毁掉职业生涯
- 破坏和平谈判
- 破坏和平进程
- 破坏经济改革
- 险些破坏
- 威胁要破坏
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- derail from the tracks
- derail by a technical problem
- derail due to funding issues
- derail in the final stages
- derail over a dispute

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从轨道上脱轨
- 因技术问题而脱轨
- 因资金问题而中断
- 在最后阶段中断
- 因争端而中断
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The train derailed near the station, but no one was seriously injured. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The sudden resignation of the CEO threatened to derail the merger negotiations. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The peace process was derailed by renewed fighting between the two factions. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The clinical trial was nearly derailed when a manufacturing defect was discovered. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Don't let a small setback derail your long-term goals.
- 介词搭配: The train derailed from the main line due to a signal failure.
- 介词搭配: The project was derailed by unexpected technical problems and budget overruns.
- 介词搭配: The peace talks derailed due to disagreements over territorial boundaries.
- 介词搭配: The promising collaboration derailed in the final stages over a dispute about intellectual property.
- 介词搭配: The negotiations derailed over the issue of compensation for displaced workers.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 火车在车站附近脱轨，但没有人受重伤。
- 首席执行官突然辞职，威胁到合并谈判的进行。
- 和平进程因两派之间重新爆发战斗而中断。
- 当发现制造缺陷时，临床试验险些中断。
- 不要让小小的挫折影响你的长远目标。
- 由于信号故障，火车从正线脱轨。
- 项目因意外的技术问题和预算超支而中断。
- 由于在领土边界问题上的分歧，和平谈判破裂了。
- 这个有前景的合作在最后阶段因知识产权争端而中断。
- 谈判因对失业工人赔偿问题的分歧而破裂。
</details>
