### 59. derive <a id="59-derive"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deriver* ("to flow from, derive"), from Latin *dērīvāre* ("to lead off, divert, derive"), from *dē-* ("away, off") + *rīvus* ("stream, brook").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deriver*（"源自，派生"），源自拉丁语 *dērīvāre*（"引出，分流，派生"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *rīvus*（"溪流"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to get something from something else; to come from a particular source or origin
- CN: 获得，得到；源于，源自

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Derive from" = to come from (most common) — "Derive from" = 源自（最常见用法）
- "Derive sth from" = to obtain from — "Derive sth from" = 从……获得某物
- Very common in academic writing — 在学术写作中非常常见
- Used for words, ideas, substances, benefits — 用于词语、思想、物质、利益等

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- derive from
- derive benefit from
- derive pleasure from
- derive satisfaction from
- derive income from
- derive knowledge from
- derive one's name from
- derive meaning from
- derive energy from
- derive its origin from

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 源自
- 从……中获益
- 从……中获得乐趣
- 从……中获得满足
- 从……中获得收入
- 从……中获得知识
- 名称源自
- 从……中获得意义
- 从……中获得能量
- 起源于
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- derive from ancient Latin
- derive by calculation
- derive through experimentation
- derive as a byproduct
- derive from the fact that

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 源自古拉丁语
- 通过计算推导
- 通过实验获得
- 作为副产品获得
- 源于……的事实
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many English words are derived from Latin and Greek roots. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The company derives most of its revenue from overseas markets. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researchers derived valuable data from the satellite images. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The theory derives its name from the Greek philosopher who first proposed it. (Science 2021)
- Common: She derives great pleasure from reading classic novels.
- 介词搭配: The word "etymology" derives from ancient Greek roots meaning "true sense of a word."
- 介词搭配: The results were derived by statistical analysis of the experimental data.
- 介词搭配: The enzyme was derived through a process of extraction and purification.
- 介词搭配: Glycerin is derived as a byproduct of soap manufacturing.
- 介词搭配: The conclusion derives from the fact that the experimental results were reproducible.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多英语词汇源自古拉丁语和希腊语词根。
- 该公司的大部分收入来自海外市场。
- 研究人员从卫星图像中获得了宝贵的数据。
- 该理论以其首次提出该理论的希腊哲学家的名字命名。
- 她从阅读经典小说中获得极大乐趣。
- "Etymology"（词源学）一词源自古希腊语词根，意为"词的真实含义"。
- 通过对实验数据的统计分析得出了这些结果。
- 这种酶是通过提取和纯化过程获得的。
- 甘油作为肥皂制造的副产品而获得。
- 这一结论源自实验结果可以被重复验证这一事实。
</details>

### 60. descend <a id="60-descend"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *descendre* ("to go down, descend"), from Latin *dēscendere* ("to climb down, descend"), from *dē-* ("down") + *scandere* ("to climb").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *descendre*（"下去，下降"），源自拉丁语 *dēscendere*（"爬下，下降"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *scandere*（"爬升"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move down from a higher position to a lower one; to be passed down through generations; to suddenly attack or visit
- CN: 下来，下降；遗传；突然袭击/访问

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Descend from" = to be a descendant of — "Descend from" = 是……的后代
- "Descend on/upon" = to visit unexpectedly or attack — "Descend on/upon" = 突然造访或袭击
- "Descend into" = to gradually get into a worse state — "Descend into" = 逐渐陷入更糟的状态
- Opposite of "ascend" — 与 "ascend" 相对

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- descend from
- descend into
- descend on/upon
- descend the stairs
- descend to a lower level
- descend rapidly/gradually
- descend into chaos
- descend into poverty
- be descended from
- descend the mountain

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……下来/是……的后代
- 陷入
- 突然降临/袭击
- 下楼梯
- 下降到较低水平
- 迅速/逐渐下降
- 陷入混乱
- 陷入贫困
- 是……的后代
- 下山
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- descend from the summit
- descend into the valley
- descend upon the village
- descend by rope
- descend to the bottom
- descend in an elevator
- descend through the clouds

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从山顶下来
- 下降到山谷
- 突然降临村庄
- 用绳子下降
- 下降到最底部
- 乘电梯下楼
- 穿过云层下降
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The path descends steeply to the beach below. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The country descended into civil war after the collapse of the government. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The helicopter descended through thick fog to land on the platform. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The species is believed to have descended from a common ancestor that lived millions of years ago. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Thousands of tourists descend on the small town every summer.
- 介词搭配: The climbers descended from the peak after reaching the summit at dawn.
- 介词搭配: The plane descended into thick cloud cover as it approached the airport.
- 介词搭配: The locusts descended upon the farmland, destroying all the crops.
- 介词搭配: The rescuers descended by rope from the helicopter to reach the stranded hiker.
- 介词搭配: The submarine descended to a depth of over 3,000 meters.
- 介词搭配: We descended in a glass elevator that offered spectacular views of the city.
- 介词搭配: The aircraft descended through layers of clouds before breaking into clear skies.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这条小路陡峭地通向下面的海滩。
- 政府垮台后，该国陷入了内战。
- 直升机穿过浓雾降落在平台上。
- 该物种被认为是由数百万年前的一个共同祖先进化而来。
- 每年夏天，成千上万的游客突然涌入这个小镇。
- 登山者在黎明登上峰顶后从山顶下来。
- 飞机在接近机场时进入厚厚的云层。
- 蝗虫突然降临农田，毁坏了所有的庄稼。
- 救援人员从直升机上用绳索降到被困徒步者身边。
- 潜艇下潜到了3,000多米的深度。
- 我们乘坐观光电梯下楼，可以欣赏到城市的壮丽景色。
- 飞机穿过层层云层后进入晴朗的天空。
</details>

### 61. describe <a id="61-describe"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēscrībere* ("to write down, copy, describe"), from *dē-* ("down") + *scrībere* ("to write").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēscrībere*（"写下，抄写，描述"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *scrībere*（"写"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to say what someone or something is like by giving details; to produce an image or shape
- CN: 描述，形容；画出……的图形

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- One of the most common verbs in English — 英语中最常见的动词之一
- "Describe sb/sth as" = to say that someone or something has particular qualities — "Describe sb/sth as" = 把某人/某物描述为
- "Describe how/what/when" + clause — 后接 wh-从句
- More detailed than "tell about" — 比 "tell about" 更详细

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- describe sb/sth
- describe as
- describe in detail
- describe accurately
- describe vividly
- describe the situation
- describe the process
- describe the symptoms
- describe the event
- describe the scene
- describe the feeling

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 描述某人/某物
- 描述为
- 详细描述
- 准确描述
- 生动描述
- 描述情况
- 描述过程
- 描述症状
- 描述事件
- 描述场景
- 描述感受
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- describe to the police
- describe in detail
- describe with precision
- describe by reference to
- describe in one's own words
- describe to an audience

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向警方描述
- 详细描述
- 精确描述
- 参照……来描述
- 用自己的话描述
- 向观众描述
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The witness described the suspect as tall with dark hair. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The researcher described the experimental procedure in great detail. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The article describes the challenges faced by immigrants in adapting to a new culture. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The paper describes a novel method for detecting early-stage cancer. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Words cannot describe how grateful I am.
- 介词搭配: She described the accident scene to the police in vivid detail.
- 介词搭配: The scientist described the chemical reaction with great precision.
- 介词搭配: The witness described the vehicle by reference to its distinctive color and license plate.
- 介词搭配: Can you describe what happened in your own words?
- 介词搭配: The explorer described his adventures to an audience of fascinated listeners.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 目击者描述嫌疑人个子高，黑头发。
- 研究人员非常详细地描述了实验步骤。
- 文章描述了移民在适应新文化过程中面临的挑战。
- 这篇论文描述了一种检测早期癌症的新方法。
- 言语无法形容我有多感激。
- 她向警方生动详细地描述了事故现场。
- 科学家非常精确地描述了化学反应。
- 目击者根据其独特的颜色和车牌描述了那辆车。
- 你能用自己的话描述一下发生了什么吗？
- 探险者向一群听得入迷的听众描述了他的冒险经历。
</details>

### 62. desert <a id="62-desert"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deserter* ("to desert, abandon"), from Late Latin *dēsertāre* ("to desert, abandon"), from Latin *dēserere* ("to abandon, forsake"), from *dē-* ("un-") + *serere* ("to join, bind together").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deserter*（"抛弃，遗弃"），源自后期拉丁语 *dēsertāre*（"抛弃"），源自拉丁语 *dēserere*（"抛弃，离弃"），源自 *dē-*（"取消"）+ *serere*（"连接，绑在一起"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to leave someone or something without help or support; to leave military service without permission
- CN: 抛弃，遗弃；擅离职守，开小差

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Not to be confused with "dessert" (sweet food) or "desert" (dry land, pronounced differently) — 不要与 "dessert"（甜点）或 "desert"（沙漠）混淆
- "Desert sb/sth for" = leave for something else — "Desert sb/sth for" = 为了……而抛弃
- "Desert one's post" = leave without permission — "Desert one's post" = 擅离职守
- Strong emotional/moral condemnation implied — 暗示强烈的感情/道德谴责

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- desert sb
- desert one's family
- desert one's post
- desert the army
- desert a cause
- desert a friend
- desert a ship
- desert in time of need
- desert under fire
- feel deserted

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 抛弃某人
- 抛弃家人
- 擅离职守
- 从军队开小差
- 背弃事业
- 背弃朋友
- 弃船
- 在困难时抛弃
- 在炮火下逃离
- 感到被遗弃
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- desert from the army
- desert to the enemy
- desert in the face of danger
- desert for a better opportunity
- desert under the cover of night

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从军队开小差
- 投敌
- 在危险面前逃离
- 为了更好的机会而抛弃
- 趁夜色逃离
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The soldier was court-martialed for deserting his post during the battle. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: She felt that her courage deserted her at the most critical moment. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The old man was deserted by his children and lived alone. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The study explores the psychological effects on children who feel deserted by their parents. (Child Psychology 2021)
- Common: Many of his friends deserted him when he was in trouble.
- 介词搭配: Several soldiers deserted from the regiment during the long and brutal campaign.
- 介词搭配: The spy deserted to the enemy side, taking classified documents with him.
- 介词搭配: He deserted his comrades in the face of extreme danger.
- 介词搭配: She deserted her job in the city for a quieter life in the countryside.
- 介词搭配: The guard deserted his post under the cover of night.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这名士兵因为在战斗中擅离职守而被军事法庭审判。
- 她觉得自己在最关键时刻失去了勇气。
- 那位老人被子女遗弃，独自生活。
- 该研究探讨了被父母遗弃的孩子的心理影响。
- 他有麻烦时，许多朋友都背弃了他。
- 在漫长而残酷的战役中，有几名士兵从团里开小差跑了。
- 间谍带着机密文件投向了敌方。
- 他在极度危险面前抛弃了战友。
- 她放弃了城市里的工作，到乡下过更安静的生活。
- 守卫趁夜色擅离职守。
</details>

### 63. deserve <a id="63-deserve"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deservir* ("to deserve, merit"), from Latin *dēservīre* ("to serve well, be devoted to"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *servīre* ("to serve").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deservir*（"应得，值得"），源自拉丁语 *dēservīre*（"好好服务，尽心尽力"），源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *servīre*（"服务"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have earned something by doing something good or bad; to be worthy of something
- CN: 应得，值得

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used for both good and bad outcomes — 可用于好的和坏的结果
- "Deserve to do" — 应得做某事
- "Get what you deserve" = to receive appropriate reward/punishment — "Get what you deserve" = 得到应得的赏罚
- Not used in continuous/progressive tenses (not "deserving") — 不用于进行时态

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deserve attention
- deserve respect
- deserve praise
- deserve credit
- deserve recognition
- deserve punishment
- deserve a chance
- deserve better
- deserve to win
- deserve to know
- deserve a break

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 值得关注
- 值得尊重
- 值得表扬
- 值得称赞
- 值得认可
- 应受惩罚
- 值得给一个机会
- 应得到更好的
- 理应获胜
- 有权知道
- 值得休息一下
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deserve for one's efforts
- deserve by hard work
- deserve in return for
- deserve on the basis of
- deserve from the community

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因努力而应得
- 通过努力工作赢得
- 作为……的回报而应得
- 基于……而应得
- 从社会得到的应有的待遇
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Everyone deserves a fair chance to succeed in life. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The issue deserves serious consideration from policymakers. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The students' hard work deserves recognition and praise. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: This groundbreaking research deserves the attention of the global scientific community. (Nature 2022)
- Common: You deserve a promotion after all your hard work.
- 介词搭配: She deserves a reward for her years of dedicated service to the company.
- 介词搭配: He earned the promotion by deserving it through years of consistent performance.
- 介词搭配: The team got the victory they deserved in return for months of intensive training.
- 介词搭配: Everyone deserves to be treated with dignity on the basis of their humanity alone.
- 介词搭配: The charity deserves greater support from the local community.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 每个人都应得到一个在生活中取得成功的公平机会。
- 这个问题值得政策制定者认真考虑。
- 学生的努力值得认可和表扬。
- 这项突破性研究值得全球科学界的关注。
- 你这么努力工作，应该得到晋升。
- 她多年来为公司尽心服务，应该得到奖励。
- 他通过多年稳定的表现赢得了晋升，这是他应得的。
- 团队赢得了胜利，这是他们几个月集训应得的回报。
- 每个人都应仅基于其人性而得到有尊严的对待。
- 这家慈善机构应该得到当地社区更多的支持。
</details>

### 64. design <a id="64-design"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēsignāre* ("to mark out, designate, design"), from *dē-* ("out") + *signāre* ("to mark, mark out"), from *signum* ("mark, sign").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēsignāre*（"标出，指定，设计"），源自 *dē-*（"出来"）+ *signāre*（"标记"），源自 *signum*（"标记，符号"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to plan or create something with a specific purpose or intention; to draw or plan the appearance or function of something
- CN: 设计；计划，构思

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Very broad usage: from fashion to engineering — 使用范围极广：从时尚到工程
- "Design A for B" = intend A for purpose B — "Design A for B" = 为B而设计A
- "Design to do" = intended to achieve something — "Design to do" = 旨在做某事
- As a noun: pattern, intention, or plan — 作名词：图案、意图或计划
- "By design" = intentionally — "By design" = 故意地，有意地

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- design a product
- design a building
- design a system
- design a plan
- design a website
- design clothes
- design a strategy
- design a program
- design an experiment
- design a test
- design a policy

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 设计产品
- 设计建筑
- 设计系统
- 设计方案
- 设计网站
- 设计服装
- 制定策略
- 设计程序
- 设计实验
- 设计测试
- 制定政策
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- design for a specific purpose
- design by an architect
- design with the user in mind
- design around a constraint
- design as a prototype
- design in collaboration with

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为特定目的设计
- 由建筑师设计
- 以用户为中心设计
- 围绕限制条件设计
- 作为原型设计
- 与……合作设计
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The architect designed a building that blends modern and traditional styles. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The course is designed to help students develop critical thinking skills. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The experiment was designed to test the effect of light on plant growth. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The researchers designed a novel algorithm for processing large datasets. (Nature 2021)
- Common: This chair is designed for maximum comfort.
- 介词搭配: The new smartphone was designed for users who prioritize battery life over screen quality.
- 介词搭配: The bridge was designed by a renowned structural engineer.
- 介词搭配: The software was designed with the needs of non-technical users in mind.
- 介词搭配: Urban planners need to design around environmental constraints such as flood zones.
- 介词搭配: The device was designed as a prototype for mass production.
- 介词搭配: The curriculum was designed in collaboration with industry experts.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 建筑师设计了一座融合现代和传统风格的建筑。
- 这门课程旨在帮助学生培养批判性思维能力。
- 实验旨在测试光照对植物生长的影响。
- 研究人员设计了一种处理大数据集的新型算法。
- 这把椅子是为了最大舒适度而设计的。
- 这款新智能手机是为优先考虑电池续航而非屏幕质量的用户设计的。
- 这座桥由一位著名的结构工程师设计。
- 该软件的设计考虑到了非技术用户的需求。
- 城市规划者需要围绕洪水区等环境限制来进行设计。
- 该设备作为原型设计用于批量生产。
- 课程是与行业专家合作设计的。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-64-design)

### 65. designate <a id="65-designate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēsignātus*, past participle of *dēsignāre* ("to mark out, nominate, designate"), from *dē-* ("out") + *signāre* ("to mark").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēsignātus*，*dēsignāre*（"标出，提名，指定"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"出来"）+ *signāre*（"标记"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to officially choose someone or something for a particular purpose; to give something a particular name or title
- CN: 指定，选定；命名

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal/official context — 正式/官方语境
- "Designate A as B" = officially name or select — "Designate A as B" = 正式命名或选定A为B
- "Designate sb to do" = appoint — "Designate sb to do" = 指定某人做某事
- Often used in passive: "be designated as" — 常用被动语态："be designated as"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- designate an area
- designate a leader
- designate a representative
- designate a site for
- designate as
- designate by name
- designate by law
- designate for a purpose
- officially designate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 指定区域
- 指定领导
- 指定代表
- 指定地点用于
- 指定为
- 指名道姓地指定
- 由法律指定
- 为某目的指定
- 正式指定
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- designate as a national park
- designate by royal decree
- designate for government use
- designate under the act
- designate on the basis of merit

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 指定为国家公园
- 由皇家法令指定
- 指定用于政府用途
- 根据该法案指定
- 基于功绩指定
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The area has been designated as a national park to protect its unique wildlife. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The president designated his successor before leaving office. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The building was designated as a historical landmark in 1980. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The region was designated a UNESCO World Heritage Site due to its cultural significance. (Nature 2021)
- Common: The teacher designated one student to collect the homework.
- 介词搭配: The wetland was designated as a protected area under the new environmental law.
- 介词搭配: The official was designated by the prime minister to lead the delegation.
- 介词搭配: A portion of the budget has been designated for educational reform.
- 介词搭配: The scholarship is designated for students from low-income families.
- 介词搭配: The land was designated for agricultural use under the zoning regulations.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该地区被指定为国家公园以保护其独特的野生动物。
- 总统在离职前指定了继任者。
- 这座建筑于1980年被指定为历史地标。
- 该地区因其文化意义被指定为联合国教科文组织世界遗产地。
- 老师指定一名学生负责收作业。
- 根据新的环境法，该湿地被指定为保护区。
- 该官员由首相指定率领代表团。
- 一部分预算已被指定用于教育改革。
- 该奖学金是为低收入家庭的学生设立的。
- 根据分区规划，这块土地被指定用于农业用途。
</details>

### 66. desire <a id="66-desire"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *desirer* ("to desire, wish"), from Latin *dēsīderāre* ("to long for, wish for, desire"), possibly from *dē-* ("from") + *sīdus* ("star, heavenly body") — "to await what the stars will bring."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *desirer*（"渴望，希望"），源自拉丁语 *dēsīderāre*（"渴望，期望，想要"），可能源自 *dē-*（"从"）+ *sīdus*（"星星，天体"）——"等待星星带来的东西"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to want something strongly, especially something that is difficult to get; to wish for something
- CN: 渴望，期望，想要

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "want" — expresses intense longing — 比 "want" 语气更强，表达强烈的渴望
- Often used in formal contexts and literature — 常用于正式语境和文学作品中
- "Desire to do sth" — 渴望做某事
- "Leave much to be desired" = to be unsatisfactory — "Leave much to be desired" = 还有很多不足之处

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- desire to do
- desire sth
- deeply desire
- strongly desire
- desire success
- desire change
- desire freedom
- desire the approval of
- greatly desired
- eagerly desired

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 渴望做
- 渴望某物
- 深深渴望
- 强烈渴望
- 渴望成功
- 渴望改变
- 渴望自由
- 渴望得到……的认可
- 非常渴望的
- 热切渴望的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- desire for peace
- desire by all
- desire in one's heart
- desire above all
- desire for its own sake

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对和平的渴望
- 人人渴望
- 内心渴望
- 高于一切的渴望
- 为渴望而渴望
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many young people desire a career that combines passion with financial stability. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The reform is desired by the majority of citizens who seek greater transparency. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Most organisms desire an environment that provides adequate resources for survival. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study explores why humans desire social connection and how it affects well-being. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Her performance left nothing to be desired.
- 介词搭配: The people's desire for freedom and democracy cannot be suppressed indefinitely.
- 介词搭配: A peaceful resolution is desired by all parties involved in the conflict.
- 介词搭配: She desired in her heart to become a doctor and help others.
- 介词搭配: What he desired above all was recognition from his peers.
- 介词搭配: Knowledge should be pursued for its own sake, not merely for practical gain.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多年轻人渴望一份既能结合激情又能提供经济稳定性的职业。
- 大多数寻求更大透明度的公民希望进行改革。
- 大多数生物体渴望一个能提供充足生存资源的环境。
- 该研究探讨了为什么人类渴望社会联系以及它如何影响幸福感。
- 她的表演无可挑剔。
- 人民对自由和民主的渴望无法无限期地压制。
- 冲突各方都希望和平解决。
- 她内心渴望成为一名医生并帮助他人。
- 他最渴望的是得到同行的认可。
- 知识应为其本身而追求，而不仅仅是为了实际利益。
</details>

### 67. despair <a id="67-despair"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *desperer* ("to despair"), from Latin *dēspērāre* ("to despair, be hopeless"), from *dē-* ("without") + *spēs* ("hope").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *desperer*（"绝望"），源自拉丁语 *dēspērāre*（"绝望，没有希望"），源自 *dē-*（"没有"）+ *spēs*（"希望"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to lose all hope; to feel that a situation is so bad that nothing can improve it
- CN: 绝望，失去希望

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Despair of doing sth" = to lose hope of achieving — "Despair of doing sth" = 对做某事不抱希望
- "Despair at/over sth" = to feel hopeless about — "Despair at/over sth" = 对某事感到绝望
- "Drive sb to despair" = to make someone lose hope — "Drive sb to despair" = 使某人绝望
- More common as a noun: "in despair" — 作名词时更常见："in despair"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- despair of
- despair at/over
- despair of ever doing
- despair of finding a solution
- drive sb to despair
- give up in despair
- cry in despair
- sink into despair

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……绝望
- 对……感到绝望
- 对做成某事不抱希望
- 对找到解决办法不抱希望
- 使某人绝望
- 绝望地放弃
- 绝望地哭泣
- 陷入绝望
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- despair of the future
- despair at the state of affairs
- despair over the loss of
- despair in the face of adversity
- despair despite efforts

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对未来绝望
- 对事态感到绝望
- 对失去……感到绝望
- 面对逆境绝望
- 尽管努力仍然绝望
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The doctors despaired of saving the patient's life after the severe accident. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Never despair of finding a solution — every problem has an answer. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The farmers despaired at the sight of their crops destroyed by the storm. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The researchers despaired over the repeated failure of the experiments. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Don't despair — things will get better eventually.
- 介词搭配: Many young people despair of the future due to economic uncertainty and climate change.
- 介词搭配: The community despaired at the state of affairs after the factory closed.
- 介词搭配: She despaired over the loss of her precious collection of rare books.
- 介词搭配: He never despaired in the face of adversity, always believing that hard work would pay off.
- 介词搭配: The team despaired despite their best efforts to save the project.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 严重事故后，医生们对挽救病人的生命不抱希望了。
- 永远不要对找到解决办法绝望——每个问题都有答案。
- 看到庄稼被暴风雨摧毁，农民们感到绝望。
- 研究人员因实验一再失败而感到绝望。
- 不要绝望——事情最终会好起来的。
- 由于经济不确定性和气候变化，许多年轻人对未来不抱希望。
- 工厂关闭后，社区对现状感到绝望。
- 她因失去珍贵的藏书而感到绝望。
- 面对逆境他从未绝望，始终相信努力会有回报。
- 尽管尽了最大努力拯救项目，团队还是感到绝望。
</details>

### 68. despise <a id="68-despise"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *despire* ("to despise, scorn"), from Latin *dēspicere* ("to look down on, despise"), from *dē-* ("down") + *specere* ("to look at").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *despire*（"鄙视，蔑视"），源自拉丁语 *dēspicere*（"俯视，鄙视"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *specere*（"看"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to strongly dislike and have no respect for someone or something; to consider something worthless or inferior
- CN: 鄙视，蔑视，厌恶

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Very strong negative emotion — 非常强烈的负面情绪
- Stronger than "hate" in some contexts; implies contempt — 在某些语境中比 "hate" 更强；暗示蔑视
- Not used in casual contexts; strong language — 不用于日常语境；语气强烈
- "Despise oneself for" = to hate oneself for something — "Despise oneself for" = 因某事而厌恶自己

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- despise sb/sth
- despise the idea of
- despise violence
- despise hypocrisy
- despise corruption
- despise injustice
- despise oneself for
- thoroughly despise
- openly despise
- secretly despise

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 鄙视某人/某物
- 鄙视……的想法
- 鄙视暴力
- 鄙视虚伪
- 鄙视腐败
- 鄙视不公
- 因……而厌恶自己
- 彻底鄙视
- 公开鄙视
- 暗中鄙视
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- despise for one's cowardice
- despise on moral grounds
- despise by society
- despise for betraying
- despise as weak

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因懦弱而鄙视
- 基于道德理由鄙视
- 被社会鄙视
- 因背叛而鄙视
- 鄙视……为软弱
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She despised the way he treated his subordinates with disrespect. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The public despises corruption in government and demands accountability. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ancient philosopher despised ignorance and spent his life pursuing knowledge. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study found that people tend to despise those who violate fundamental moral principles. (Science 2021)
- Common: I despise being lied to — honesty is very important to me.
- 介词搭配: He was despised for his cowardice during the crisis by his colleagues.
- 介词搭配: The practice of child labor is despised on moral grounds by human rights organizations.
- 介词搭配: Traitors have always been despised by society throughout history.
- 介词搭配: She despised herself for betraying her friend's trust.
- 介词搭配: The regime was despised as corrupt and oppressive by its own people.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她鄙视他那种不尊重地对待下属的方式。
- 公众鄙视政府腐败并要求问责。
- 这位古代哲学家鄙视无知，一生追求知识。
- 研究发现，人们往往会鄙视那些违反基本道德原则的人。
- 我讨厌被骗——诚实对我来说非常重要。
- 他因危机期间的懦弱行为而受到同事们的鄙视。
- 人权组织基于道德理由鄙视使用童工的做法。
- 历史上，叛徒一直受到社会的鄙视。
- 她因背叛了朋友的信任而厌恶自己。
- 该政权被其人民鄙视为腐败和压迫。
</details>

### 69. destroy <a id="69-destroy"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *destruire* ("to destroy"), from Latin *dēstruere* ("to tear down, demolish"), from *dē-* ("down, un-") + *struere* ("to build, pile").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *destruire*（"破坏，毁灭"），源自拉丁语 *dēstruere*（"拆毁，破坏"），源自 *dē-*（"向下，取消"）+ *struere*（"建造，堆积"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to damage something so severely that it no longer exists or cannot be repaired; to ruin something completely
- CN: 破坏，毁灭，摧毁

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "damage" — implies complete ruin — 比 "damage" 语气更强，暗示完全毁灭
- Both physical (destroy a building) and abstract (destroy a relationship) — 既有字面义（摧毁建筑）也有比喻义（破坏关系）
- "Destroy oneself" = to ruin one's own life — "Destroy oneself" = 毁掉自己
- Related noun: "destruction" (毁灭) — 相关名词："destruction"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- destroy a building
- destroy evidence
- destroy a relationship
- destroy a reputation
- destroy the environment
- destroy confidence
- destroy hope
- destroy a plan
- completely destroy
- partially destroy

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 摧毁建筑物
- 销毁证据
- 破坏关系
- 毁掉声誉
- 破坏环境
- 摧毁信心
- 毁灭希望
- 破坏计划
- 完全摧毁
- 部分破坏
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- destroy by fire
- destroy in a war
- destroy through neglect
- destroy with explosives
- destroy beyond repair
- destroy for good

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被火烧毁
- 在战争中被毁
- 因疏忽而毁坏
- 用炸药摧毁
- 毁坏到无法修复
- 永远摧毁
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The earthquake destroyed thousands of homes in the region. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The scandal completely destroyed his political career. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Invasive species can destroy the ecological balance of an area. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The virus destroys the host cell's ability to reproduce normally. (Cell Biology 2021)
- Common: The fire destroyed all the documents in the office.
- 介词搭配: Several historic buildings were destroyed by fire during the riot.
- 介词搭配: The city was destroyed in the war and had to be completely rebuilt.
- 介词搭配: The old house was destroyed through years of neglect and exposure to the elements.
- 介词搭配: The enemy fortifications were destroyed with precision airstrikes.
- 介词搭配: The ancient artifact was damaged beyond repair by the careless handling.
- 介词搭配: The pest population was destroyed for good by the introduction of natural predators.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 地震摧毁了该地区数千所房屋。
- 丑闻彻底毁掉了他的政治生涯。
- 入侵物种可以破坏一个地区的生态平衡。
- 该病毒破坏了宿主细胞正常繁殖的能力。
- 大火烧毁了办公室里所有的文件。
- 骚乱期间，几座历史建筑被大火烧毁。
- 这座城市在战争中被毁，不得不完全重建。
- 这座老房子因多年疏忽和风吹日晒而毁坏。
- 敌人的防御工事被精确空袭摧毁。
- 这件古代文物因搬运不慎而损坏到无法修复。
- 通过引入天敌，害虫种群被永久消灭了。
</details>

### 70. detach <a id="70-detach"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *destachier* ("to detach, separate"), from *des-* ("apart") + *attachier* ("to attach"), from *à* ("to") + *tache* ("nail, fastening"), of Germanic origin.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *destachier*（"分开，分离"），源自 *des-*（"分开"）+ *attachier*（"连接"），源自 *à*（"到"）+ *tache*（"钉子，紧固"），源自日耳曼语。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to separate something from something larger; to become separated; to become emotionally uninvolved
- CN: 拆卸，分离；使超脱，使不卷入

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Detach A from B" = to separate A from B — "Detach A from B" = 将A从B上拆下
- Opposite of "attach" — 与 "attach" 相反
- "Detached" (adj.) = separate OR emotionally uninvolved — "Detached"（形容词）= 分离的/超然的
- Common in physical, technical, and psychological contexts — 常用于物理、技术和心理学语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- detach from
- detach a part
- detach oneself from
- detach the form
- detach the document
- detach from reality
- remain detached
- become detached
- emotionally detached

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……分离
- 拆卸零件
- 使自己脱离
- 撕下表/附件
- 分离文件
- 脱离现实
- 保持超然
- 变得疏离
- 情感上疏离的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- detach from the main body
- detach by cutting
- detach with a tool
- detach from the group
- detach under pressure

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从主体上分离
- 通过切割分离
- 用工具拆下
- 与群体分离
- 在压力下分离
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Please detach the bottom portion of the form and mail it back. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: It is important for doctors to remain emotionally detached from their patients. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The spacecraft's landing module detached from the main orbiter as planned. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The researchers observed how the cell membrane detaches from the cell wall during plasmolysis. (Biology 2021)
- Common: He found it hard to detach himself from the memories of his childhood home.
- 介词搭配: The trailer was detached from the truck before the vehicle entered the parking lot.
- 介词搭配: The damaged section of the pipeline was detached by cutting it with a saw.
- 介词搭配: The component can be detached with a simple screwdriver for cleaning.
- 介词搭配: He gradually detached himself from the social group after the disagreement.
- 介词搭配: The spacecraft's capsule detached under pressure as it re-entered the atmosphere.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 请撕下表格的下半部分并寄回。
- 医生与患者在情感上保持超然态度是很重要的。
- 航天器的登月舱按计划从主轨道飞行器分离。
- 研究人员观察到在质壁分离过程中细胞膜如何与细胞壁分离。
- 他发现自己很难从童年故乡的记忆中抽离出来。
- 拖车在车辆进入停车场之前与卡车分离。
- 管道的受损部分用锯子切割下来。
- 这个部件可以用一把简单的螺丝刀拆下进行清洁。
- 那次分歧之后，他逐渐脱离了那个社交群体。
- 航天器的返回舱在重新进入大气层时在压力下分离。
</details>

### 71. detain <a id="71-detain"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *detenir* ("to detain, hold back"), from Latin *dētinēre* ("to hold off, detain"), from *dē-* ("away, from") + *tenēre* ("to hold").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *detenir*（"扣留，阻止"），源自拉丁语 *dētinēre*（"阻止，扣留"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *tenēre*（"持有"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to keep someone in an official position, especially in prison or a police station; to delay someone
- CN: 拘留，扣留；耽搁，延误

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Legal context: detain a suspect — 法律语境：拘留嫌疑人
- "Detain for questioning" = hold for investigation — "Detain for questioning" = 扣留审问
- More formal than "hold" or "keep" — 比 "hold" 或 "keep" 更正式
- Related nouns: "detention", "detainee" — 相关名词："detention", "detainee"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- detain a suspect
- detain for questioning
- detain without trial
- detain by police
- detain illegally
- detain indefinitely
- detain for hours/days
- detain pending investigation
- detain at the border

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拘留嫌疑人
- 扣留审问
- 未经审判拘留
- 被警方扣留
- 非法拘留
- 无限期拘留
- 扣留数小时/数天
- 在调查期间扣留
- 在边境扣留
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- detain for further questioning
- detain under the Prevention of Terrorism Act
- detain by the authorities
- detain on suspicion of
- detain at the airport
- detain without charge

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 扣留进一步审问
- 根据《防止恐怖主义法》拘留
- 被当局扣留
- 因涉嫌……而拘留
- 在机场扣留
- 未经指控拘留
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The police detained two suspects for questioning about the robbery. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The journalist was detained by the authorities for reporting on government corruption. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Passengers were detained at the airport for several hours due to a security alert. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study examines the psychological effects of prolonged detention on asylum seekers. (Psychological Science 2021)
- Common: I was detained at work by an unexpected meeting.
- 介词搭配: The witness was detained for further questioning about what he had seen.
- 介词搭配: The suspect was detained under the Prevention of Terrorism Act for questioning.
- 介词搭配: Three individuals were detained by the police on suspicion of drug trafficking.
- 介词搭配: He was detained at the airport for carrying undeclared goods.
- 介词搭配: The protesters were detained without charge for several days before being released.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 警方拘留了两名嫌疑人以调查抢劫案。
- 这名记者因报道政府腐败而被当局扣留。
- 由于安全警报，乘客们在机场被滞留了数小时。
- 该研究考察了长期拘留对寻求庇护者的心理影响。
- 我因一个意外的会议而被耽搁在工作上。
- 证人被扣留以进一步询问他所见的情况。
- 嫌疑人根据《防止恐怖主义法》被拘留审问。
- 三名嫌疑人因涉嫌贩毒被警方拘留。
- 他因携带未申报物品在机场被扣留。
- 抗议者在未经指控的情况下被拘留数日后获释。
</details>

### 72. detect <a id="72-detect"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dētectus*, past participle of *dētegere* ("to uncover, detect"), from *dē-* ("off, away") + *tegere* ("to cover").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dētectus*，*dētegere*（"揭开，发现"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *tegere*（"覆盖"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to discover or notice something that is not easy to see, hear, or notice; to find evidence of something
- CN: 发现，察觉；检测出

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used in science, technology, and everyday observation — 用于科学、技术和日常观察
- "Detect a change/difference" — 察觉变化/差异
- "Detect sb doing sth" — 发现某人做某事
- Related noun: "detection" (检测，发现) — 相关名词："detection"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- detect a problem
- detect a change
- detect a signal
- detect a leak
- detect a disease
- detect early
- detect quickly
- detect easily
- detect the presence of
- detect something unusual

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 发现问题
- 察觉变化
- 检测信号
- 检测泄漏
- 检测疾病
- 早期检测
- 迅速检测
- 容易检测
- 检测到……的存在
- 察觉异常
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- detect in the early stages
- detect by radar
- detect through testing
- detect with the naked eye
- detect from a distance
- detect at low levels

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在早期阶段检测
- 通过雷达检测
- 通过测试检测
- 用肉眼发现
- 从远处发现
- 在低水平检测
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The device can detect the presence of harmful gases in the air. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The study detected a significant correlation between exercise and mental health. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Scientists have developed a new method to detect early signs of Alzheimer's disease. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The telescope can detect light from galaxies billions of light-years away. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I detected a hint of sadness in her voice.
- 介词搭配: Cancer is more treatable when detected in the early stages.
- 介词搭配: The aircraft was detected by radar long before it entered our airspace.
- 介词搭配: The virus was detected through routine blood testing of the patient.
- 介词搭配: The tiny cracks were barely detectable with the naked eye.
- 介词搭配: The sensor can detect movement from a distance of up to 50 meters.
- 介词搭配: The chemical can be detected at very low levels in the water supply.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该设备可以检测空气中有害气体的存在。
- 该研究发现锻炼与心理健康之间存在显著相关性。
- 科学家开发出一种检测阿尔茨海默病早期迹象的新方法。
- 这台望远镜可以探测到数十亿光年外星系发出的光。
- 我觉察到她声音中有一丝悲伤。
- 如果在早期阶段发现，癌症更容易治疗。
- 那架飞机在进入我国领空前很早就被雷达发现。
- 通过对患者的常规血液检测发现了该病毒。
- 这些细微的裂缝用肉眼几乎看不到。
- 该传感器可以在50米外检测到移动。
- 这种化学物质可以在供水系统中以非常低的水平检测到。
</details>

### 73. deter <a id="73-deter"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēterrēre* ("to frighten away, deter"), from *dē-* ("away") + *terrēre* ("to frighten, terrify").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēterrēre*（"吓住，威慑"），源自 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *terrēre*（"恐吓，吓唬"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to discourage someone from doing something by making them realize it will be difficult or have unpleasant results
- CN: 威慑，阻止；使打消念头

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deter sb from doing sth" — 阻止某人做某事
- Usually used with "from" + gerund — 通常与 "from" + 动名词连用
- Implies the use of fear, difficulty, or consequences as a disincentive — 暗示使用恐惧、困难或后果作为抑制因素
- Related nouns: "deterrence", "deterrent" — 相关名词："deterrence", "deterrent"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deter crime
- deter theft
- deter potential attackers
- deter sb from doing
- deter investment
- deter entry
- deter competition
- effectively deter
- act as a deterrent
- military deterrence

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 威慑犯罪
- 防止盗窃
- 威慑潜在攻击者
- 阻止某人做某事
- 阻止投资
- 阻止进入
- 阻止竞争
- 有效威慑
- 起到威慑作用
- 军事威慑
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deter from committing a crime
- deter by the threat of
- deter through strict enforcement
- deter with harsh penalties
- deter under penalty of law

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 阻止犯罪
- 以……的威胁来威慑
- 通过严格执法来阻止
- 以严厉惩罚来威慑
- 在法律惩罚下阻止
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The high price of tickets deterred many people from attending the concert. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The new security measures are designed to deter potential cyber attacks. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The presence of security cameras can deter theft in retail stores. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The risk of severe side effects may deter patients from undergoing the treatment. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Don't let failure deter you from pursuing your dreams.
- 介词搭配: Neither the threat of punishment nor public criticism deterred him from committing the crime.
- 介词搭配: The government hopes that the new regulations will deter companies from polluting the environment.
- 介词搭配: The high cost of legal fees deters many people from seeking justice through the courts.
- 介词搭配: Potential investors were deterred by the political instability in the region.
- 介词搭配: The country's nuclear capability acts as a deterrent under its defense policy.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 高昂的票价使许多人望而却步，没有去听音乐会。
- 新的安全措施旨在阻止潜在的网络攻击。
- 监控摄像头的存在可以防止零售店的盗窃行为。
- 严重副作用的风险可能会阻止患者接受治疗。
- 不要让失败阻止你追求梦想。
- 惩罚的威胁和公开的批评都没能阻止他犯罪。
- 政府希望新规定能阻止公司污染环境。
- 高昂的法律费用阻止了许多人通过法庭寻求正义。
- 该地区的政治不稳定阻止了潜在投资者。
- 根据其国防政策，该国的核能力起到了威慑作用。
</details>

### 74. deteriorate <a id="74-deteriorate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Late Latin *deteriōrātus*, past participle of *deteriōrāre* ("to make worse"), from Latin *deterior* ("worse, inferior"), from *dē-* ("down") + *-ter* (comparative suffix).

**词源：** 源自后期拉丁语 *deteriōrātus*，*deteriōrāre*（"使恶化"）的过去分词，源自拉丁语 *deterior*（"更差的，较低的"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *-ter*（比较级后缀）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become gradually worse in quality or condition; to decline in strength or value
- CN: 恶化，退化；变坏

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Gradual process, not sudden — 渐进过程，非突然
- Used for health, relationships, conditions, quality — 用于健康、关系、状况、质量等
- "Deteriorate into" = to become worse and develop into something bad — "Deteriorate into" = 恶化为
- Stronger than "worsen" in formal contexts — 在正式语境中比 "worsen" 语气更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deteriorate rapidly/gradually
- deteriorate over time
- deteriorate into
- health deteriorates
- relation deteriorates
- condition deteriorates
- quality deteriorates
- deteriorate sharply/significantly
- deteriorate with age

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 迅速/逐渐恶化
- 随时间恶化
- 恶化为
- 健康恶化
- 关系恶化
- 状况恶化
- 质量下降
- 急剧/显著恶化
- 随年龄增长而恶化
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deteriorate into violence
- deteriorate due to neglect
- deteriorate from lack of maintenance
- deteriorate to the point of
- deteriorate under pressure

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 恶化为暴力
- 因疏忽而恶化
- 因缺乏维护而恶化
- 恶化到……程度
- 在压力下恶化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The patient's condition deteriorated rapidly overnight. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Relations between the two countries deteriorated after the trade dispute. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The quality of the air in the city has deteriorated significantly over the past decade. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The study warns that the condition of coral reefs will continue to deteriorate without urgent action. (Nature 2021)
- Common: The weather deteriorated quickly, so we decided to head back.
- 介词搭配: The peaceful protest deteriorated into violent clashes between the two groups.
- 介词搭配: The building's structure deteriorated due to decades of neglect and water damage.
- 介词搭配: The roads deteriorated from lack of proper maintenance by the local government.
- 介词搭配: His health deteriorated to the point where he could no longer work.
- 介词搭配: The relationship deteriorated under the constant stress of financial problems.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 患者的病情在一夜间迅速恶化。
- 贸易争端后两国关系恶化了。
- 该市的空气质量在过去十年中显著恶化。
- 研究警告说，如果不采取紧急行动，珊瑚礁的状况将继续恶化。
- 天气迅速恶化，于是我们决定返回。
- 和平抗议恶化为两个群体之间的暴力冲突。
- 由于几十年的疏忽和水损害，建筑结构恶化了。
- 由于当地政府缺乏适当的维护，道路恶化了。
- 他的健康恶化到了无法继续工作的程度。
- 在财务问题的持续压力下，这段关系恶化了。
</details>

### 75. determine <a id="75-determine"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *determiner* ("to determine, settle"), from Latin *dētermināre* ("to limit, determine, fix"), from *dē-* ("down") + *terminus* ("boundary, end").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *determiner*（"确定，决定"），源自拉丁语 *dētermināre*（"限定，确定，固定"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *terminus*（"边界，终点"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to decide or settle something finally; to discover the facts about something; to be the cause of or influence something directly
- CN: 决定，确定；查明；是……的决定因素

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "decide" — implies finality — 比 "decide" 语气更强，暗示最终性
- "Determine to do sth" = to make a firm decision — "Determine to do sth" = 下决心做某事
- "Determine whether/what/when/how" + clause — 后接 wh-从句
- Common in scientific, legal, and formal contexts — 常用于科学、法律和正式语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- determine the cause of
- determine the outcome of
- determine the extent of
- determine the value of
- determine the identity of
- determine whether
- determine how
- determine what
- be determined by
- determine sb's fate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 确定……的原因
- 决定……的结果
- 确定……的程度
- 确定……的价值
- 确定……的身份
- 确定是否
- 确定如何
- 确定什么
- 由……决定
- 决定某人的命运
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- determine by experiment
- determine through analysis
- determine on the basis of
- determine with accuracy
- determine from the evidence
- determine at a later date

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过实验确定
- 通过分析确定
- 基于……确定
- 精确确定
- 从证据中确定
- 稍后确定
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The investigation will determine the cause of the accident. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The success of the project is determined largely by the quality of the planning. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Scientists used carbon dating to determine the age of the fossil. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The study aims to determine whether the drug is effective in treating the disease. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Your attitude often determines your success in life.
- 介词搭配: The exact composition of the substance was determined by chemical analysis.
- 介词搭配: The outcome of the election will be determined through a fair counting of votes.
- 介词搭配: The value of the property was determined on the basis of a professional appraisal.
- 介词搭配: The age of the artifact was determined with remarkable accuracy using radiocarbon dating.
- 介词搭配: The truth was determined from the contradictory testimonies of the witnesses.
- 介词搭配: The final decision will be determined at a later date by the board of directors.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 调查将确定事故的原因。
- 项目的成功很大程度上取决于规划的质量。
- 科学家使用碳定年法来确定化石的年代。
- 该研究旨在确定该药物是否能有效治疗该疾病。
- 你的态度往往决定了你生活中的成功。
- 该物质的确切成分是通过化学分析确定的。
- 选举结果将通过公正计票来确定。
- 该房产的价值是根据专业评估确定的。
- 利用放射性碳定年法，该文物的年代被非常精确地确定。
- 真相是从证人相互矛盾的证词中确定的。
- 最终决定将由董事会在稍后日期做出。
</details>

### 76. devastate <a id="76-devastate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēvāstātus*, past participle of *dēvāstāre* ("to lay waste, devastate"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *vāstāre* ("to lay waste"), from *vāstus* ("empty, desolate").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēvāstātus*，*dēvāstāre*（"使荒废，毁灭"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *vāstāre*（"使荒废"），源自 *vāstus*（"空的，荒凉的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to destroy or damage something severely; to make someone extremely shocked or sad
- CN: 严重破坏，毁灭；使极度震惊或悲伤

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Very strong word — implies severe damage AND emotional impact — 语气非常强烈——暗示严重破坏和情感冲击
- Both literal (devastate a city) and figurative (devastate sb emotionally) — 既有字面义（毁灭一座城市）也有比喻义（使某人情绪崩溃）
- "Devastated" (adj.) = extremely upset — "Devastated"（形容词）= 极度悲伤的
- Related noun: "devastation" — 相关名词："devastation"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- devastate a city
- devastate a community
- devastate an ecosystem
- devastate crops
- devastate the economy
- devastate one's health
- devastate one's hopes
- be devastated by
- completely devastate
- utterly devastate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 毁灭城市
- 摧毁社区
- 破坏生态系统
- 毁坏庄稼
- 摧毁经济
- 损害健康
- 使希望破灭
- 被……摧毁
- 彻底破坏
- 完全摧毁
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- devastate by war
- devastate by natural disaster
- devastate by the news of
- devastate beyond recognition
- devastate in a matter of days

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被战争摧毁
- 被自然灾害摧毁
- 被……的消息击垮
- 破坏得面目全非
- 在数日内摧毁
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The tsunami devastated coastal communities, leaving thousands homeless. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The economic crisis devastated the country's manufacturing sector. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The invasive species devastated the native plant population in the region. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study reveals how deforestation devastates the habitat of endangered species. (Nature 2021)
- Common: She was devastated by the news of her father's sudden death.
- 介词搭配: The city was devastated by the earthquake, with entire neighborhoods reduced to rubble.
- 介词搭配: The farming community was devastated by the worst drought in a century.
- 介词搭配: The forest was devastated beyond recognition by the massive wildfire.
- 介词搭配: The entire village was devastated in a matter of hours by the volcanic eruption.
- 介词搭配: He was devastated by the news that his application had been rejected.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 海啸摧毁了沿海社区，使数千人无家可归。
- 经济危机摧毁了该国的制造业。
- 入侵物种破坏了该地区的原生植物种群。
- 该研究揭示了森林砍伐如何破坏濒危物种的栖息地。
- 父亲突然去世的消息使她悲痛欲绝。
- 这座城市被地震摧毁，整个社区化为瓦砾。
- 农业社区被百年一遇的严重干旱摧毁。
- 森林被大规模野火烧得面目全非。
- 整个村庄在数小时内被火山喷发摧毁。
- 得知申请被拒的消息，他悲痛欲绝。
</details>

### 77. develop <a id="77-develop"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *desveloper* ("to unwrap, unfold"), from *des-* ("un-") + *veloper* ("to wrap"), from Latin *volvere* ("to roll"), *velum* ("a wrap") + *operire* ("to cover").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *desveloper*（"展开，解开"），源自 *des-*（"取消"）+ *veloper*（"包裹"），源自拉丁语 *volvere*（"卷"），*velum*（"包裹物"）+ *operire*（"覆盖"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to grow or cause something to grow or change gradually; to create something new over a period of time; to design or make a new product
- CN: 发展，开发；培养；研制；形成

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Extremely versatile verb with many meanings — 含义极其丰富的动词
- Can be both transitive (develop a product) and intransitive (a situation develops) — 既可及物也可不及物
- Common in business (develop a market), science (develop a theory), personal growth (develop a skill) — 广泛用于商业、科学、个人成长等
- "Developed" (adj.) = advanced (developed country) — "Developed"（形容词）= 发达的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- develop a plan
- develop a product
- develop a skill
- develop a system
- develop a relationship
- develop technology
- develop the economy
- develop a habit
- develop a disease
- develop from/into

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 制定计划
- 开发产品
- 培养技能
- 开发系统
- 发展关系
- 发展技术
- 发展经济
- 养成习惯
- 患上疾病
- 从……发展/成长为
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- develop from an idea
- develop into a crisis
- develop over time
- develop with practice
- develop in stages
- develop through experience
- develop under favorable conditions

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从想法发展而来
- 发展成危机
- 随时间发展
- 通过实践培养
- 分阶段发展
- 通过经验培养
- 在有利条件下发展
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company has developed a new software application for managing finances. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: It is important to develop critical thinking skills during university education. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The embryo develops rapidly during the first few weeks of pregnancy. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The researchers developed a vaccine that proved effective against multiple strains. (Nature 2022)
- Common: She developed a strong interest in photography after traveling to Japan.
- 介词搭配: The project developed from a simple idea into a large-scale international collaboration.
- 介词搭配: The dispute developed into a full-blown crisis that threatened regional stability.
- 介词搭配: These skills develop over time with consistent practice and dedication.
- 介词搭配: The plant only develops properly under favorable conditions of sunlight and moisture.
- 介词搭配: The product was developed in stages, with each version improving on the previous one.
- 介词搭配: Leadership abilities are often developed through experience rather than formal training.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司开发了一款新的财务管理软件应用。
- 在大学教育期间培养批判性思维能力非常重要。
- 胚胎在怀孕的最初几周内发育迅速。
- 研究人员开发了一种被证明对多种毒株有效的疫苗。
- 去日本旅行后，她对摄影产生了浓厚的兴趣。
- 这个项目从一个简单的想法发展成为一个大规模的国际合作。
- 争端发展成了一场威胁地区稳定的全面危机。
- 这些技能随着时间推移通过持续的练习和投入而发展。
- 这种植物只有在阳光和水分的有利条件下才能正常生长。
- 该产品分阶段开发，每个版本都在先前版本的基础上改进。
- 领导能力往往通过经验而非正规培训来培养。
</details>

### 78. deviate <a id="78-deviate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Late Latin *dēviātus*, past participle of *dēviāre* ("to turn aside, deviate"), from Latin *dē-* ("off, away") + *via* ("way, road").

**词源：** 源自后期拉丁语 *dēviātus*，*dēviāre*（"偏离，背离"）的过去分词，源自拉丁语 *dē-*（"离开"）+ *via*（"道路"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move away from a planned path or accepted standard; to change from a usual or expected way of behaving
- CN: 偏离，背离；脱离

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Deviate from" = to diverge from a standard/path — "Deviate from" = 偏离标准/路径
- Used for physical paths, behavioral norms, and statistical averages — 用于物理路径、行为规范和统计平均值
- Related nouns: "deviation", "deviant" — 相关名词："deviation", "deviant"
- Often has a negative connotation in social contexts — 在社会语境中常带负面含义

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- deviate from
- deviate from the norm
- deviate from the standard
- deviate from a plan
- deviate from the truth
- deviate from one's principles
- deviate from the route
- deviate significantly
- deviate slightly

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 偏离
- 偏离常规
- 偏离标准
- 偏离计划
- 偏离事实
- 偏离原则
- 偏离路线
- 显著偏离
- 略微偏离
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- deviate from the original plan
- deviate from the accepted standard
- deviate by a small margin
- deviate under pressure
- deviate for personal gain

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 偏离原计划
- 偏离公认标准
- 偏离微小幅度
- 在压力下偏离
- 为个人利益而偏离
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The pilot was forced to deviate from the original flight path due to severe weather. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The author deviates from the main topic to discuss a related issue. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The results of the experiment deviated significantly from what the researchers had predicted. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study examines how adolescents deviate from parental expectations as they develop independence. (Child Development 2021)
- Common: He never deviates from his daily routine, no matter what.
- 介词搭配: The aircraft deviated from its intended course by several miles before being corrected.
- 介词搭配: The product deviates from the accepted standard of quality that customers expect.
- 介词搭配: The actual cost deviated from the budget by only a small margin.
- 介词搭配: Under pressure from senior management, he deviated from the company's ethical guidelines.
- 介词搭配: Some executives deviate from honest accounting practices for personal gain.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 由于恶劣天气，飞行员被迫偏离原定航线。
- 作者偏离了主要话题来讨论一个相关问题。
- 实验结果与研究人员预测的结果有显著偏差。
- 该研究考察了青少年在发展独立性时如何偏离父母的期望。
- 无论发生什么，他从不偏离自己的日常作息。
- 飞机偏离了预定航线数英里后才被修正。
- 该产品偏离了客户期望的公认质量标准。
- 实际成本与预算仅相差很小的幅度。
- 在高级管理层的压力下，他偏离了公司的道德准则。
- 一些高管为了个人利益而偏离诚实的会计实践。
</details>

### 79. devise <a id="79-devise"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *deviser* ("to divide, arrange, devise"), from Latin *dīvīsus* ("divided"), past participle of *dīvidere* ("to separate, divide").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *deviser*（"分开，安排，设计"），源自拉丁语 *dīvīsus*（"分开的"），*dīvidere*（"分离，分开"）的过去分词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to plan or invent something new and clever; to think of a way of doing something
- CN: 设计，发明；策划

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Implies creativity and ingenuity — 暗示创造力和智慧
- Similar to "invent" but more focused on planning — 与 "invent" 类似，但更侧重规划
- "Devise a plan/strategy/system/method" — 设计计划/策略/系统/方法
- Common in business, technology, and strategic contexts — 常用于商业、技术和战略语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- devise a plan
- devise a strategy
- devise a method
- devise a system
- devise a scheme
- devise a solution
- devise a way to
- devise a test
- devise a program
- cleverly devise

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 设计方案
- 制定策略
- 设计方法
- 设计系统
- 策划方案
- 提出解决方案
- 想出办法
- 设计测试
- 设计程序
- 巧妙设计
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- devise for the purpose of
- devise by the team
- devise in response to
- devise over a period of
- devise with the aim of

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为……目的而设计
- 由团队设计
- 为应对……而设计
- 用一段时间设计
- 以……为目标设计
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The engineers devised an innovative system to reduce energy consumption. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The government devised a new economic plan to boost employment. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researchers devised a novel method for extracting clean energy from seawater. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The team devised a series of experiments to test their hypothesis. (Science 2021)
- Common: We need to devise a better way of organizing our workflow.
- 介词搭配: The evacuation plan was devised for the purpose of protecting residents during natural disasters.
- 介词搭配: The new security protocol was devised by the IT department in response to the recent cyber attack.
- 介词搭配: The recovery strategy was devised over a period of six months by a team of experts.
- 介词搭配: The training program was devised with the aim of improving employee productivity.
- 介词搭配: A comprehensive approach was devised to address the complex issue of urban poverty.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 工程师设计了一个创新的系统来降低能耗。
- 政府制定了一项新的经济计划以促进就业。
- 研究人员设计了一种从海水中提取清洁能源的新方法。
- 团队设计了一系列实验来验证他们的假设。
- 我们需要想出一种更好的方式来组织工作流程。
- 疏散计划是为了在自然灾害期间保护居民而设计的。
- 新的安全协议由IT部门为应对最近的网络攻击而设计。
- 复苏策略由专家团队在六个月内制定完成。
- 培训计划旨在提高员工生产力。
- 为了解决城市贫困这一复杂问题，制定了一项综合方案。
</details>

### 80. devote <a id="80-devote"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dēvōtus*, past participle of *dēvovēre* ("to vow, dedicate, devote"), from *dē-* (intensive) + *vovēre* ("to vow").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dēvōtus*，*dēvovēre*（"发誓，奉献，投入"）的过去分词，源自 *dē-*（加强前缀）+ *vovēre*（"发誓"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give all or most of one's time, energy, or attention to someone or something; to use a space or area for a specific purpose
- CN: 致力于，奉献；把……用于

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Devote A to B" = give A to B — "Devote A to B" = 将A投入到B
- "Devote oneself to" = to commit completely to — "Devote oneself to" = 全身心投入
- Similar to "dedicate" but slightly less formal — 与 "dedicate" 类似但稍不正式
- Related noun: "devotion" — 相关名词："devotion"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- devote time to
- devote energy to
- devote attention to
- devote resources to
- devote one's life to
- devote oneself to
- devote effort to
- devote a chapter to
- devote space to
- entirely devote

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 把时间用于
- 把精力用于
- 把注意力用于
- 把资源用于
- 奉献一生
- 全身心投入
- 把努力用于
- 用一章来论述
- 把空间用于
- 完全投入
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- devote to the cause of
- devote to the study of
- devote to research
- devote for the benefit of
- devote over the years to

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 致力于……的事业
- 致力于……的研究
- 致力于研究
- 为……的利益而奉献
- 多年来致力于
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She devoted most of her spare time to volunteering at the local hospital. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The scientist devoted his entire career to finding a cure for the disease. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The museum devotes an entire wing to modern art. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The journal devoted a special issue to the topic of climate change adaptation. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Parents devote countless hours to raising their children.
- 介词搭配: He devoted his life to the cause of social justice and equality.
- 介词搭配: The professor devoted thirty years to the study of ancient civilizations.
- 介词搭配: Significant resources were devoted to research on renewable energy.
- 介词搭配: The foundation allocated funds devoted for the benefit of underprivileged children.
- 介词搭配: She has devoted herself over the years to protecting endangered species.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她把大部分业余时间用于在当地医院做志愿者。
- 这位科学家将整个职业生涯致力于寻找治疗这种疾病的方法。
- 该博物馆将整整一个展区用于现代艺术。
- 该期刊出版了一期特刊，专门讨论气候变化适应问题。
- 父母投入无数时间来抚养孩子。
- 他毕生致力于社会正义和平等事业。
- 这位教授花了三十年时间研究古代文明。
- 大量资源被用于可再生能源研究。
- 基金会拨出专项资金用于贫困儿童的利益。
- 她多年来致力于保护濒危物种。
</details>

### 81. devour <a id="81-devour"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *devorer* ("to devour, consume"), from Latin *dēvorāre* ("to swallow down, devour"), from *dē-* ("down") + *vorāre* ("to swallow, eat greedily").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *devorer*（"吞食，吞噬"），源自拉丁语 *dēvorāre*（"吞下，吞噬"），源自 *dē-*（"向下"）+ *vorāre*（"吞食，贪婪地吃"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to eat something quickly and hungrily; to read or watch something with great eagerness and interest; to destroy something completely
- CN: 狼吞虎咽；贪婪地阅读/观看；吞噬，毁灭

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger and more vivid than "eat" — 比 "eat" 语气更强、更生动
- Figurative: devour a book/movie = to enjoy very much — 比喻义：如饥似渴地阅读/观看
- Can be used for fire/flames devouring a building — 可用于火焰吞噬建筑物
- Also used for emotions: devoured by jealousy/curiosity — 也可用于情感：被嫉妒/好奇吞噬

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- devour food
- devour a book
- devour a novel
- devour information
- devour knowledge
- devour the contents
- devour with fire
- be devoured by
- devour greedily
- devour eagerly

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 狼吞虎咽地吃
- 如饥似渴地读书
- 贪婪地读小说
- 大量获取信息
- 渴求知识
- 贪婪地看内容
- 被火焰吞噬
- 被……吞噬
- 贪婪地吞食
- 急切地吞食
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- devour in one sitting
- devour with great appetite
- devour by fire
- devour over the years
- devour at an alarming rate

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 一口气读完/吃完
- 津津有味地吃
- 被火焰吞噬
- 多年来逐渐吞噬
- 以惊人的速度吞噬
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The hungry children devoured the entire meal in minutes. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: She devoured every book on classical philosophy she could find. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The wildfire devoured thousands of acres of forest before it was contained. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The invasive species devours native vegetation, disrupting the local ecosystem. (Ecology 2021)
- Common: He devoured the latest thriller novel in one weekend.
- 介词搭配: The novel was so gripping that I devoured it in a single sitting.
- 介词搭配: The guests devoured the meal with great appetite and asked for more.
- 介词搭配: The entire warehouse was devoured by fire within hours.
- 介词搭配: Coastal erosion has devoured the shoreline over the years, claiming many properties.
- 介词搭配: Rising costs were devouring the company's profits at an alarming rate.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 饥饿的孩子们几分钟内就把整顿饭狼吞虎咽地吃完了。
- 她如饥似渴地阅读每一本能找到的古典哲学书籍。
- 野火吞噬了数千英亩的森林才被控制住。
- 入侵物种吞食原生植被，破坏了当地的生态系统。
- 他一个周末就贪婪地读完了最新的惊悚小说。
- 这本小说引人入胜，我一口气就看完了。
- 客人们津津有味地吃完了饭，还要求添加。
- 整个仓库在几小时内就被大火吞噬了。
- 多年来海岸侵蚀吞噬了海岸线，夺走了许多房产。
- 不断上升的成本正以惊人的速度吞噬公司的利润。
</details>

### 82. diagnose <a id="82-diagnose"></a>

**Etymology:** From Greek *diagnōsis* ("a discerning, distinguishing"), from *diagignōskein* ("to distinguish, discern"), from *dia-* ("between") + *gignōskein* ("to know, perceive").

**词源：** 源自希腊语 *diagnōsis*（"辨别，诊断"），源自 *diagignōskein*（"区分，辨别"），源自 *dia-*（"在……之间"）+ *gignōskein*（"知道，感知"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to identify the nature of an illness or problem by examining the symptoms; to determine the cause of a problem
- CN: 诊断（疾病）；判断（问题原因）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily medical: diagnose a disease/condition — 主要用于医学：诊断疾病
- Also used figuratively: diagnose a problem/fault — 也可用于比喻：判断问题/故障
- "Diagnose sb with sth" = identify someone's illness — "Diagnose sb with sth" = 诊断某人患有……
- "Diagnose as" = identify as a particular condition — "Diagnose as" = 诊断为

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- diagnose a disease
- diagnose a condition
- diagnose cancer
- diagnose an infection
- diagnose a problem
- diagnose early
- diagnose accurately
- diagnose correctly
- be diagnosed with
- diagnose by symptoms

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 诊断疾病
- 诊断状况
- 诊断癌症
- 诊断感染
- 判断问题
- 早期诊断
- 准确诊断
- 正确诊断
- 被诊断出患有
- 通过症状诊断
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- diagnose with cancer
- diagnose as diabetic
- diagnose by a doctor
- diagnose through tests
- diagnose at an early stage
- diagnose based on symptoms

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 诊断为癌症
- 诊断为糖尿病
- 由医生诊断
- 通过检测诊断
- 在早期阶段诊断
- 基于症状诊断
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The doctor diagnosed her with a rare autoimmune disorder. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The mechanic diagnosed the problem with the engine as a faulty fuel pump. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Early diagnosis of the disease significantly improves the chances of recovery. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The condition is often difficult to diagnose because its symptoms resemble those of other diseases. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: The technician diagnosed the network issue within minutes.
- 介词搭配: The patient was diagnosed with diabetes at the age of 40.
- 介词搭配: His condition was diagnosed as a rare genetic disorder by a team of specialists.
- 介词搭配: The problem was diagnosed by an experienced engineer after a thorough inspection.
- 介词搭配: The disease can be diagnosed through a simple blood test.
- 介词搭配: The illness was diagnosed at an early stage, allowing for prompt treatment.
- 介词搭配: The fault was diagnosed based on the error codes produced by the system.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 医生诊断她患上了一种罕见的自身免疫性疾病。
- 机械师将发动机的问题诊断为燃油泵故障。
- 疾病的早期诊断显著提高了康复的机会。
- 这种病症往往难以诊断，因为其症状与其他疾病相似。
- 技术人员几分钟内就诊断出了网络问题。
- 患者在40岁时被诊断出患有糖尿病。
- 他的病情被一个专家团队诊断为一种罕见的遗传性疾病。
- 问题由一位经验丰富的工程师在全面检查后诊断出来。
- 这种疾病可以通过简单的血液检测来诊断。
- 该疾病在早期阶段被诊断出来，从而能够及时治疗。
- 根据系统产生的错误代码诊断出了故障。
</details>

### 83. dial <a id="83-dial"></a>

**Etymology:** From Medieval Latin *diālis* ("daily"), from Latin *diēs* ("day"), from the use of a sundial. The verb evolved from the noun "dial" (the face of a clock or a telephone).

**词源：** 源自中世纪拉丁语 *diālis*（"每日的"），源自拉丁语 *diēs*（"日"），源自日晷的使用。动词由名词 "dial"（钟面或电话拨号盘）演变而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to call someone using a telephone or mobile phone by entering a number; to adjust a control on a device
- CN: 拨号，打电话；调校（刻度盘、控制器）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Dial a number" = to enter phone digits — "Dial a number" = 拨电话号码
- "Dial in" = to connect via phone or adjust — "Dial in" = 拨入/调准
- "Dial up" = to make a phone call (or internet connection) — "Dial up" = 拨号
- Increasingly replaced by "call" in everyday speech — 日常口语中逐渐被 "call" 取代

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dial a number
- dial 911/emergency
- dial a phone
- dial in
- dial up
- dial the wrong number
- dial directly
- dial a code
- dial the operator
- dial carefully

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拨号码
- 拨打911/紧急电话
- 拨打电话
- 拨入
- 拨号上网
- 拨错号码
- 直接拨打
- 拨区号
- 拨接线员
- 小心拨打
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dial for help
- dial from memory
- dial with a prefix
- dial by mistake
- dial across the room

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 拨打电话求助
- 凭记忆拨打
- 加前缀拨打
- 拨错
- 隔空拨打
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She dialed the emergency number and asked for an ambulance. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: I tried to dial his number several times, but the line was always busy. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: You need to dial the country code before the area code for international calls. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The study examined how quickly people dial emergency services in crisis situations. (Social Psychology 2020)
- Common: I accidentally dialed the wrong number and apologized.
- 介词搭配: He dialed for help when he saw the smoke coming from the building.
- 介词搭配: She dialed her mother's number from memory without looking at her phone.
- 介词搭配: To call overseas, you must dial 00 followed by the country code.
- 介词搭配: I dialed his number by mistake — I meant to call someone else.
- 介词搭配: You can dial the room service number directly from your hotel room.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她拨打了紧急电话并呼叫救护车。
- 我拨了几次他的号码，但一直占线。
- 拨打国际电话时，需要在区号前加拨国家代码。
- 该研究考察了人们在危机情况下拨打紧急服务的速度。
- 我不小心拨错了号码并道了歉。
- 看到大楼冒烟时，他拨打电话求助。
- 她没看手机，凭记忆拨打了母亲的号码。
- 打越洋电话时，必须先拨00再拨国家代码。
- 我拨错了他的号码——我本来是打给别人。
- 你可以从酒店房间直接拨打客房服务电话。
</details>

### 84. dictate <a id="84-dictate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *dictātus*, past participle of *dictāre* ("to say repeatedly, dictate, order"), frequentative of *dīcere* ("to say, speak").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *dictātus*，*dictāre*（"反复说，口述，命令"）的过去分词，*dīcere*（"说，讲"）的反复形式。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to say words for someone else to write down or record; to tell someone what to do in a forceful way; to be the reason for something
- CN: 口述，听写；命令，指使；决定，支配

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Dictate to sb" = to give orders — "Dictate to sb" = 向某人发号施令
- "Dictate that" = to state with authority — "Dictate that" = 规定
- "Dictated by" = determined by circumstances — "Dictated by" = 由……决定
- Can be used literally (dictate a letter) and figuratively (dictate terms) — 可用于字面和比喻

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dictate a letter
- dictate terms
- dictate policy
- dictate the pace of
- dictate the rules
- dictate to sb
- be dictated by
- dictate what to do
- dictate the outcome
- dictate conditions

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 口述信件
- 规定条件
- 制定政策
- 决定节奏
- 制定规则
- 对某人发号施令
- 由……决定
- 规定做什么
- 决定结果
- 规定条件
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dictate to the secretary
- dictate by circumstances
- dictate under duress
- dictate on the basis of
- dictate according to one's own interests

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对秘书口述
- 由情况决定
- 在胁迫下命令
- 基于……决定
- 根据自身利益决定
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The manager dictated a letter to her assistant for immediate distribution. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The treaty dictated the terms of peace between the two nations. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Market forces dictate the prices of goods and services in a capitalist economy. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The experimental protocol dictated that all measurements be taken at the same time of day. (Nature 2021)
- Common: You can't dictate to me what I should do with my life.
- 介词搭配: The CEO dictated a memo to her secretary summarizing the meeting outcomes.
- 介词搭配: The choice of location was dictated by the need for easy access to public transportation.
- 介词搭配: The confession was dictated under duress and later recanted.
- 介词搭配: The rules were dictated on the basis of what the majority considered acceptable.
- 介词搭配: He always dictates according to his own interests, without considering others.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经理向助理口述了一封信件以便立即分发。
- 该条约规定了两国之间的和平条件。
- 在资本主义经济中，市场力量决定商品和服务的价格。
- 实验方案规定所有测量必须在一天中的同一时间进行。
- 你不能命令我该如何生活。
- 首席执行官向秘书口述了一份总结会议成果的备忘录。
- 地点的选择取决于便捷公共交通的需求。
- 这份供词是在胁迫下作出的，后来被推翻了。
- 规则是根据大多数人认为可接受的标准来制定的。
- 他总是根据自己的利益来做决定，不考虑他人。
</details>

### 85. die <a id="85-die"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *dīegan* ("to die"), from Proto-Germanic *diwaną* ("to die"), from Proto-Indo-European *dʰew-* ("to pass away, die").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *dīegan*（"死亡"），源自原始日耳曼语 *diwaną*（"死亡"），源自原始印欧语 *dʰew-*（"去世，死亡"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to stop living; to stop functioning; to become gradually less strong or active before disappearing completely
- CN: 死亡；停止运转；消失，消亡

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- The most basic verb for death — 表示死亡最基本的动词
- "Die of/from" = to die because of — "Die of/from" = 因……而死
- "Die for" = to die in order to achieve something — "Die for" = 为……而死
- "Die out/off" = to become extinct — "Die out/off" = 灭绝
- "Be dying to do" = to want something very much — "Be dying to do" = 非常想做某事
- Many idiomatic uses: die hard, die laughing, never say die, etc. — 许多习语用法

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- die of/from
- die for
- die in
- die at
- die out
- die off
- die a natural death
- die young
- die peacefully
- be dying to/for

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 死于（疾病/原因）
- 为……而死
- 死于（地点/事件）
- 在……岁时去世
- 灭绝
- 相继死去
- 自然死亡
- 英年早逝
- 安详离世
- 非常渴望
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- die of a heart attack
- die from injuries
- die in an accident
- die at the age of
- die by suicide
- die for one's country
- die from natural causes
- die of starvation
- die in battle
- die at home

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 死于心脏病发作
- 因伤而死
- 死于事故
- 在……岁时去世
- 自杀而死
- 为国捐躯
- 自然死亡
- 死于饥饿
- 战死沙场
- 在家中离世
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The elderly man died peacefully in his sleep at the age of 94. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The traditional art form is slowly dying out as fewer young people learn it. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many species died out as a result of drastic climate changes in prehistoric times. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The study found that the cells die off naturally through a process called apoptosis. (Cell Biology 2022)
- Common: I'm dying to know what happened at the meeting!
- 介词搭配: The patient died of complications following surgery.
- 介词搭配: Two people died from injuries sustained in the car crash.
- 介词搭配: The soldier died for his country in the line of duty.
- 介词搭配: The writer died at the age of 85, leaving behind a rich literary legacy.
- 介词搭配: The sound of the music died out as the band left the stage.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这位老人在睡梦中安详离世，享年94岁。
- 由于学习它的年轻人越来越少，这种传统艺术形式正在慢慢消亡。
- 许多物种因史前时期剧烈的气候变化而灭绝。
- 研究发现，细胞通过一种称为凋亡的过程自然死亡。
- 我很想知道会议上发生了什么！
- 患者死于手术后的并发症。
- 两人在车祸中受伤不治身亡。
- 这名士兵在执行任务时为国捐躯。
- 这位作家在85岁时去世，留下了丰富的文学遗产。
- 乐队离开舞台时，音乐声逐渐消失。
</details>
