### 86. differ <a id="86-differ"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *differer* ("to differ, delay"), from Latin *differre* ("to carry apart, differ, postpone"), from *dis-* ("apart") + *ferre* ("to carry, bear").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *differer*（"不同，推迟"），源自拉丁语 *differre*（"分开携带，不同，推迟"），源自 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *ferre*（"携带，承受"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be different from something or someone; to have a different opinion from someone
- CN: 不同，有差异；持不同意见，有分歧

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Differ from" = to be unlike — "Differ from" = 与……不同
- "Differ in" = to have differences in a specific aspect — "Differ in" = 在……方面不同
- "Differ with/from" = to disagree with someone — "Differ with/from" = 与某人意见不同
- "Beg to differ" = a polite way to disagree — "Beg to differ" = 恕不同意

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- differ from
- differ in
- differ with
- differ on
- differ over
- differ significantly
- differ greatly
- differ markedly
- differ widely
- agree to differ

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与……不同
- 在……方面不同
- 与……意见不同
- 在……上意见不同
- 就……有分歧
- 显著不同
- 大不相同
- 明显不同
- 有很大差异
- 同意各自保留不同意见
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- differ from the norm
- differ in opinion
- differ on the issue
- differ over the details
- differ as to how
- differ among themselves
- differ by age group

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 不同于常规
- 意见不同
- 在这个问题上意见不同
- 在细节上有分歧
- 就如何……有分歧
- 内部有分歧
- 因年龄组而不同
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The two proposals differ significantly in their approach to solving the problem. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Experts differ on the effectiveness of the new treatment. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Languages differ from one another in grammar, vocabulary, and pronunciation. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The findings of this study differ markedly from those of previous research. (Science 2022)
- Common: I beg to differ with your assessment of the situation.
- 介词搭配: The new model differs from the previous version in several important respects.
- 介词搭配: Scientists differ in opinion about the causes of the recent climate anomalies.
- 介词搭配: The committee members differed on the issue of budget allocation for the project.
- 介词搭配: They differed over the financial details of the merger agreement.
- 介词搭配: The experts differed as to how best to implement the new regulations.
- 介词搭配: The board members differed among themselves regarding the strategic direction.
- 介词搭配: Consumer preferences differ by age group and geographic location.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这两个方案在解决问题的方法上有显著不同。
- 专家们对新的治疗方法的效果意见不一。
- 各种语言在语法、词汇和发音方面互不相同。
- 这项研究的发现与先前的研究结果明显不同。
- 恕我对您对形势的评估不敢苟同。
- 新款与旧款在几个重要方面有所不同。
- 科学家们对近期气候异常的起因意见不一。
- 委员会成员在项目预算分配问题上存在分歧。
- 他们就合并协议的财务细节产生了分歧。
- 专家们就如何最好地实施新规定意见不一。
- 董事会成员在战略方向上存在内部分歧。
- 消费者的偏好因年龄组和地理位置而异。
</details>

### 87. differentiate <a id="87-differentiate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Medieval Latin *differentiātus*, past participle of *differentiāre* ("to distinguish, differentiate"), from Latin *differentia* ("difference, distinction"), from *differre* ("to differ").

**词源：** 源自中世纪拉丁语 *differentiātus*，*differentiāre*（"区分，辨别"）的过去分词，源自拉丁语 *differentia*（"差异，区别"），源自 *differre*（"不同"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to recognize or show that two things are different; to make something different from others; (biology) cells become specialized
- CN: 区分，辨别；使不同；分化

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Differentiate A from B" / "Differentiate between A and B" — 区分A和B
- More formal than "tell apart" — 比 "tell apart" 更正式
- Used in business (differentiate a product), education, and science — 用于商业（产品差异化）、教育和科学
- Related noun: "differentiation" — 相关名词："differentiation"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- differentiate between
- differentiate from
- differentiate a product
- differentiate oneself
- differentiate the two
- differentiate clearly
- differentiate sharply
- easily differentiate
- hard to differentiate
- differentiate cells

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分
- 与……区分开
- 使产品差异化
- 使自己与众不同
- 区分两者
- 清楚区分
- 明显区分
- 容易区分
- 难以区分
- 使细胞分化
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- differentiate between good and bad
- differentiate from competitors
- differentiate by quality
- differentiate through innovation
- differentiate on the basis of
- differentiate at various stages

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分好与坏
- 与竞争对手区分
- 以质量区分
- 通过创新使不同
- 基于……区分
- 在不同阶段分化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is important to differentiate between facts and opinions in academic writing. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The company differentiates itself from its competitors through superior customer service. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ability to differentiate colors varies among different species of animals. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: Stem cells differentiate into various types of specialized cells during development. (Nature 2021)
- Common: The twins look so alike that it's hard to differentiate them.
- 介词搭配: Children learn to differentiate between right and wrong as they grow up.
- 介词搭配: The brand differentiates itself from competitors by offering eco-friendly products.
- 介词搭配: The two products are differentiated by quality rather than price.
- 介词搭配: The company differentiates through continuous innovation in technology.
- 介词搭配: Candidates were differentiated on the basis of their qualifications and experience.
- 介词搭配: Embryonic cells differentiate at various stages of development into specific tissues.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在学术写作中区分事实和观点非常重要。
- 该公司通过卓越的客户服务使自己与竞争对手区分开来。
- 辨别颜色的能力因动物物种而异。
- 干细胞在发育过程中分化成各种类型的特化细胞。
- 这对双胞胎长得太像了，很难区分他们。
- 孩子们在成长过程中学会区分对与错。
- 该品牌通过提供环保产品使自己与竞争对手区分开来。
- 这两种产品以质量而非价格来区分。
- 该公司通过持续的技术创新来实现差异化。
- 候选人根据其资历和经验进行区分。
- 胚胎细胞在发育的不同阶段分化为特定组织。
</details>

### 88. diffuse <a id="88-diffuse"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin *diffūsus*, past participle of *diffundere* ("to pour out, spread, diffuse"), from *dis-* ("apart") + *fundere* ("to pour").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *diffūsus*，*diffundere*（"倒出，扩散，散开"）的过去分词，源自 *dis-*（"分开"）+ *fundere*（"倾倒"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to spread over a wide area; to make light or gas spread evenly; to spread information or ideas among many people
- CN: 扩散，弥漫；传播，散布

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both physical (light, gas, liquid) and metaphorical (ideas, knowledge) — 既用于物理（光、气体、液体）也用于比喻（思想、知识）
- "Diffuse through/throughout" = spread across — "Diffuse through/throughout" = 扩散到整个
- Different from "defuse" (to make safe) — 注意与 "defuse"（使安全）区别
- Related noun: "diffusion" — 相关名词："diffusion"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- diffuse light
- diffuse knowledge
- diffuse information
- diffuse through
- diffuse throughout
- diffuse across
- diffuse into the air
- diffuse widely
- gradually diffuse
- rapidly diffuse

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使光线漫射
- 传播知识
- 传播信息
- 扩散通过
- 扩散到整个
- 扩散到各处
- 扩散到空气中
- 广泛传播
- 逐渐扩散
- 迅速扩散
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- diffuse through the membrane
- diffuse into the atmosphere
- diffuse across the cell wall
- diffuse throughout the population
- diffuse among the public

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过细胞膜扩散
- 扩散到大气中
- 穿过细胞壁扩散
- 在整个人群中传播
- 在公众中传播
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The new technology has diffused rapidly throughout the developing world. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Knowledge diffuses more quickly now thanks to the internet and digital media. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The scent of the flowers diffused through the entire garden. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study examines how innovations diffuse across different cultural contexts. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The smoke gradually diffused into the air and disappeared.
- 介词搭配: Oxygen diffuses through the cell membrane into the bloodstream.
- 介词搭配: The gas diffused into the atmosphere, causing air pollution.
- 介词搭配: Light diffuses across the surface of the frosted glass, creating a soft glow.
- 介词搭配: The practice of using renewable energy diffused throughout the population over time.
- 介词搭配: The rumor quickly diffused among the public through social media platforms.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 新技术已迅速传播到整个发展中世界。
- 由于互联网和数字媒体，现在知识传播得更快了。
- 花香弥漫了整个花园。
- 该研究考察了创新如何在不同文化背景中传播。
- 烟雾逐渐扩散到空气中并消失了。
- 氧气通过细胞膜扩散到血液中。
- 气体扩散到大气中，造成了空气污染。
- 光线漫过磨砂玻璃表面，形成柔和的光晕。
- 使用可再生能源的做法逐渐在人群中传播开来。
- 谣言通过社交媒体平台在公众中迅速传播。
</details>

### 89. dig <a id="89-dig"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English *diggen* ("to dig"), of uncertain origin; possibly from Old French *diger* ("to dig, hollow out"), from Latin *dīcāre* ("to set, fix").

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *diggen*（"挖"），词源不确定；可能源自古法语 *diger*（"挖，挖空"），源自拉丁语 *dīcāre*（"安置，固定"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a hole in the earth or ground; to find or discover something by searching; to like or understand something (informal)
- CN: 挖，掘；发掘，寻找；喜欢，理解（非正式）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: dig a hole/tunnel — 字面义：挖洞/隧道
- Figurative: dig for information, dig into a topic — 比喻义：探究信息，深入研究话题
- "Dig in" = to start eating (informal) or to establish a position — "Dig in" = 开始吃/巩固阵地
- "Dig up" = to find or unearth — "Dig up" = 挖出，发掘
- Slang: "I dig it" = I like it — 俚语："I dig it" = 我喜欢

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dig a hole
- dig a tunnel
- dig a grave
- dig up
- dig out
- dig through
- dig into
- dig for gold
- dig for information
- dig deep

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 挖洞
- 挖隧道
- 挖坟墓
- 挖出
- 挖出/找出
- 挖穿
- 探究
- 挖金
- 搜集信息
- 深挖/全力以赴
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dig into the ground
- dig for treasure
- dig through the rubble
- dig up evidence
- dig in the garden
- dig with a shovel
- dig under the wall

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 挖入地下
- 挖宝
- 在废墟中挖掘
- 挖出证据
- 在花园里挖土
- 用铲子挖
- 在墙下挖
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The archaeologists dug carefully around the ancient artifact. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The journalist dug deep into the corruption scandal and uncovered new evidence. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researchers dug through layers of sediment to reach the fossil-bearing rock. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study examines how earthworms dig through soil and improve its structure. (Ecology 2021)
- Common: I really dig this band's new album!
- 介词搭配: The rescue team dug into the ground to reach the trapped miners.
- 介词搭配: Children love to dig for buried treasure on the beach.
- 介词搭配: Rescue workers dug through the rubble looking for survivors.
- 介词搭配: The detective managed to dig up new evidence that led to a conviction.
- 介词搭配: He was digging in the garden when he found an old coin.
- 介词搭配: The prisoners dug a tunnel under the prison wall with makeshift tools.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 考古学家小心翼翼地在那件古代器物周围挖掘。
- 那位记者深挖腐败丑闻，发现了新证据。
- 研究人员挖穿沉积层，到达了含化石的岩石层。
- 该研究考察了蚯蚓如何挖掘土壤并改善土壤结构。
- 我真的很喜欢这个乐队的新专辑！
- 救援队挖入地下，到达被困矿工的位置。
- 孩子们喜欢在海滩上挖寻"宝藏"。
- 救援人员在废墟中挖掘，寻找幸存者。
- 侦探设法挖出了导致定罪的新证据。
- 他在花园里挖土时发现了一枚古币。
- 囚犯们用简陋的工具在监狱墙下挖了一条隧道。
</details>

### 90. discourage <a id="90-discourage"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *descouragier* ("to discourage"), from *des-* ("away, apart") + *corage* ("courage"), from Latin *cor* ("heart").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *descouragier*（"使气馁，阻止"），源自 *des-*（"离开"）+ *corage*（"勇气"），源自拉丁语 *cor*（"心"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone less confident or less willing to do something; to try to prevent something by showing disapproval or raising difficulties
- CN: 使气馁，使灰心；阻止，劝阻

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Discourage sb from doing sth" = to advise against — "Discourage sb from doing sth" = 劝阻某人做某事
- Opposite of "encourage" — 与 "encourage" 相反
- Can refer to both emotional state (making someone feel less confident) and practical prevention — 既可指情绪状态也可指实际阻止
- Related noun: "discouragement" — 相关名词："discouragement"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- discourage sb
- discourage sb from doing
- discourage smoking
- discourages investment
- discourage the use of
- feel discouraged
- become discouraged
- discourage competition
- discourage bad habits
- actively discourage

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人气馁
- 劝阻某人做某事
- 劝阻吸烟
- 抑制投资
- 阻止使用
- 感到气馁
- 变得灰心
- 阻止竞争
- 阻止坏习惯
- 积极阻止
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- discourage from pursuing
- discourage by negative feedback
- discourage through regulations
- discourage on health grounds
- discourage by example

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 劝阻追求
- 因负面反馈而气馁
- 通过法规阻止
- 基于健康原因劝阻
- 以实例劝阻
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Parents should discourage their children from spending too much time on video games. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: High interest rates discourage borrowing and slow down economic growth. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The school's strict policy discourages students from cheating on exams. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The study found that early negative experiences can discourage children from pursuing scientific careers. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Don't let one failure discourage you from trying again.
- 介词搭配: The teacher discouraged students from pursuing careers in fields with limited job prospects.
- 介词搭配: The young artist was discouraged by the constant negative feedback from critics.
- 介词搭配: The government discourages the use of plastic bags through a tax on their distribution.
- 介词搭配: Smoking is discouraged on health grounds in all public buildings.
- 介词搭配: The team's poor performance discouraged others by example from attempting the same approach.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 父母应该劝阻孩子花太多时间玩电子游戏。
- 高利率抑制借贷并减缓经济增长。
- 学校的严格政策阻止学生考试作弊。
- 研究发现，早期的负面经历会阻止儿童追求科学事业。
- 不要因为一次失败就灰心而不愿再尝试。
- 老师劝阻学生不要从事就业前景有限的领域。
- 这位年轻艺术家因评论家不断的负面反馈而气馁。
- 政府通过对塑料袋的流通征税来阻止其使用。
- 在所有公共建筑中，基于健康原因禁止吸烟。
- 团队的糟糕表现以实例劝阻了其他人尝试同样的方法。
</details>

### 91. discover <a id="91-discover"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French descovrir (Modern French découvrir), from Late Latin discooperire (dis- 'un-, apart' + cooperire 'to cover'), from Latin cooperire 'to cover over.'

**词源：** 源自古法语 descovrir（现代法语 découvrir），源自晚期拉丁语 discooperire（dis- '不，分开' + cooperire '覆盖'），源自拉丁语 cooperire '完全覆盖'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to find information, a place, or an object, especially for the first time; to find out about something
- CN: 发现；发觉；找到（尤指首次发现的事物）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used for finding something that existed but was unknown — 常用于发现已存在但不为人知的事物
- Can be followed by a noun or a that-clause — 后可接名词或 that 从句
- Related noun: discovery — 相关名词：discovery

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- discover a new planet
- discover a cure
- discover the truth
- discover a talent
- discover a secret
- discover a solution
- newly discovered
- recently discovered
- surprisingly discover

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 发现新行星
- 发现疗法
- 发现真相
- 发现才能
- 发现秘密
- 找到解决方案
- 新发现的
- 最近发现的
- 惊讶地发现
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- discover by accident/chance
- discover through research
- discover in the course of
- discover among the ruins
- discover during the excavation
- discover at an early stage

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 偶然发现
- 通过研究发现
- 在……过程中发现
- 在废墟中发现
- 在挖掘期间发现
- 在早期阶段发现
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Scientists have discovered a new species of frog in the Amazon rainforest. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: Researchers discovered a link between sleep deprivation and decreased cognitive function. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The expedition discovered the ruins of an ancient civilization buried beneath the desert. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study discovered a genetic mutation responsible for the rare disease. (Cell 2021)
- Common: I discovered that I have a talent for painting when I took a class last year.
- 介词搭配: The ancient manuscript was discovered by accident in a monastery library.
- 介词搭配: Penicillin was discovered through research on mold cultures.
- 介词搭配: The fossils were discovered during the construction of a new highway.
- 介词搭配: She discovered her passion for music at an early age.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 科学家在亚马逊雨林中发现了一种新的蛙类。
- 研究人员发现了睡眠不足与认知功能下降之间的联系。
- 探险队发现了埋在沙漠下的古城废墟。
- 该研究发现了一种导致这种罕见疾病的基因突变。
- 去年上课时，我发现自己有绘画天赋。
- 这份古代手稿是在一座修道院图书馆偶然发现的。
- 青霉素是通过对霉菌培养的研究而发现的。
- 这些化石是在修建一条新高速公路期间发现的。
- 她早年就发现了自己对音乐的热爱。
</details>

### 92. discriminate <a id="92-discriminate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin discriminatus, past participle of discriminare ('to divide, separate, distinguish'), from discrimen ('division, distinction'), from discernere ('to discern, distinguish').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 discriminatus，discriminare（'分开，区分'）的过去分词，源自 discrimen（'区分，区别'），源自 discernere（'辨别，区分'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to recognize a difference between things; to treat a person or group unfairly because of prejudice
- CN: 区分，辨别；歧视，区别对待

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Discriminate between A and B' = to tell the difference — 'Discriminate between A and B' = 区分 A 和 B
- 'Discriminate against' = to treat unfairly (negative) — 'Discriminate against' = 歧视（贬义）
- 'Discriminate in favor of' = to favor — 'Discriminate in favor of' = 偏袒
- Adjective: discriminatory; Noun: discrimination — 形容词：discriminatory；名词：discrimination

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- discriminate between
- discriminate against
- discriminate in favor of
- discriminate on the basis of
- discriminate by race
- discriminate by age
- discriminate by gender
- discriminate against minorities
- learn to discriminate
- difficult to discriminate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分……之间
- 歧视
- 偏袒
- 基于……歧视
- 种族歧视
- 年龄歧视
- 性别歧视
- 歧视少数群体
- 学会辨别
- 难以区分
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- discriminate between right and wrong
- discriminate against women/minorities
- discriminate in favor of the rich
- discriminate on grounds of
- discriminate by skin color
- discriminate on the basis of age

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分对错
- 歧视妇女/少数群体
- 偏袒富人
- 以……理由歧视
- 以肤色歧视
- 基于年龄歧视
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is illegal to discriminate against someone because of their religious beliefs. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The study found that companies still discriminate on the basis of gender in hiring practices. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Young children cannot always discriminate between reality and fantasy. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The algorithm was found to discriminate against certain ethnic groups in its recommendations. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The law prohibits employers from discriminating against people with disabilities.
- 介词搭配: It is important to discriminate between facts and opinions when reading the news.
- 介词搭配: The policy discriminates in favor of local businesses over international corporations.
- 介词搭配: Job applicants should not be discriminated against on grounds of their age.
- 介词搭配: The study revealed that the system discriminates by postcode, favoring wealthier areas.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 因宗教信仰歧视他人是违法的。
- 研究发现公司在招聘实践中仍然存在性别歧视。
- 幼儿并不总能区分现实与幻想。
- 该算法被发现在其推荐中歧视某些族群。
- 法律禁止雇主歧视残疾人士。
- 阅读新闻时区分事实和观点很重要。
- 该政策偏袒本地企业而非跨国公司。
- 求职者不应因其年龄而受到歧视。
- 研究显示该系统按邮政编码进行歧视，偏向富裕地区。
</details>

### 93. discuss <a id="93-discuss"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin discussus, past participle of discutere ('to dash to pieces, shake, strike'), from dis- ('apart') + quatere ('to shake, strike'). Later meaning shifted to 'examine, investigate, debate.'

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 discussus，discutere（'打碎，摇动，击打'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ quatere（'摇动，击打'）。后来词义转变为'检查，调查，辩论'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to talk about something with someone in detail; to examine or consider a subject in speech or writing
- CN: 讨论；谈论；商讨

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Directly followed by the topic (no preposition): discuss the matter — 直接接话题（不需要介词）：discuss the matter
- 'Discuss about' is INCORRECT (common error) — 'Discuss about' 是错误的（常见错误）
- Can be followed by wh-words: discuss what/how/when — 后可接疑问词：discuss what/how/when
- Related noun: discussion — 相关名词：discussion

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- discuss an issue
- discuss a topic
- discuss a plan
- discuss the details
- discuss the matter
- discuss the possibility of
- discuss openly
- discuss frankly
- discuss thoroughly
- discuss at length

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 讨论问题
- 讨论话题
- 讨论计划
- 讨论细节
- 讨论此事
- 讨论……的可能性
- 公开讨论
- 坦诚讨论
- 彻底讨论
- 详细讨论
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- discuss with sb
- discuss in detail
- discuss at the meeting
- discuss over lunch
- discuss among themselves
- discuss in private
- discuss on the phone

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人讨论
- 详细讨论
- 在会上讨论
- 在午餐时讨论
- 内部讨论
- 私下讨论
- 通过电话讨论
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The committee will discuss the proposed changes to the curriculum at the next meeting. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The conference provided a platform for experts to discuss the future of renewable energy. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students were encouraged to discuss their ideas in small groups before sharing with the class. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The paper discusses the implications of climate change on global food security. (Nature 2023)
- Common: We need to sit down and discuss this problem calmly.
- 介词搭配: I would like to discuss this matter with you in more detail.
- 介词搭配: The issue was discussed at length during the board meeting.
- 介词搭配: The team discussed the proposal among themselves before making a decision.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 委员会将在下次会议上讨论对课程设置的拟议修改。
- 该会议为专家们讨论可再生能源的未来提供了平台。
- 鼓励学生先在小范围内讨论自己的想法，然后再与全班分享。
- 本文讨论了气候变化对全球粮食安全的影响。
- 我们需要坐下来冷静地讨论这个问题。
- 我想与您更详细地讨论这件事。
- 在董事会上详细讨论了这个问题。
- 团队内部讨论了这个提案，然后才做出决定。
</details>

### 94. disguise <a id="94-disguise"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desguiser ('to disguise, change one's appearance'), from des- ('away, off') + guise ('manner, fashion, appearance'), from Germanic origin.

**词源：** 源自古法语 desguiser（'伪装，改变外表'），源自 des-（'离开'）+ guise（'方式，时尚，外表'），源自日耳曼语。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to change the appearance or nature of something in order to conceal its identity; to hide a feeling or intention
- CN: 伪装；假扮；掩饰；隐瞒

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Disguise oneself as' = to dress up as — 'Disguise oneself as' = 装扮成
- 'Disguise the fact that' = to hide the truth — 'Disguise the fact that' = 掩饰事实
- Common phrase: 'a blessing in disguise' — 常用短语：'a blessing in disguise' = 塞翁失马
- Both literal (costume) and figurative (emotion) use — 既用于字面（服装）也用于比喻（情感）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disguise oneself
- disguise one's voice
- disguise one's feelings
- disguise the truth
- disguise a smell
- disguise the fact that
- thinly disguised
- poorly disguised
- cleverly disguised
- disguise one's identity

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 伪装自己
- 伪装声音
- 掩饰情感
- 掩盖真相
- 掩盖气味
- 掩盖……的事实
- 几乎不加掩饰的
- 伪装拙劣的
- 巧妙伪装的
- 隐藏身份
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disguise as a waiter
- disguise in a uniform
- disguise behind a smile
- disguise with makeup
- disguise under a false name
- disguise by wearing a mask

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 穿制服伪装
- 用微笑掩饰
- 用化妆伪装
- 用假名掩饰
- 戴口罩伪装
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: He tried to disguise his disappointment with a forced smile. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Certain animals can disguise themselves by blending into their surroundings. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The study suggests that some plants disguise themselves as rocks to avoid being eaten. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Losing that job turned out to be a blessing in disguise.
- 介词搭配: The criminal disguised himself as a security guard to enter the building.
- 介词搭配: She disguised her nervousness behind a confident smile.
- 介词搭配: The bitter taste is disguised with sugar and lemon.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 他试图用强装的笑容来掩饰自己的失望。
- 某些动物可以通过融入周围环境来伪装自己。
- 研究表明某些植物伪装成岩石来避免被吃掉。
- 失去那份工作反而是塞翁失马。
- 罪犯假扮成保安进入了大楼。
- 她用自信的微笑掩饰了紧张。
- 用糖和柠檬来掩盖苦味。
</details>

### 95. disgust <a id="95-disgust"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle French desgouster ('to cause disgust, to loathe'), from des- ('away') + goust/gout ('taste'), from Latin gustus ('taste'). Literally 'to take away the taste.'

**词源：** 源自中古法语 desgouster（'引起反感，厌恶'），源自 des-（'离开'）+ goust/gout（'味道'），源自拉丁语 gustus（'味道'）。字面意为'使失去味道'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause a strong feeling of dislike or disapproval; to make someone feel sick or offended
- CN: 使厌恶，使反感；令人作呕

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive: be disgusted at/by/with — 常用于被动语态：be disgusted at/by/with
- Stronger than 'dislike', weaker than 'loathe' — 语气比 'dislike' 强，比 'loathe' 弱
- Related adjective: disgusting; Noun: disgust — 相关形容词：disgusting；名词：disgust

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- be disgusted at
- be disgusted by
- be disgusted with
- feel disgust
- express disgust
- show disgust
- in disgust
- disgust the public
- disgust the audience
- utter disgust

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……感到厌恶
- 被……恶心到
- 对……反感
- 感到恶心
- 表示厌恶
- 显出厌恶
- 厌恶地
- 使公众反感
- 使观众恶心
- 极其厌恶
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disgust at the behavior
- disgust by the cruelty
- disgust with oneself
- disgust at the sight of
- disgust by the smell
- disgust with the situation

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对行为的厌恶
- 对残忍行为的反感
- 对自己感到厌恶
- 看到……感到恶心
- 闻到……感到恶心
- 对情况的厌恶
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The public was disgusted by the corruption scandal involving government officials. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: Many people expressed disgust at the company's unethical business practices. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The sight of the polluted river disgusted the environmental activists. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study found that feelings of disgust can influence moral judgments more than rational analysis. (Science 2022)
- Common: The way he treated his employees absolutely disgusts me.
- 介词搭配: She was disgusted at the waste of food in the cafeteria.
- 介词搭配: The audience was disgusted by the violent scenes in the movie.
- 介词搭配: He felt disgusted with himself for lying to his friend.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 公众对涉及政府官员的腐败丑闻感到厌恶。
- 许多人对该公司不道德的商业行为表示反感。
- 看到被污染的河流，环保活动人士感到厌恶。
- 研究发现厌恶感比理性分析更能影响道德判断。
- 他对待员工的方式让我极为反感。
- 她对食堂浪费食物的行为感到厌恶。
- 观众被电影中的暴力场面恶心到了。
- 他因对朋友撒谎而对自己感到厌恶。
</details>

### 96. disintegrate <a id="96-disintegrate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin disintegrare ('to break apart, shatter'), from dis- ('apart') + integrare ('to make whole'), from integer ('whole, intact').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 disintegrare（'打碎，粉碎'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ integrare（'使完整'），源自 integer（'完整的，完好无损的'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to break into small parts or pieces; to become destroyed or fail completely
- CN: 碎裂，崩裂；瓦解，崩溃

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used for physical objects and abstract concepts — 可用于具体物体和抽象概念
- Often suggests complete destruction or fragmentation — 通常暗示彻底破坏或碎裂
- Related noun: disintegration — 相关名词：disintegration

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disintegrate into
- disintegrate completely
- disintegrate rapidly
- disintegrate gradually
- disintegrate in the air
- disintegrate upon impact
- disintegrate over time
- meteor disintegrates
- relationship disintegrates
- empire disintegrates

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 碎裂成
- 完全碎裂
- 迅速崩解
- 逐渐瓦解
- 在空中解体
- 撞击时碎裂
- 随时间瓦解
- 流星解体
- 关系破裂
- 帝国瓦解
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disintegrate into pieces
- disintegrate in the atmosphere
- disintegrate upon re-entry
- disintegrate from heat
- disintegrate under pressure
- disintegrate over the years

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 碎裂成碎片
- 在大气层中解体
- 重新进入时解体
- 因高温而分解
- 在压力下瓦解
- 经过多年而瓦解
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The spacecraft disintegrated as it entered the Earth's atmosphere. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The ancient empire gradually disintegrated due to internal conflicts and external pressures. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Paper documents can disintegrate over time if not stored under proper conditions. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The protein complex disintegrates when exposed to high temperatures. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The old building's walls have begun to disintegrate after years of neglect.
- 介词搭配: The meteor disintegrated into thousands of fragments upon entering the atmosphere.
- 介词搭配: The coalition government disintegrated under the pressure of political infighting.
- 介词搭配: The friendship disintegrated over the years due to misunderstandings.
</details>

</details>

### 97. dislike <a id="97-dislike"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English disliken, from dis- ('not, opposite of') + liken ('to please, be pleasing'), from Old English līcian ('to please').

**词源：** 源自中古英语 disliken，源自 dis-（'不，相反'）+ liken（'使喜欢'），源自古英语 līcian（'使高兴'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to not like something or someone; to find something unpleasant or disagreeable
- CN: 不喜欢；厌恶；反感

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Milder than 'hate', stronger than 'not like' — 比 'hate' 语气弱，比 'not like' 强
- Can be followed by noun/pronoun or gerund: dislike doing sth — 后可接名词/代词或动名词：dislike doing sth
- Also used as a noun: have a dislike of/for — 也可作名词：have a dislike of/for

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dislike sb/sth
- dislike the idea of
- dislike the thought of
- dislike doing sth
- strongly dislike
- intensely dislike
- take a dislike to
- dislike the way
- grow to dislike
- openly dislike

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 不喜欢……的想法
- 不喜欢……的念头
- 不喜欢做某事
- 非常不喜欢
- 极其厌恶
- 开始讨厌
- 不喜欢……的方式
- 逐渐变得不喜欢
- 公开表示不喜欢
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dislike for sb
- dislike of doing sth
- dislike about sb/sth
- dislike in a person
- dislike on principle

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 不喜欢某人
- 不喜欢做某事
- 不喜欢某人/某事的方面
- 对某人的不喜欢
- 原则上不喜欢
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many people dislike the idea of working overtime without extra pay. (CET-4 2019-12)

- TOEFL/IELTS: Some students strongly dislike group projects because of unequal participation. (TOEFL TPO 47)

- 介词搭配: She took an instant dislike to the new manager.

</details>

### 98. dismantle <a id="98-dismantle"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desmanteler ('to dismantle, raze'), from des- ('away, off') + mantel ('mantle, cloak'), from Latin mantellum ('cloak'). Originally meant 'to remove a cloak,' later 'to strip of defenses.'

**词源：** 源自古法语 desmanteler（'拆除，夷平'），源自 des-（'离开'）+ mantel（'斗篷，罩袍'），源自拉丁语 mantellum（'斗篷'）。原义为'脱去斗篷'，后引申为'拆除防御'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take apart a machine or structure; to end a system or organization systematically
- CN: 拆除；拆卸；（系统性地）废除，解散

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: dismantle a machine/engine/building — 具体：拆卸机器/引擎/建筑
- Figurative: dismantle a system/law/monopoly — 比喻：废除制度/法律/垄断
- Related noun: dismantlement — 相关名词：dismantlement

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dismantle a machine
- dismantle a building
- dismantle a system
- dismantle barriers
- dismantle the old regime
- dismantle nuclear weapons
- dismantle apartheid
- dismantle an organization
- completely dismantle
- carefully dismantle

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拆除建筑
- 废除制度
- 消除障碍
- 推翻旧政权
- 拆除核武器
- 废除种族隔离
- 解散组织
- 完全拆除
- 小心拆卸
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dismantle into parts
- dismantle for safety
- dismantle by workers
- dismantle over time
- dismantle in stages
- dismantle with tools

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 拆分成零件
- 为安全而拆除
- 由工人拆除
- 逐渐废除
- 分阶段拆除
- 用工具拆卸
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The workers carefully dismantled the old scaffolding after the construction was completed. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The new government moved quickly to dismantle the previous administration's policies. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In order to move the equipment, we had to dismantle it into smaller pieces. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study proposes a framework to dismantle institutional barriers to equal education. (Nature 2022)

- 介词搭配: The nuclear warheads were dismantled in stages under international supervision.
- 介词搭配: The engine was dismantled into hundreds of individual parts for cleaning and repair.
- 介词搭配: The discriminatory laws were gradually dismantled over the course of a decade.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

### 99. dismay <a id="99-dismay"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desmayer ('to dismay, trouble, alarm'), from des- ('away, off') + mäer ('to trouble, disturb'), from Proto-Germanic *mag- ('power, ability'), originally 'to deprive of power.'

**词源：** 源自古法语 desmayer（'使惊慌，困扰，警告'），源自 des-（'离开'）+ mäer（'打扰，困扰'），源自原始日耳曼语 *mag-（'力量，能力'），原义为'使失去力量'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone to feel worried, disappointed, or upset; to fill with alarm or consternation
- CN: 使惊愕，使焦虑；使失望，使沮丧

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Express strong disappointment or worry — 表达强烈的失望或担忧
- Often used in passive: be dismayed at/by — 常用于被动语态：be dismayed at/by
- Also used as a noun: to one's dismay — 也可作名词：to one's dismay

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- be dismayed at
- be dismayed by
- be dismayed to learn/find
- greatly dismayed
- deeply dismayed
- much to my dismay
- express dismay
- feel dismay
- fill with dismay
- cause dismay

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……感到惊愕
- 被……吓到
- 惊愕地发现
- 大为惊愕
- 深感沮丧
- 令我大为失望的是
- 表示沮丧
- 感到沮丧
- 充满沮丧
- 引起惊慌
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dismay at the result
- dismay by the lack of progress
- dismay over the decision
- dismay among the staff
- dismay in the face of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对结果感到沮丧
- 对缺乏进展感到沮丧
- 对决定感到不安
- 员工中感到不安
- 面对……时感到沮丧
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: To the dismay of many parents, the school decided to cancel the sports festival. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The researchers were dismayed to find that their experiment had produced no significant results. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Much to the students' dismay, the exam turned out to be much more difficult than expected. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: Environmentalists expressed dismay at the government's decision to approve the new oil drilling project. (Nature 2022)
- Common: She was dismayed by the news that her flight had been cancelled.
- 介词搭配: The team expressed dismay at the lack of support from upper management.
- 介词搭配: There was widespread dismay among the employees over the sudden layoffs.
- 介词搭配: He was dismayed by the realization that he had lost his passport.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 令许多家长沮丧的是，学校决定取消运动会。
- 研究人员沮丧地发现他们的实验没有产生显著的结果。
- 令学生们大为沮丧的是，考试比预期的难得多。
- 环保人士对政府批准新石油钻探项目的决定表示失望。
- 她得知航班取消的消息后感到沮丧。
- 团队对高层管理缺乏支持表示沮丧。
- 员工中对突然裁员普遍感到不安。
- 他发现自己丢了护照后感到惊慌。
</details>

### 100. dismiss <a id="100-dismiss"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dimissus, past participle of dimittere ('to send away, dismiss'), from dis- ('away, apart') + mittere ('to send').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dimissus，dimittere（'送走，解散'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'离开，分开'）+ mittere（'送'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to officially remove someone from a job; to decide that something is not important; to allow someone to leave
- CN: 解雇；驳回，不予理会；解散，遣散

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Dismiss sb from a job' = to fire — 'Dismiss sb from a job' = 解雇
- 'Dismiss an idea/claim/suggestion' = to reject — 'Dismiss an idea/claim/suggestion' = 驳回/不予考虑
- 'Dismiss the class/court' = to allow to leave — 'Dismiss the class/court' = 解散
- Related noun: dismissal — 相关名词：dismissal

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dismiss an idea
- dismiss a suggestion
- dismiss a claim
- dismiss concerns
- dismiss doubts
- dismiss the possibility
- dismiss a case
- dismiss an employee
- dismiss the class
- summarily dismiss

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 驳回想法
- 无视建议
- 驳回主张
- 打消顾虑
- 消除疑虑
- 排除可能性
- 驳回案件
- 解雇员工
- 下课
- 立即解雇/驳回
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dismiss from a job
- dismiss as irrelevant
- dismiss by the court
- dismiss for misconduct
- dismiss with prejudice
- dismiss on the grounds of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从工作中解雇
- 以不相关为由驳回
- 由法院驳回
- 因行为不端解雇
- 有偏见地驳回
- 以……理由驳回
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The manager dismissed the suggestion as impractical without proper consideration. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The scientist's findings were initially dismissed by the academic community. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The judge dismissed the case due to lack of sufficient evidence. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The theory was dismissed as unsubstantiated until new evidence emerged decades later. (Nature 2023)
- Common: The teacher dismissed the class five minutes early.
- 介词搭配: He was dismissed from his position for violating company policy.
- 介词搭配: The suggestion was dismissed as irrelevant to the current discussion.
- 介词搭配: The employee was dismissed on the grounds of repeated absenteeism.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经理未经适当考虑就认为该建议不切实际而予以驳回。
- 这位科学家的发现最初被学术界驳回了。
- 法官因缺乏足够证据驳回了此案。
- 该理论因缺乏证据被斥为无稽之谈，直到几十年后新证据出现。
- 老师提前五分钟下课了。
- 他因违反公司政策而被解职。
- 该建议被认为与当前讨论无关而被驳回。
- 该员工因屡次旷工被解雇。
</details>

### 101. dispatch <a id="101-dispatch"></a>

**Etymology:** From Italian dispacciare ('to dispatch, send off'), from Spanish despachar ('to dispatch, expedite'), from des- ('away') + pact- ('to fasten'), from Latin pactum ('something fastened').

**词源：** 源自意大利语 dispacciare（'派遣，发送'），源自西班牙语 despachar（'派遣，加快'），源自 des-（'离开'）+ pact-（'系紧'），源自拉丁语 pactum（'系紧之物'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to send someone or something to a particular place for a particular purpose; to deal with a task quickly and efficiently
- CN: 派遣；发送；迅速处理，快速办妥

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal term for sending people (troops, messengers) or goods — 正式用语，派遣人或发送物品
- 'Dispatch a task' = to complete quickly — 'Dispatch a task' = 迅速完成
- Also a noun: dispatch (message/report) or with dispatch (speed) — 也作名词：dispatch（快讯/公文）或 with dispatch（迅速）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dispatch a message
- dispatch troops
- dispatch a team
- dispatch a letter
- dispatch goods
- dispatch a report
- dispatch a police unit
- dispatch quickly
- dispatch urgently
- dispatch immediately

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 发送消息
- 派遣军队
- 派遣团队
- 寄信
- 发货
- 发送报告
- 派遣警力
- 迅速派遣
- 紧急派遣
- 立即发送
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dispatch to the scene
- dispatch from headquarters
- dispatch by courier
- dispatch on a mission
- dispatch with haste
- dispatch via email

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 派遣到现场
- 从总部派遣
- 由快递发送
- 派遣执行任务
- 迅速派遣
- 通过电子邮件发送
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government dispatched emergency aid to the flood-affected region. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The company dispatched a team of engineers to investigate the technical failure. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The goods were dispatched from the warehouse within 24 hours of receiving the order. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The research vessel was dispatched to collect samples from the ocean floor. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Please dispatch the package as soon as possible.
- 介词搭配: Several ambulances were dispatched to the scene of the accident.
- 介词搭配: The documents were dispatched by express courier this morning.
- 介词搭配: A special envoy was dispatched on a diplomatic mission to the region.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府向洪灾地区派遣了紧急救援物资。
- 公司派遣了一个工程师团队调查技术故障。
- 货物在接到订单后24小时内从仓库发出。
- 科考船被派遣从海底采集样本。
- 请尽快寄出包裹。
- 几辆救护车被派往事故现场。
- 文件已于今早通过快递发出。
- 一名特使被派往该地区执行外交任务。
</details>

### 102. dispense <a id="102-dispense"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dispensare ('to weigh out, distribute, manage'), frequentative of dispendere ('to weigh out'), from dis- ('out') + pendere ('to weigh, pay').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dispensare（'称量，分发，管理'），dispendere（'称量'）的反复动词，源自 dis-（'出'）+ pendere（'称量，支付'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give out or distribute something; to prepare and give out medicine; to provide a service
- CN: 分发，分配；配药；提供（服务）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Dispense medicine' = to prepare and give out medication — 'Dispense medicine' = 配药
- 'Dispense with' = to do without; to get rid of — 'Dispense with' = 免除，摒弃；无须
- More formal than 'give out' or 'hand out' — 比 'give out' 或 'hand out' 更正式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dispense medicine
- dispense medication
- dispense advice
- dispense justice
- dispense information
- dispense cash
- dispense tickets
- dispense with formalities
- dispense with services
- dispense freely

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 配药
- 配发药物
- 提供建议
- 主持正义
- 提供信息
- 出钞
- 发票
- 免去礼节
- 免去服务
- 免费分发
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dispense to patients
- dispense at the pharmacy
- dispense by a licensed pharmacist
- dispense through a vending machine
- dispense with a signature
- dispense under prescription

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 分发给患者
- 在药房配药
- 由持证药剂师配药
- 通过自动售货机出售
- 无需签名
- 凭处方配药
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The pharmacist is qualified to dispense prescription medications. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The new system dispenses with the need for paper-based record keeping. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ATM dispenses both local currency and US dollars. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The clinic dispenses free health advice to low-income families. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Let's dispense with the formalities and get straight to business.
- 介词搭配: Medication should only be dispensed by a qualified pharmacist.
- 介词搭配: The machine dispenses coffee at the touch of a button.
- 介词搭配: We can dispense with the preliminary checks this time.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 药物只应由合格的药剂师配发。

- 这次我们可以省去初步检查。
</details>

### 103. disperse <a id="103-disperse"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dispersus, past participle of dispersere ('to scatter, spread'), from dis- ('apart') + spargere ('to scatter, strew').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dispersus，dispersere（'分散，散布'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ spargere（'散开，撒播'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move apart and go in different directions; to spread over a wide area; to break up a group
- CN: 分散，散开；驱散；消散，散布

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Both active and passive: The crowd dispersed / Police dispersed the crowd — 既可用主动也可用被动
- Physical: people, clouds, smoke, seeds — 具体：人群、云、烟、种子
- Figurative: doubts, fears, attention — 比喻：疑虑、恐惧、注意力

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disperse the crowd
- disperse the protesters
- disperse the clouds
- disperse the fog
- disperse seeds
- disperse information
- disperse doubts
- widely dispersed
- evenly dispersed
- quickly disperse

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 驱散人群
- 驱散抗议者
- 驱散云层
- 驱散雾气
- 传播种子
- 传播信息
- 消除疑虑
- 广泛分布的
- 均匀分布的
- 迅速散开
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disperse throughout the city
- disperse across the region
- disperse into the air
- disperse over a wide area
- disperse by the wind
- disperse among the population

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 散布在整个城市
- 分散到整个地区
- 消散在空中
- 分散到大片区域
- 被风吹散
- 在人群中传播
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Police used tear gas to disperse the crowd of protesters. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The morning fog gradually dispersed as the sun rose higher. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Seeds are dispersed by the wind, allowing plants to colonize new areas. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The population of this species is widely dispersed across the continent. (Ecology 2022)
- Common: After the concert ended, the crowd slowly dispersed.
- 介词搭配: The smoke from the fire dispersed into the atmosphere.
- 介词搭配: The protesters were dispersed by police using water cannons.
- 介词搭配: Seeds of this plant are dispersed over a wide area by birds.
</details>

- 晨雾随着太阳升高而逐渐消散。
- 种子靠风传播，使植物能够开垦新的区域。
- 该物种的种群广泛分布于整个大陆。
- 音乐会结束后，人群慢慢散去了。
- 火灾的烟雾消散在大气中。
- 抗议者被警察用水炮驱散。
- 这种植物的种子靠鸟类散布到广大区域。
</details>

### 104. displace <a id="104-displace"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desplacer ('to displace, move'), from des- ('away, apart') + placer ('to place'), from place ('place'), from Latin platea ('courtyard, street').

**词源：** 源自古法语 desplacer（'移动，转移'），源自 des-（'离开，分开'）+ placer（'放置'），源自 place（'地方'），源自拉丁语 platea（'庭院，街道'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move something from its usual position; to force people to leave their homes; to take the place of something or someone
- CN: 移动，迫使离开原位；使背井离乡；取代，替代

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: displace an object — 具体：移动物体
- Social: displace people/populations — 社会：使人们流离失所
- Technological: displace workers/jobs — 技术：取代工人/工作
- Related noun: displacement — 相关名词：displacement

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- displace people
- displace populations
- displace workers
- displace jobs
- displace the original inhabitants
- displace water
- displace a bone
- displace an object
- forcibly displaced
- displaced by war

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使人们流离失所
- 使人口迁移
- 取代工人
- 取代工作岗位
- 驱逐原住民
- 排水/排开水量
- 使骨头错位
- 移动物体
- 被迫流离失所
- 因战争流离失所
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- displace from one's home
- displace by the flood
- displace from the market
- displace in favor of
- displace as a result of
- displace due to conflict

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从家中赶出
- 被洪水赶走
- 从市场中被取代
- 被……取代
- 因……而流离失所
- 因冲突而流离失所
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The construction of the dam displaced thousands of families from their homes. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: Automation is increasingly displacing workers in the manufacturing sector. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The earthquake displaced large populations along the coast. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: Climate change is expected to displace millions of people in the coming decades. (Nature 2023)
- Common: The new technology has displaced many traditional methods of production.
- 介词搭配: Many families were displaced from their homes by the devastating flood.
- 介词搭配: Print media has been largely displaced in favor of digital platforms.
- 介词搭配: Workers were displaced as a result of factory automation.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 许多家庭因毁灭性的洪水而被迫离开家园。
- 印刷媒体在很大程度上已被数字平台取代。
- 工人们因工厂自动化而失业。
</details>

### 105. display <a id="105-display"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desploier ('to unfold, display'), from Latin displicare ('to scatter, unfold'), from dis- ('apart') + plicare ('to fold').

**词源：** 源自古法语 desploier（'展开，展示'），源自拉丁语 displicare（'散开，展开'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ plicare（'折叠'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to put something in a place where people can see it easily; to show signs of a quality, feeling, or skill
- CN: 展示，陈列；显示，表现出（品质、情感、技能）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- For physical objects: display products/artworks — 具体物品：展示产品/艺术品
- For abstract qualities: display courage/talent/interest — 抽象品质：表现出勇气/才能/兴趣
- Also a noun: on display (exhibition) — 也作名词：on display（展览中）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- display products
- display artwork
- display information
- display results
- display talent
- display courage
- display interest
- display emotion
- display symptoms
- display on a screen

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 展示产品
- 展出艺术品
- 显示信息
- 显示结果
- 展示才能
- 表现出勇气
- 表现出兴趣
- 表露情感
- 表现出症状
- 在屏幕上显示
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- display in a museum
- display at an exhibition
- display on a shelf
- display for the public
- display to customers
- display with pride

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在展览会上展示
- 在架子上陈列
- 向公众展示
- 向顾客展示
- 自豪地展示
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The museum displays a collection of ancient artifacts from the Tang Dynasty. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The athlete displayed remarkable skill and determination during the competition. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The temperature is displayed on the digital screen of the thermostat. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study found that children who display early signs of empathy tend to have better social relationships. (Child Development 2022)
- Common: The store displays new arrivals in the front window.
- 介词搭配: The paintings will be displayed in the gallery for the next three months.
- 介词搭配: The company displayed its latest products at the trade fair.
- 介词搭配: The results are displayed on the screen in real time.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这位运动员在比赛中展现了非凡的技巧和决心。
- 温度显示在恒温器的数字屏幕上。
- 研究发现，早期表现出同理心的孩子往往拥有更好的社交关系。
- 商店在前窗展示新品。
- 这些画作将在画廊展出三个月。

- 结果实时显示在屏幕上。
</details>

### 106. dispose <a id="106-dispose"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French disposer ('to arrange, dispose'), from Latin disponere ('to arrange, set in order'), from dis- ('apart') + ponere ('to place, put').

**词源：** 源自古法语 disposer（'安排，处理'），源自拉丁语 disponere（'排列，安排'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ ponere（'放置'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to get rid of something or throw something away; to arrange or deal with something in a particular way
- CN: 处理，处置；丢掉，清除；安排，解决

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Dispose of' = to throw away or get rid of — 'Dispose of' = 扔掉，处理掉
- 'Be disposed to do sth' = be willing or likely — 'Be disposed to do sth' = 有意愿/倾向于
- 'Well/ill disposed toward' = having a good/bad opinion of — 'Well/ill disposed toward' = 对……有好感/恶感

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dispose of waste
- dispose of garbage
- dispose of evidence
- dispose of an asset
- dispose of property
- dispose of the body
- be disposed to
- well disposed
- ill disposed
- safely dispose

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理废物
- 处理垃圾
- 销毁证据
- 处置资产
- 处置财产
- 处理尸体
- 倾向于
- 有好感的
- 有恶感的
- 安全处理
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dispose of in a landfill
- dispose of by recycling
- dispose of through incineration
- dispose of at a facility
- dispose of according to regulations
- dispose of with care

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在垃圾填埋场处理
- 通过回收处理
- 通过焚烧处理
- 在设施处处理
- 按照规定处理
- 小心处理
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Households must dispose of hazardous waste properly to protect the environment. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The company disposed of its underperforming assets to improve its financial position. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Proper methods of disposing of electronic waste are becoming increasingly important. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study examines how different cultures dispose of their dead and the rituals involved. (Anthropology 2021)
- Common: Please dispose of your trash in the bins provided.
- 介词搭配: Chemical waste must be disposed of according to strict environmental regulations.
- 介词搭配: The old furniture was disposed of by donating it to charity.
- 介词搭配: Nuclear waste is disposed of at specially designed facilities.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 家庭必须妥善处理危险废物以保护环境。
- 公司处置了表现不佳的资产以改善财务状况。
- 正确处理电子废物的方法变得越来越重要。
- 该研究考察了不同文化如何处理死者及相关仪式。
- 请将垃圾扔进提供的垃圾桶中。
- 化学废物必须按照严格的环境法规处理。
- 旧家具通过捐赠给慈善机构处理掉了。
- 核废料在专门设计的设施中进行处理。
</details>

### 107. dispute <a id="107-dispute"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French desputer ('to dispute, argue'), from Latin disputare ('to discuss, argue, debate'), from dis- ('apart, in different directions') + putare ('to think, reckon, prune').

**词源：** 源自古法语 desputer（'辩论，争论'），源自拉丁语 disputare（'讨论，争论，辩论'），源自 dis-（'分开，不同方向'）+ putare（'思考，估计'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to argue or disagree strongly with something; to question the truth or validity of something
- CN: 争论，辩论；质疑，对……提出异议

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Dispute that/wh-' = to argue against a claim — 'Dispute that/wh-' = 质疑……的说法
- 'Dispute with sb over/about sth' = to argue — 'Dispute with sb over/about sth' = 与某人争论某事
- Also a noun: in dispute, beyond dispute — 也作名词：in dispute（有争议），beyond dispute（无可争议）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dispute a claim
- dispute a decision
- dispute the validity of
- dispute the accuracy of
- dispute the findings
- dispute the results
- dispute the conclusion
- hotly disputed
- widely disputed
- fiercely disputed

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 质疑说法
- 质疑决定
- 质疑……的有效性
- 质疑……的准确性
- 质疑研究结果
- 质疑结果
- 质疑结论
- 激烈争论的
- 广泛质疑的
- 激烈争议的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dispute with sb
- dispute over a matter
- dispute about the details
- dispute in court
- dispute before a committee
- dispute on the grounds of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人争论
- 为某事争论
- 争论细节
- 在法庭上争论
- 在委员会前争论
- 以……为由质疑
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: No one disputes the fact that the climate is changing; the question is how to respond. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The accuracy of the research findings has been widely disputed by other scientists. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ownership of the land was disputed between the two families for generations. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The causal relationship between the two variables remains hotly disputed in the literature. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The two neighbors are disputing over the property boundary line.
- 介词搭配: The validity of the contract was disputed in court.
- 介词搭配: They disputed with each other over the best way to solve the problem.
- 介词搭配: The inheritance was disputed among the relatives for years.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 没有人质疑气候变化这一事实；问题在于如何应对。
- 该研究结果的准确性受到其他科学家的广泛质疑。
- 该土地的所有权在这两个家族之间争议了数代。
- 这两个变量之间的因果关系在文献中仍存在激烈争议。
- 两个邻居正在就地产边界线争吵。
- 该合同的有效性在法庭上受到质疑。
- 他们互相争论解决问题的最佳方式。
- 遗产在亲属之间争议了多年。
</details>

### 108. disregard <a id="108-disregard"></a>

**Etymology:** From dis- ('not, opposite of') + regard ('to look at, consider'), from Old French regarder ('to look at, consider'), from re- ('back') + garder ('to guard, keep').

**词源：** 源自 dis-（'不，相反'）+ regard（'看，考虑'），源自古法语 regarder（'看，考虑'），源自 re-（'回'）+ garder（'守护，保持'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to pay no attention to something; to treat something as unimportant or unworthy of notice
- CN: 不理会，不顾；漠视，轻视

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal term, stronger than 'ignore' — 正式用语，比 'ignore' 语气更强
- Implies deliberate or willful neglect — 暗示故意或任性的忽视
- Also a noun: disregard for safety/rules — 也作名词：disregard for（对安全/规则的漠视）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disregard the rules
- disregard the law
- disregard safety
- disregard warnings
- disregard advice
- disregard instructions
- disregard the consequences
- disregard the fact that
- completely disregard
- willfully disregard

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 无视规则
- 藐视法律
- 忽视安全
- 忽视警告
- 无视建议
- 无视指示
- 不顾后果
- 无视……的事实
- 完全无视
- 故意无视
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disregard for safety
- disregard of the rules
- disregard in favor of
- disregard in the interest of
- disregard at one's peril

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对安全的漠视
- 对规则的漠视
- 为……而不顾
- 为了……的利益而不顾
- 忽视后果自负
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The driver was fined for disregarding traffic signals. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The company showed a blatant disregard for environmental regulations. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Some cultures tend to disregard individual achievements in favor of group harmony. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study criticizes the government's disregard of scientific evidence in policy-making. (Science 2022)
- Common: He completely disregarded my advice and did whatever he wanted.
- 介词搭配: The construction company showed a complete disregard for safety regulations.
- 介词搭配: The law was disregarded in favor of political expediency.
- 介词搭配: Such warnings should not be disregarded at one's peril.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该司机因无视交通信号而被罚款。
- 该公司公然漠视环境法规。
- 有些文化倾向于忽视个人成就，更注重群体和谐。
- 该研究批评政府在制定政策时无视科学证据。
- 他完全无视了我的建议，随心所欲。
- 这家建筑公司完全无视安全规定。
- 为了政治上的便利，该法律被置之不顾。
- 此类警告不可忽视，否则后果自负。
</details>

### 109. disrupt <a id="109-disrupt"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin disruptus, past participle of disrumpere ('to break apart, shatter'), from dis- ('apart') + rumpere ('to break').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 disruptus，disrumpere（'打碎，分裂'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ rumpere（'打破'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to interrupt or cause disorder in a process or activity; to radically change an industry or market
- CN: 扰乱，打断；颠覆（行业、市场）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Negative: disrupt a class/meeting/traffic — 贬义：扰乱课堂/会议/交通
- Positive: disrupt the market/industry (innovation) — 褒义：颠覆市场/行业（创新）
- Related noun: disruption — 相关名词：disruption

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disrupt a class
- disrupt a meeting
- disrupt traffic
- disrupt service
- disrupt communications
- disrupt supply chains
- disrupt the market
- disrupt the industry
- severely disrupt
- completely disrupt

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 扰乱课堂
- 扰乱会议
- 扰乱交通
- 中断服务
- 中断通信
- 扰乱供应链
- 颠覆市场
- 颠覆行业
- 严重扰乱
- 完全中断
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disrupt by noise
- disrupt due to weather
- disrupt for hours
- disrupt throughout the region
- disrupt on a large scale
- disrupt across industries

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被噪音打扰
- 因天气而中断
- 中断数小时
- 扰乱整个地区
- 大规模扰乱
- 跨行业颠覆
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The heavy snow disrupted transportation across the whole city. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: New technology has disrupted traditional business models in countless industries. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The volcanic ash cloud disrupted air travel across Europe for weeks. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The pandemic severely disrupted global supply chains and international trade. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Please don't disrupt the class by talking during the lecture.
- 介词搭配: Flights were disrupted for hours due to the snowstorm.
- 介词搭配: The startup aims to disrupt the traditional banking industry.
- 介词搭配: Communication networks were disrupted throughout the region after the earthquake.
</details>

- 请不要在课堂上讲话扰乱秩序。
- 暴风雪导致航班中断了数小时。
- 这家初创公司旨在颠覆传统银行业。
- 地震后整个地区的通信网络都中断了。
</details>

### 110. dissent <a id="110-dissent"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dissentire ('to differ in feeling, disagree'), from dis- ('apart, in different directions') + sentire ('to feel, think').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dissentire（'感觉不同，不同意'），源自 dis-（'分开，不同方向'）+ sentire（'感觉，思考'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have or express a different opinion from the majority; to disagree with an official or established view
- CN: 持异议，不同意；反对（官方或主流观点）

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal term, especially in political, legal, and religious contexts — 正式用语，尤用于政治、法律和宗教语境
- 'Dissent from' = to disagree with — 'Dissent from' = 不赞同
- Also a noun: dissent, dissenter — 也作名词：dissent（异议），dissenter（持异议者）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dissent from the view
- dissent from the opinion
- dissent from the decision
- dissent from the majority
- dissent openly
- dissent publicly
- dissent strongly
- right to dissent
- voice dissent
- political dissent

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 不同意观点
- 持不同意见
- 反对决定
- 不同意多数意见
- 公开表示异议
- 公然反对
- 强烈反对
- 持异议的权利
- 表达异议
- 政治异见
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dissent from the official position
- dissent on the grounds of
- dissent in favor of
- dissent against the policy
- dissent among the members

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 不赞同官方立场
- 以……理由反对
- 为支持……而反对
- 反对该政策
- 成员中有不同意见
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Several members of the committee dissented from the majority opinion. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Citizens have the right to dissent from government policies through peaceful means. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In a democracy, the right to dissent is considered a fundamental freedom. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The judge wrote a dissenting opinion arguing that the ruling set a dangerous precedent. (Harvard Law Review 2022)
- Common: I respectfully dissent from the committee's final decision.
- 介词搭配: Several board members dissented from the decision to merge with the competitor.
- 介词搭配: The proposal was passed despite strong dissent from the environmental group.
- 介词搭配: There was considerable dissent among the members over the new regulations.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该法官撰写了反对意见，认为该裁决开创了危险先例。
- 我谨对委员会的最后决定持保留意见。
- 几位董事反对与竞争对手合并的决定。
- 尽管环保团体强烈反对，该提案还是通过了。
- 成员们对新规定存在相当大的分歧。
</details>

### 111. dissolve <a id="111-dissolve"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dissolvere ('to loosen up, dissolve, destroy'), from dis- ('apart') + solvere ('to loosen, untie').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dissolvere（'松开，溶解，毁灭'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ solvere（'松开，解开'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become or cause something to become mixed with a liquid; to end or close officially; to disappear gradually
- CN: 溶解；解散，终止；消除，逐渐消失

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Chemical: dissolve in water/solution — 化学：溶解于水/溶液
- Formal: dissolve a partnership/marriage/parliament — 正式：解除合作关系/婚姻/解散议会
- Figurative: doubts dissolve, tension dissolves — 比喻：疑虑消除，紧张消散

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dissolve in water
- dissolve a substance
- dissolve sugar
- dissolve salt
- dissolve parliament
- dissolve a partnership
- dissolve a marriage
- dissolve an organization
- dissolve doubts
- dissolve gradually

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 溶于水
- 溶解物质
- 溶化糖
- 溶解盐
- 解散议会
- 解除合作关系
- 解除婚姻
- 解散组织
- 消除疑虑
- 逐渐消失
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dissolve in liquid
- dissolve into tears
- dissolve into laughter
- dissolve by heating
- dissolve with water
- dissolve under pressure

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 溶解于液体
- 泪流满面
- 大笑起来
- 加热溶解
- 用水溶解
- 在压力下解散
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Stir the mixture until the sugar completely dissolves. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The board voted to dissolve the company and distribute the assets. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many types of minerals dissolve slowly in water over thousands of years. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The experiment measured how quickly different compounds dissolve at various temperatures. (Chemistry 2022)
- Common: Her initial nervousness soon dissolved when she started speaking.
- 介词搭配: The salt dissolves in water, forming a saline solution.
- 介词搭配: The tension in the room dissolved into laughter at his joke.
- 介词搭配: The partnership was dissolved by mutual agreement.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 搅拌混合物直到糖完全溶解。
- 董事会投票决定解散公司并分配资产。
- 许多种矿物质在水中经数千年缓慢溶解。
- 该实验测量了不同化合物在不同温度下溶解的速度。
- 她一开始的紧张情绪在开始讲话后很快就消失了。
- 盐在水中溶解，形成盐溶液。
- 房间里的紧张气氛被他的笑话化解为笑声。
- 合作关系经双方同意而解除。
</details>

### 112. distill <a id="112-distill"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin distillare ('to trickle down, drip'), from de- ('down') + stillare ('to drip, drop'), from stilla ('a drop').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 distillare（'滴下，滴落'），源自 de-（'向下'）+ stillare（'滴下'），源自 stilla（'一滴'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to purify or extract a substance by heating and cooling; to extract the essential meaning or idea from something
- CN: 蒸馏；提取……的精华，提炼

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: distill water/alcohol/oil — 具体：蒸馏水/酒精/油
- Figurative: distill wisdom/knowledge from experience — 比喻：从经验中提炼智慧/知识
- UK spelling: distil — 英式拼写：distil

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distill water
- distill alcohol
- distill whiskey
- distill crude oil
- distill the essence of
- distill wisdom from
- distill knowledge
- distill information
- carefully distill
- distill pure

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 蒸馏水
- 蒸馏酒精
- 蒸馏威士忌

- 从……中提炼智慧
- 提炼知识
- 提炼信息
- 精心提炼
- 提纯
</details>

- distill from plants
- distill through a process
- distill into a pure form
- distill at high temperature
- distill under pressure
- distill by evaporation

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过一个过程提炼

- 在压力下蒸馏
- 通过蒸发蒸馏
</details>

- TOEFL/IELTS: Seawater can be distilled to produce fresh drinking water. (TOEFL TPO 47)

- 介词搭配: Alcohol is distilled through a process of heating and condensation.
- 介词搭配: The report distills years of research into a few key recommendations.
</details>

- 精油是从桉树叶中蒸馏提取的。

### 113. distinguish <a id="113-distinguish"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French distinguer, from Latin distinguere ('to separate between, distinguish, mark off'), from dis- ('apart') + stinguere ('to prick, mark, extinguish').

**词源：** 源自古法语 distinguer，源自拉丁语 distinguere（'区分，辨别，标记'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ stinguere（'刺，标记，熄灭'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to recognize or show the difference between people or things; to be a characteristic that makes someone or something different
- CN: 区分，辨别；使有别于，是……的特征

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Distinguish between A and B' = to tell the difference — 'Distinguish between A and B' = 区分 A 和 B
- 'Distinguish A from B' = to be the difference — 'Distinguish A from B' = 使 A 有别于 B
- 'Distinguish oneself' = to do something very well — 'Distinguish oneself' = 使出众，使扬名
- Related adjective: distinctive, distinct — 相关形容词：distinctive, distinct

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distinguish between
- distinguish from
- distinguish oneself
- distinguish the differences
- distinguish the features
- distinguish clearly
- distinguish easily
- hard to distinguish
- distinguish right from wrong
- become distinguished for

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分……之间
- 与……区分
- 使出众
- 区分差异
- 辨别特征
- 清楚区分
- 容易区分
- 难以区分
- 区分对错
- 因……而闻名
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- distinguish between two options
- distinguish from similar products
- distinguish by its quality
- distinguish through hard work
- distinguish on the basis of
- distinguish among the candidates

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 区分两个选项
- 与类似产品区分
- 以其质量著称
- 通过努力而出众
- 基于……区分
- 在候选人中区分
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is sometimes difficult to distinguish between genuine antique furniture and reproductions. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: What distinguishes successful entrepreneurs is their ability to take calculated risks. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ability to distinguish colors varies significantly among different animal species. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The research distinguishes three distinct types of immune response to the virus. (Cell 2022)
- Common: The twins look so similar that it's hard to distinguish one from the other.
- 介词搭配: The two species are distinguished by the shape of their beaks.
- 介词搭配: She distinguished herself as a leader through her decisive actions.
- 介词搭配: The company distinguishes itself from competitors by its commitment to sustainability.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 有时很难区分真正的古董家具和仿制品。
- 使成功创业者与众不同的是他们承担计算风险的能力。
- 辨别颜色的能力在不同动物物种之间差异很大。
- 该研究区分了三种不同类型的病毒免疫反应。
- 这对双胞胎长得太像了，很难区分谁是谁。
- 这两个物种以其喙的形状来区分。
- 她以果断的行动使自己脱颖而出成为领袖。
- 该公司以其对可持续发展的承诺而区别于竞争对手。
</details>

### 114. distort <a id="114-distort"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin distortus, past participle of distorquere ('to twist different ways, distort'), from dis- ('apart, away') + torquere ('to twist').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 distortus，distorquere（'扭歪，使变形'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ torquere（'扭曲'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to twist or change the shape, sound, or appearance of something; to change facts or ideas to give a false impression
- CN: 使变形，扭曲；歪曲，曲解（事实、想法）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: distort the shape/face/sound — 具体：使形状/脸/声音变形
- Figurative: distort the truth/facts/meaning — 比喻：歪曲真相/事实/意思
- Related noun: distortion — 相关名词：distortion

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distort the truth
- distort facts
- distort reality
- distort the meaning of
- distort the image
- distort the sound
- distort one's face
- distort the data
- deliberately distort
- grossly distort

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 歪曲真相
- 歪曲事实
- 扭曲现实
- 曲解……的意思
- 使图像变形
- 使声音失真
- 使脸部扭曲
- 篡改数据
- 故意歪曲
- 严重歪曲
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- distort by media
- distort for political gain
- distort through editing
- distort in the press
- distort out of proportion
- distort beyond recognition

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被媒体歪曲
- 为政治利益歪曲
- 通过剪辑扭曲
- 在媒体中被歪曲
- 夸大歪曲
- 扭曲到无法辨认
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Some news reports deliberately distort the facts to attract readers. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The politician accused the media of distorting his comments about immigration. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The lens distorts the image at the edges, creating a fish-eye effect. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study warns that economic models can distort reality when they rely on oversimplified assumptions. (Nature 2022)
- Common: His face was distorted with anger when he heard the news.
- 介词搭配: The statistics were deliberately distorted for political purposes.
- 介词搭配: Her voice was distorted by the poor phone connection.
- 介词搭配: The metal bars were distorted beyond recognition by the intense heat of the fire.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这位政治家指责媒体歪曲了他关于移民问题的言论。
- 该镜头使图像边缘变形，产生鱼眼效果。
- 该研究警告说，经济模型如果依赖过于简化的假设，可能会扭曲现实。
- 他听到这个消息后，愤怒得脸都扭曲了。
- 这些统计数据被故意歪曲以达到政治目的。
- 她的声音因电话信号不好而失真。
- 金属棒被大火的高温烧得面目全非。
</details>

### 115. distract <a id="115-distract"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin distractus, past participle of distrahere ('to draw apart, pull in different directions'), from dis- ('apart') + trahere ('to draw, pull').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 distractus，distrahere（'拉开，向不同方向拉'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ trahere（'拉，拖'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone stop paying attention to what they are doing; to take someone's attention away from something
- CN: 分散注意力；使分心

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- 'Distract sb from sth' = to divert attention — 'Distract sb from sth' = 分散某人对某事的注意力
- Often used as an adjective: distracted, distracting — 常作形容词：distracted（分心的），distracting（令人分心的）
- Related noun: distraction — 相关名词：distraction

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distract attention
- distract the audience
- distract the opponent
- distract oneself
- easily distracted
- slightly distracted
- constantly distracted
- distract from the main issue
- distract from the goal
- temporarily distract

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分散注意力
- 分散听众注意力
- 分散对手注意力
- 分散自己的注意力
- 容易分心
- 稍微分心
- 不断分心
- 偏离主要问题
- 偏离目标
- 暂时分散
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- distract from work
- distract by noise
- distract with a game
- distract from one's studies
- distract during the exam
- distract through constant interruptions

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从工作中分心
- 被噪音打扰
- 用游戏分散注意力
- 影响学习

- 不断被打断而分心
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Students are easily distracted by their smartphones during class. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The constant noise from the construction site distracted the employees from their work. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Multitasking can actually distract you from completing any single task effectively. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study found that open-plan offices can distract workers and reduce productivity. (Journal of Environmental Psychology 2022)
- Common: She tried to distract herself from anxiety by watching TV.
- 介词搭配: The loud music distracted the students from their studies.
- 介词搭配: He was easily distracted by any small movement in the room.
- 介词搭配: The government is trying to distract public attention from the real issues.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 研究发现，开放式办公室会让员工分心并降低生产力。
- 她试图通过看电视来分散焦虑情绪。
- 吵闹的音乐使学生无法专心学习。
- 他很容易被房间里任何细微的动作分心。
- 政府正试图转移公众对真正问题的注意力。
</details>

### 116. distress <a id="116-distress"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French destrece ('distress, misery, straits'), from Latin districtus ('pulled apart, hindered'), past participle of distringere ('to draw apart, hinder'), from dis- ('apart') + stringere ('to draw tight, press').

**词源：** 源自古法语 destrece（'痛苦，苦难，困境'），源自拉丁语 districtus（'被拉开的，受阻的'），distringere（'拉开，阻碍'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ stringere（'拉紧，压迫'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone to feel extremely worried, unhappy, or in pain; to cause difficulty or trouble
- CN: 使痛苦，使忧虑；使陷入困境

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive: be distressed by/at/about — 常用于被动：be distressed by/at/about
- Stronger emotional impact than 'upset' — 情感冲击比 'upset' 强
- Also a noun: emotional/financial distress — 也作名词：emotional/financial distress

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distress sb
- deeply distressed
- visibly distressed
- emotionally distressed
- distressed by the news
- distressed at the situation
- cause distress
- financial distress
- respiratory distress
- in distress

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人痛苦
- 深感痛苦的
- 明显痛苦的
- 情绪痛苦的
- 因消息而痛苦
- 对情况感到痛苦
- 造成痛苦
- 财务困境
- 呼吸窘迫
- 处于困境中
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- distress at the loss
- distress over the decision
- distress by the conditions
- distress about the future
- distress among the population
- distress in the community

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因失去而痛苦
- 对决定感到忧虑
- 因条件而痛苦
- 对未来忧虑
- 人群中的痛苦
- 社区中的困扰
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The news of the accident distressed the family deeply. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Many families were distressed by the economic downturn and loss of jobs. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The animal showed clear signs of distress when separated from its young. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study examines how chronic pain distresses patients and affects their quality of life. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Please don't distress yourself over such a small matter.
- 介词搭配: She was visibly distressed at the news of her friend's illness.
- 介词搭配: The population was distressed by the prolonged drought.
- 介词搭配: The company is in financial distress due to mounting debts.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

### 117. distribute <a id="117-distribute"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin distributus, past participle of distribuere ('to distribute, divide'), from dis- ('apart') + tribuere ('to assign, give, allot').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 distributus，distribuere（'分配，分发'）的过去分词，源自 dis-（'分开'）+ tribuere（'分配，给予'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give things to a group of people, especially in a planned way; to spread something over an area; to supply goods to stores
- CN: 分配，分发；散布，分布；配送，分销

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Give out: distribute food/aid/leaflets — 分发：食物/援助/传单
- Spread: distribute evenly/randomly across an area — 分布：均匀/随机分布在一个区域
- Commerce: distribute products to retailers — 商业：向零售商配送产品
- Related noun: distribution — 相关名词：distribution

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- distribute food
- distribute aid
- distribute leaflets
- distribute resources
- distribute wealth
- distribute power
- distribute products
- distribute tasks
- distribute evenly
- widely distributed

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>
