- 分发援助物资
- 分发传单
- 分配资源
- 分配财富
- 分配权力
- 分销产品
- 分配任务
- 均匀分布
- 广泛分布的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- distribute among the participants
- distribute to the poor
- distribute across the region
- distribute by a company
- distribute through channels
- distribute over a period of time

<details>

- 在一段时间内分配
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The organization distributes food and clothing to homeless people every winter. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The government must find a fairer way to distribute resources among different regions. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: This species of plant is widely distributed throughout tropical regions of the world. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study analyzes how economic benefits are distributed across different social classes. (Nature 2023)

- 介词搭配: Aid supplies were distributed among the refugees in the camp.

### 118. disturb <a id="118-disturb"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French destorber, from Latin disturbare ('to throw into confusion, disorder'), from dis- ('apart, in different directions') + turbare ('to throw into disorder'), from turba ('turmoil, crowd').

**词源：** 源自古法语 destorber，源自拉丁语 disturbare（'扰乱，使混乱'），源自 dis-（'分开，不同方向'）+ turbare（'扰乱'），源自 turba（'骚动，人群'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to interrupt someone when they are trying to work, sleep, or rest; to cause someone to feel worried or upset
- CN: 打扰，干扰；使不安，使心烦

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical interruption: disturb sleep/work/peace — 具体干扰：打扰睡眠/工作/安宁
- Emotional: be disturbed by the news — 情感：被消息困扰
- Less strong than 'distress' — 语气比 'distress' 轻
- Common signs: Do Not Disturb — 常见标识：请勿打扰

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- disturb sb
- disturb the peace
- disturb the balance
- disturb the order
- disturb the silence
- disturb one's sleep
- disturb one's concentration
- disturbing the class
- deeply disturbed
- slightly disturbed

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 打扰某人
- 扰乱安宁
- 打破平衡
- 扰乱秩序
- 打破寂静
- 打扰睡眠
- 分散注意力
- 扰乱课堂
- 深感不安的
- 稍感不安的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- disturb by noise
- disturb during work
- disturb in the middle of
- disturb at night
- disturb with questions
- disturb through interruptions

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被噪音打扰
- 工作时打扰
- 在……中途打扰
- 在晚上打扰
- 用问题打扰
- 通过打断打扰
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Please do not disturb the students while they are taking the exam. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The discovery of the ancient tomb disturbed the peaceful life of the small village. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The construction work disturbed the natural habitat of many bird species. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study found that light pollution significantly disturbs the migration patterns of birds. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I'm sorry to disturb you at such a late hour.
- 介词搭配: She was disturbed by the loud noise coming from the construction site.
- 介词搭配: The ecological balance was disturbed by the introduction of invasive species.
- 介词搭配: He was disturbed in the middle of his work by a phone call.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学生考试时请勿打扰。
- 古墓的发现打破了小村庄的宁静生活。
- 施工作业扰乱了许多鸟类的自然栖息地。
- 研究发现光污染严重干扰了鸟类的迁徙模式。
- 抱歉这么晚打扰您。
- 她被建筑工地传来的巨大噪音所困扰。
- 生态平衡因引入入侵物种而被打破。
- 他正在工作中被一个电话打断。
</details>

### 119. ditch <a id="119-ditch"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dīc ('ditch, dike, trench'), from Proto-Germanic *dīkaz ('ditch, dike'). As a verb, originally 'to dig a ditch,' later 'to abandon.'

**词源：** 源自古英语 dīc（'沟渠，排水沟'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dīkaz（'沟渠，堤坝'）。作动词原义为'挖沟'，后引申为'抛弃，丢弃'。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to get rid of or leave something or someone suddenly; to abandon a plan or idea; to land an aircraft on water in an emergency
- CN: 丢弃，抛弃；放弃（计划、想法）；水上迫降

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Informal: ditch sb = leave sb without intending to return — 非正式：ditch sb = 甩掉某人
- Informal: ditch a class/plan = skip or abandon — 非正式：ditch a class/plan = 逃课/放弃计划
- Aviation: ditch = to make an emergency landing on water — 航空：ditch = 水上紧急迫降

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ditch a plan
- ditch a project
- ditch a class
- ditch one's friends
- ditch the car
- ditch the old system
- ditch outdated ideas
- ditch a relationship
- ditch work
- deliberately ditch

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 放弃计划
- 放弃项目
- 逃课
- 甩掉朋友
- 丢弃汽车
- 抛弃旧系统
- 抛弃过时的想法
- 结束关系
- 旷工
- 故意抛弃
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ditch in favor of
- ditch for a newer model
- ditch in the river
- ditch off the coast
- ditch without warning
- ditch at the last minute

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 放弃……而选择
- 为更新型号而丢弃
- 丢弃在河里
- 在海岸附近迫降
- 未通知就抛弃
- 在最后一刻放弃
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company decided to ditch its outdated software and adopt a new system. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: Many young people are choosing to ditch traditional career paths and pursue their passions instead. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The pilot managed to ditch the plane safely in the ocean after the engine failed. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The research suggests that companies should ditch rigid hierarchies in favor of more flexible structures. (Harvard Business Review 2022)
- Common: We ditched the original plan and went to the beach instead.
- 介词搭配: The old system was ditched in favor of a more efficient one.
- 介词搭配: He ditched his old friends for a new crowd.
- 介词搭配: The aircraft was ditched off the coast of Japan.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 公司决定抛弃过时的软件，采用新系统。
- 许多年轻人选择放弃传统职业道路，转而追求自己的热情所在。
- 引擎故障后，飞行员成功地将飞机迫降在海洋中。
- 研究表明，公司应该摒弃僵化的等级制度，采用更灵活的结构。
- 我们放弃了原计划，改去了海滩。
- 旧系统已被弃用，取而代之的是更高效的版本。
- 他为了一群新朋友而抛弃了老朋友。
- 飞机在日本海岸附近进行了水上迫降。
</details>

### 120. dive <a id="120-dive"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dȳfan ('to dive, dip') and dūfan ('to dive, sink'), from Proto-Germanic *dūban ('to dive, dip'), from Proto-Indo-European *dʰeup- ('deep, hollow').

**词源：** 源自古英语 dȳfan（'潜水，浸入'）和 dūfan（'潜水，下沉'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dūban（'潜水，浸入'），源自原始印欧语 *dʰeup-（'深，凹'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to jump into water with your head and arms first; to go to a deeper level underwater; to move quickly downwards
- CN: 跳水；潜水；俯冲，猛冲

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: dive into water/pool/sea — 具体：跳入水中/泳池/海里
- Figurative: dive into a topic/subject — 比喻：深入研究话题
- Sports: dive for a ball (football/goalkeeper) — 体育：扑球（足球/守门员）
- Past tense: dived (UK) or dove (US) — 过去式：dived（英式）或 dove（美式）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dive into water
- dive into the pool
- dive into the sea
- dive deep
- dive for pearls
- dive for cover
- dive into a topic
- dive headfirst
- dive bomb
- take a dive

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 跳入水中
- 跳入泳池
- 潜水入海
- 深潜
- 潜水采珠
- 扑向掩体
- 深入研究话题
- 头朝下跳入
- 俯冲轰炸
- 假摔/急降
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dive into the ocean
- dive from a cliff
- dive off the boat
- dive under the water
- dive to the bottom
- dive for cover

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 跳入海洋
- 从悬崖跳水
- 从船上跳下水
- 潜入水下
- 潜到水底
- 扑向掩体
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The children dived into the swimming pool to escape the summer heat. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The stock price dove sharply after the company announced lower-than-expected earnings. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Experienced divers can dive to depths of over 40 meters without special equipment. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The research team dove into the data to uncover patterns that had previously gone unnoticed. (Science 2022)
- Common: She decided to dive into learning Chinese before her trip to Beijing.
- 介词搭配: The goalkeeper dived for the ball but missed it by inches.
- 介词搭配: He dived from the high board into the pool with perfect form.
- 介词搭配: The plane dove to avoid the mountain.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 孩子们跳进游泳池躲避夏日的炎热。
- 该公司公布的收益低于预期后，股价大幅下跌。
- 经验丰富的潜水员可以无需特殊设备潜到40米以下的深度。
- 研究团队深入数据中挖掘以前未被注意的模式。
- 她决定在北京之行前开始认真学习中文。
- 守门员扑向球，但差了几英寸没碰到。
- 他以完美的姿势从高台跳入泳池。
- 飞机俯冲以避开山峰。
</details>

### 121. diversify <a id="121-diversify"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French diversifier, from Medieval Latin diversificare ('to make different, diversify'), from Latin diversus ('different, diverse') + -ficare ('to make').

**词源：** 源自古法语 diversifier，源自中世纪拉丁语 diversificare（'使不同，使多样化'），源自拉丁语 diversus（'不同的'）+ -ficare（'使……'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to develop a wider range of products, interests, or investments; to become more varied or diverse
- CN: 使多样化，使不同；多元化发展

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Business: diversify products/markets/investments — 商业：产品/市场/投资多元化
- Ecology: diversify species/habitats — 生态：物种/栖息地多样化
- General: diversify one's skills/interests — 一般：发展多种技能/兴趣
- Related noun: diversification — 相关名词：diversification

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- diversify the economy
- diversify one's portfolio
- diversify products
- diversify into new markets
- diversify risk
- diversify income
- diversify the workforce
- diversify the curriculum
- diversify investments
- diversify supply chains

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使经济多元化
- 使投资组合多元化
- 使产品多元化
- 拓展新市场
- 分散风险
- 多元化收入
- 使员工队伍多元化
- 使课程多样化
- 多元化投资
- 使供应链多元化
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- diversify into new areas
- diversify beyond traditional markets
- diversify by investing in
- diversify through acquisition
- diversify across different sectors
- diversify over time

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 拓展新领域
- 拓展到传统市场之外
- 通过投资……实现多元化
- 通过收购实现多元化
- 跨不同行业多元化
- 随时间逐步多样化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many companies are trying to diversify their product range to attract more customers. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The country needs to diversify its economy away from its heavy reliance on oil exports. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Financial advisors recommend diversifying your investment portfolio to reduce risk. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study found that farms that diversify their crops are more resilient to climate change. (Nature 2023)
- Common: She decided to diversify her skills by learning a new language.
- 介词搭配: The company is diversifying into the renewable energy sector.
- 介词搭配: Investors should diversify across different asset classes.
- 介词搭配: The university aims to diversify its student body by recruiting internationally.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多公司正试图使产品线多样化以吸引更多客户。
- 该国需要使其经济多元化，减少对石油出口的严重依赖。
- 理财顾问建议分散投资组合以降低风险。
- 研究发现，作物多样化的农场对气候变化的适应能力更强。
- 她决定通过学习一门新语言来拓展自己的技能。
- 该公司正向可再生能源领域扩展。
- 投资者应该在不同资产类别之间分散投资。
- 该大学旨在通过国际招生来实现学生群体的多元化。

### 122. divert <a id="122-divert"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin divertere ('to turn aside, go in different directions'), from dis- ('apart, aside') + vertere ('to turn').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 divertere（'转向，转向不同方向'），源自 dis-（'分开，旁边'）+ vertere（'转动'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to change the direction of something; to use something for a different purpose; to entertain or amuse
- CN: 使转向，使绕行；转移（注意力、资金等）；使消遣，娱乐

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: divert traffic/river/flight — 具体：疏导交通/使河流改道/航班改航
- Abstract: divert attention/funds/resources — 抽象：转移注意力/资金/资源
- Formal: divert sb = to entertain — 正式：divert sb = 使消遣，娱乐

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- divert traffic
- divert a river
- divert a flight
- divert attention
- divert funds
- divert resources
- divert suspicion
- divert the course of
- divert calls
- divert oneself

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 疏导交通
- 使河流改道
- 改变航班航线
- 转移注意力
- 转移资金
- 转移资源
- 转移怀疑
- 改变……的方向
- 转接电话
- 消遣，自娱自乐
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- divert from the highway
- divert to another airport
- divert into a new channel
- divert away from the main issue
- divert towards a project
- divert through a detour

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从高速公路分流
- 改航到另一个机场
- 引入新渠道
- 偏离主要问题
- 投向一个项目
- 绕道而行
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The road was closed for repairs, and traffic was diverted to alternative routes. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The government was accused of diverting public funds away from education. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The flight was diverted to a nearby airport due to severe weather conditions. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study examines how social media diverts attention away from important social issues. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The children were easily diverted by the clown's antics.
- 介词搭配: Water was diverted from the river into irrigation canals.
- 介词搭配: The police diverted traffic away from the accident scene.
- 介词搭配: Resources were diverted towards the healthcare system during the pandemic.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该研究考察了社交媒体如何将注意力从重要的社会问题上转移开。
- 孩子们很容易被小丑的滑稽动作逗乐。
- 水从河流引入灌溉渠。
- 警察疏导车辆远离事故现场。
- 疫情期间资源被调往医疗保健系统。
</details>

### 123. divide <a id="123-divide"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dividere ('to force apart, split, distribute'), from dis- ('apart') + -videre ('to separate'), related to vidua ('widow, separated').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dividere（'分开，分裂，分配'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ -videre（'分开'），与 vidua（'寡妇，分离的人'）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to separate into parts or groups; to cause disagreement or disunity; to calculate how many times one number contains another
- CN: 分开，分割；使产生分歧，使分裂；除，除以

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: divide a room/cake/land — 具体：分隔房间/蛋糕/土地
- Social: divide opinions/society/family — 社会：使意见分歧/社会分裂/家庭不和
- Mathematics: divide by 5 — 数学：除以5
- Related noun: division — 相关名词：division

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- divide into groups
- divide the work
- divide resources
- divide the profits
- divide a country
- divide opinion
- divide a number by
- divide equally
- divide fairly
- divide up

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分成小组
- 分工
- 分配资源
- 分配利润
- 分裂国家
- 使意见分歧
- 将数字除以
- 均分
- 公平分配
- 分割
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- divide among the members
- divide between two people
- divide into sections
- divide by a common factor
- divide along party lines
- divide on the issue

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在成员中分配
- 在两人之间分配
- 分成几个部分
- 除以公因数
- 按党派路线分裂
- 在这个问题上分歧
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The teacher divided the class into small groups for the discussion. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The issue of immigration has deeply divided the country. (考研英语 2022)

- Common: Let's divide the pizza equally among all four of us.
- 介词搭配: The estate was divided equally among the three children.
- 介词搭配: Opinion on the matter is divided along political lines.
- 介词搭配: The profits will be divided between the two partners.
</details>

</details>

### 124. divorce <a id="124-divorce"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French divorce, from Latin divortium ('separation, divorce'), from divertere ('to turn apart, separate'), from dis- ('apart') + vertere ('to turn').

**词源：** 源自古法语 divorce，源自拉丁语 divortium（'分离，离婚'），源自 divertere（'转向，分开'），源自 dis-（'分开'）+ vertere（'转动'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to legally end a marriage; to separate two things that are connected
- CN: 与……离婚；使分离，使脱离

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: divorce one's spouse = end marriage — 字面：与配偶离婚
- Figurative: divorce from reality/theory/practice — 比喻：脱离现实/理论/实践
- Often used in passive: be divorced from — 常用于被动：be divorced from

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- divorce one's husband/wife
- divorce sb
- get divorced
- file for divorce
- divorce proceedings
- divorce rate
- divorce from reality
- divorce from the facts
- divorce theory from practice
- bitterly divorced

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与丈夫/妻子离婚
- 与某人离婚
- 离婚了
- 申请离婚
- 离婚诉讼
- 离婚率
- 脱离现实
- 脱离事实
- 使理论与实践脱节
- 不愉快地离婚
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- divorce from one's spouse
- divorce on the grounds of
- divorce by mutual consent
- divorce after years of marriage
- divorce in court
- divorce for irreconcilable differences

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与配偶离婚
- 以……理由离婚
- 经双方同意离婚
- 结婚多年后离婚
- 在法庭上离婚
- 因不可调和的分歧离婚
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: They decided to divorce after being married for over twenty years. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Art cannot be divorced from the social and historical context in which it was created. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The divorce rate has been steadily increasing in many industrialized countries. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study argues that economic policy should not be divorced from ethical considerations. (Nature 2022)
- Common: He divorced his wife last year after months of separation.
- 介词搭配: They divorced on the grounds of irreconcilable differences.
- 介词搭配: Modern life is increasingly divorced from nature.
- 介词搭配: The couple divorced by mutual consent.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他们结婚二十多年后决定离婚。
- 艺术不能脱离其创作的社会和历史背景。

- 该研究认为，经济政策不应脱离伦理考量。
- 他在分居数月后于去年与妻子离婚。
- 他们因不可调和的分歧而离婚。
- 现代生活越来越脱离自然。
- 这对夫妇经双方同意离婚。
</details>

### 125. do <a id="125-do"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dōn ('to do, act, perform'), from Proto-Germanic *dōną ('to do'), from Proto-Indo-European *dʰeh₁- ('to put, place, do').

**词源：** 源自古英语 dōn（'做，行动，执行'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dōną（'做'），源自原始印欧语 *dʰeh₁-（'放置，做'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to perform an action or task; to carry out an activity; to work on something
- CN: 做，干，从事；执行，完成

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- One of the most versatile verbs in English; used as an auxiliary and main verb — 英语中最灵活的动词之一；可用作助动词和实义动词
- Used in questions, negatives, and emphasis — 用于疑问句、否定句和强调
- Countless collocations: do business, do homework, do a favor, do one's best — 无数搭配：做生意，做作业，帮个忙，尽力而为

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- do homework
- do business
- do a favor
- do one's best
- do research
- do a job
- do the dishes
- do the laundry
- do exercise
- do damage

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 做作业
- 做生意
- 帮个忙
- 尽力而为
- 做研究
- 做工作
- 洗碗
- 洗衣服
- 做运动
- 造成损害
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- do for a living
- do with care
- do without assistance
- do by oneself
- do at one's own pace
- do in a hurry

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为谋生而做
- 仔细做
- 独立完成
- 自己做
- 按自己的节奏做
- 匆忙地做
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The students are doing an experiment in the chemistry lab. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Small businesses have to do more with fewer resources to survive in a competitive market. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Doing regular exercise is essential for maintaining good health. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study does not support the claim that the treatment is universally effective. (The Lancet 2023)
- Common: I have a lot of work to do this weekend.
- 介词搭配: What do you do for a living?
- 介词搭配: You should do this task with great care.
- 介词搭配: We can do without the formalities this time.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学生们正在化学实验室里做实验。
- 小企业必须用更少的资源做更多的事，才能在竞争激烈的市场中生存。
- 经常锻炼对保持健康至关重要。
- 该研究不支持该疗法普遍有效的说法。
- 这周末我有很多工作要做。
- 你做什么工作？
- 你应该非常仔细地做这项任务。
- 这次我们可以免去礼节。
</details>

### 126. dock <a id="126-dock"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle Dutch docke ('dock, harbor'), from Proto-Germanic *dukkōn ('to duck, dive'). The verb sense developed from 'to bring a ship into dock.'

**词源：** 源自中古荷兰语 docke（'码头，港口'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dukkōn（'潜入，潜水'）。动词义从'使船入港'发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to bring a ship into a dock; to join a spacecraft with another in space; to deduct money from someone's wages
- CN: 使靠码头，使入船坞；对接（航天器）；扣减（工资）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Maritime: dock a ship/vessel/boat — 航海：使船靠码头
- Space: dock a spacecraft with the station — 航天：航天器与空间站对接
- Employment: dock sb's pay/wages — 雇佣：扣减某人的工资

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dock a ship
- dock a boat
- dock at the port
- dock with the space station
- dock a spacecraft
- dock one's pay
- dock wages
- dock the bonus
- safely dock
- temporarily dock

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使小船靠岸
- 停靠在港口
- 与空间站对接
- 对接航天器
- 扣减工资
- 扣工资
- 扣发奖金
- 安全靠岸
- 临时停靠
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dock at the harbor
- dock in the port
- dock along the pier
- dock with the ISS
- dock for repairs
- dock due to bad weather

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 停靠在港湾
- 停泊在港口
- 沿着码头停靠

- 因恶劣天气停靠
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: Cruise ships dock at the island's main harbor to let passengers explore the local attractions. (TOEFL TPO 46)

- 介词搭配: The ferry docks at the terminal every two hours.
- 介词搭配: The spaceship will dock with the orbital station tomorrow morning.
- 介词搭配: The boat docked along the pier to unload its cargo.
</details>

### 127. document <a id="127-document"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin documentum ('lesson, proof, example'), from docēre ('to teach, show'). The verb meaning 'to record or provide evidence' developed from the noun.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 documentum（'教训，证据，例子'），源自 docēre（'教导，展示'）。动词'记录，提供证据'之义从名词发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to record information in written, photographic, or video form; to support with evidence or documents
- CN: 记录，记载；用文件证明，为……提供依据

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Record: document an event/process/findings — 记录：记录事件/过程/发现
- Formal: carefully documented case/evidence — 正式：有完善文件记载的案件/证据
- Related noun: documentation — 相关名词：documentation

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- document a case
- document the process
- document the history of
- document the incident
- document evidence
- document findings
- document changes
- document everything
- carefully documented
- well documented

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 记录案例
- 记录过程
- 记载……的历史
- 记录事件
- 记录证据
- 记录研究发现
- 记录变化
- 记录一切
- 仔细记录的
- 有充分记载的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- document in detail
- document by photographs
- document through interviews
- document for the record
- document throughout the project
- document with citations

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 详细记录
- 用照片记录
- 通过采访记录
- 为记录而记载
- 在整个项目期间记录
- 用引文证明
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The photographer documented the daily lives of people living in remote villages. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: Historians have documented many cases of cultural exchange along the Silk Road. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The research team documented the effects of climate change on coral reefs over a ten-year period. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study documents a significant decline in biodiversity across tropical regions. (Science 2023)
- Common: Make sure to document all your expenses for the reimbursement process.
- 介词搭配: The event was documented in detail by several independent journalists.
- 介词搭配: The restoration process was documented through a series of photographs.
- 介词搭配: The case is well documented in the medical literature.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 摄影师记录了偏远村庄居民的日常生活。
- 历史学家记载了丝绸之路上的许多文化交流案例。
- 研究团队记录了十年间气候变化对珊瑚礁的影响。
- 该研究记录了热带地区生物多样性的显著下降。
- 记得记录所有开支以便报销。
- 这一事件被几位独立记者详细记录下来。
- 修复过程通过一系列照片记录下来。
- 该病例在医学文献中有充分记载。
</details>

### 128. dodge <a id="128-dodge"></a>

**Etymology:** Of unknown origin; first recorded in the 16th century. Possibly from dialectal dodge ('to jog along, to move to and fro'), or from Old English *dycgan ('to bend, bow').

**词源：** 词源未知；最早记载于16世纪。可能来自方言 dodge（'慢跑，来回移动'），或源自古英语 *dycgan（'弯曲，弯腰'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to avoid something by moving quickly to one side; to deliberately avoid a duty, question, or difficult situation
- CN: 闪开，躲开；躲避，回避（责任、问题、困难）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: dodge a ball/bullet/car — 具体：躲开球/子弹/汽车
- Figurative: dodge a question/issue/responsibility — 比喻：回避问题/责任
- Noun: dodger (evader), e.g. tax dodger — 名词：dodger（逃避者），如 tax dodger（逃税者）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dodge a question
- dodge a bullet
- dodge a ball
- dodge traffic
- dodge a problem
- dodge responsibility
- dodge the issue
- dodge a duty
- dodge an obstacle
- dodge a crisis

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 回避问题
- 躲开子弹
- 躲球
- 躲避车辆
- 回避问题
- 逃避责任
- 回避问题
- 逃避职责
- 避开障碍
- 躲过危机
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dodge out of the way
- dodge behind a tree
- dodge through the crowd
- dodge around the corner
- dodge between cars
- dodge from side to side

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 闪到一边
- 躲到树后
- 在人群中穿行
- 躲到拐角
- 在车辆之间穿行
- 左右躲闪
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The driver had to swerve sharply to dodge a pedestrian who stepped into the road. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Politicians often try to dodge difficult questions during interviews. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The goalkeeper managed to dodge past several defenders and score a goal. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study suggests that companies sometimes dodge environmental regulations by moving operations overseas. (Nature 2022)
- Common: He's been dodging my calls all week because he owes me money.
- 介词搭配: She dodged behind a wall to avoid being seen.
- 介词搭配: The children dodged through the crowd chasing each other.
- 介词搭配: The driver dodged between cars to get to the front of the traffic.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 司机在车辆之间穿行以开到车流前面。

### 129. dominate <a id="129-dominate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin dominatus, past participle of dominari ('to rule, dominate, control'), from dominus ('lord, master'), from domus ('house').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 dominatus，dominari（'统治，支配，控制'）的过去分词，源自 dominus（'主人，主宰'），源自 domus（'房子'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have control or power over someone or something; to be the most important or noticeable feature of something
- CN: 支配，统治，控制；在……中占主导地位；高耸于

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Power: dominate a country/market/industry — 权力：统治国家/主导市场/行业
- Sports: dominate a game/competition/opponent — 体育：主宰比赛/对手
- Physical: dominate the skyline/landscape — 具体：高耸于天际线/景观之上
- Related noun: dominance; adjective: dominant — 相关名词：dominance；形容词：dominant

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dominate the market
- dominate the game
- dominate an industry
- dominate politics
- dominate the conversation
- dominate the discussion
- dominate the headlines
- dominate the field
- completely dominate
- totally dominate

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 主导市场
- 主宰比赛
- 主导行业
- 主导政治

- 主导讨论
- 占据头条
- 在该领域领先

- 完全主导
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dominate in the region

- dominate among competitors

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

</details>

<details open>

</details>

### 130. donate <a id="130-donate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin donatus, past participle of donare ('to give as a gift, bestow'), from donum ('gift'), from dare ('to give').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 donatus，donare（'赠送，赠与'）的过去分词，源自 donum（'礼物'），源自 dare（'给予'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give money, food, clothes, etc. to a person or organization, especially in order to help them; to give blood or organs for medical use
- CN: 捐赠，捐献（金钱、物品）；捐献（血液、器官）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Money/goods: donate to charity/relief efforts — 金钱/物品：捐赠给慈善机构/救援工作
- Medical: donate blood/organs/plasma — 医疗：献血/捐献器官/血浆
- Related noun: donation; donor — 相关名词：donation；donor（捐赠者）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- donate money
- donate food
- donate clothes
- donate blood
- donate organs
- donate to charity
- donate generously
- donate anonymously
- donate online
- donate for research

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 捐赠衣物
- 献血

- 慷慨捐赠
- 匿名捐赠

</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- donate to the poor

</details>

<details open>

### 131. doom <a id="131-doom"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dōm ('judgment, destiny'), from Proto-Germanic *dōmaz. The verb sense 'condemn to a terrible fate' emerged in the 16th century.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dōm（'审判，命运'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dōmaz。动词'注定遭厄运'之义于16世纪出现。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to condemn someone or something to a particular unpleasant outcome or fate
- CN: 注定，判定（尤指不好的命运或结局）；使……在劫难逃

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Usually used in passive: be doomed to sth / to do sth — 通常用于被动语态：be doomed to sth / to do sth
- Stronger than "destine" — implies an inescapable negative fate — 比 "destine" 语气更强，暗示无法逃脱的厄运
- Related noun: doom; adjective: doomed — 相关名词：doom；形容词：doomed

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- doom to fail
- doomed from the start
- doom a project
- be doomed to
- doom by fate
- doom to extinction
- doom to repeat
- doom by circumstances
- feel doomed

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 注定失败
- 从一开始就注定
- 注定使项目失败
- 注定要
- 被命运注定
- 注定灭绝
- 注定重蹈覆辙
- 因环境所迫
- 感到在劫难逃
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- doom to sth
- doom by fate
- be doomed from the start
- be doomed to failure/extinction

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 注定……
- 被命运注定
- 从一开始就注定
- 注定失败/灭绝
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The project was doomed from the start due to a lack of funding. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Without significant reform, the industry is doomed to decline. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many species are doomed to extinction if habitat destruction continues at the current rate. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study warns that coastal cities are doomed to face severe flooding by 2100. (Nature 2023)
- Common: If we keep ignoring the warnings, we are doomed to repeat the same mistakes.
- 介词搭配: The plan was doomed by a combination of poor management and bad timing.
- 介词搭配: The species is doomed to extinction without urgent conservation measures.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该项目由于缺乏资金从一开始就注定失败。
- 如果不进行重大改革，该行业注定要衰退。
- 如果栖息地破坏以目前的速度持续下去，许多物种注定要灭绝。
- 研究警告说，沿海城市到2100年将注定面临严重洪水。
- 如果我们继续忽视警告，就注定要重蹈覆辙。
- 由于管理不善加上时机不佳，该计划注定失败。
- 如不采取紧急保护措施，该物种注定要灭绝。
</details>

### 132. dose <a id="132-dose"></a>

**Etymology:** From French dose, from Late Latin dosis, from Greek dosis ('a giving, a dose'), from didonai ('to give').

**词源：** 源自法语 dose，来自晚期拉丁语 dosis，源自希腊语 dosis（'给予，剂量'），源自 didonai（'给予'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give someone a measured amount of a medicine or drug
- CN: 给……服药；给……一定剂量

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Usually used with "with": dose sb with sth — 通常与 "with" 连用：dose sb with sth
- Common in medical contexts — 常用于医疗语境
- Related noun: dosage (the amount prescribed) — 相关名词：dosage（处方用量）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dose sb with
- dose oneself with
- dose the patient
- dose with antibiotics
- dose according to weight
- dose at regular intervals
- dose by injection
- carefully dose

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 给某人服用
- 自行服药
- 给病人服药
- 使用抗生素治疗
- 按体重给药
- 定期给药
- 通过注射给药
- 仔细控制剂量
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dose sb with sth
- dose according to weight
- dose at intervals
- dose by injection
- dose in small amounts

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 给某人服用某药
- 按体重给药
- 每隔一段时间给药
- 通过注射给药
- 小剂量给药
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The nurse carefully dosed the patient with painkillers after the surgery. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Patients are dosed according to their body weight and medical condition. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The medication should be dosed at regular intervals to maintain effective concentration in the blood. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: Mice in the experimental group were dosed with the compound daily for four weeks. (Nature 2022)
- Common: Be careful not to overdose yourself when taking over-the-counter medicine.
- 介词搭配: The child was dosed with a sweet-tasting syrup to make it easier to swallow.
- 介词搭配: The drug is dosed by injection rather than orally.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 护士在手术后小心地给病人服用了止痛药。
- 根据患者的体重和病情确定用药剂量。
- 药物应定期服用以维持有效的血药浓度。
- 实验组的小鼠每天服用该化合物，持续四周。
- 服用非处方药时注意不要过量。
- 给孩子服用了甜味糖浆以便于吞咽。
- 该药物通过注射而非口服给药。
</details>

### 133. dot <a id="133-dot"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dott ('head of a boil, point'), from Proto-Germanic *duttaz. The verb meaning 'mark with a dot' dates from the 18th century.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dott（'疖子的头，点'），源自原始日耳曼语 *duttaz。动词'打点，点缀'之义可追溯至18世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to mark with a small round spot; to be scattered or placed here and there over an area
- CN: 打点于；星罗棋布于，遍布

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive: be dotted with — 常用于被动语态：be dotted with
- Metaphorical: dot the i's and cross the t's (pay attention to details) — 比喻：dot the i's and cross the t's（注重细节）
- Common in geographical descriptions — 常用于地理描述

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dot the i's and cross the t's
- dot the landscape
- be dotted with
- dot the countryside
- dot the page with
- dotted around
- dot along the coast
- dot the map

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 一丝不苟
- 点缀风景
- 布满，点缀着
- 遍布乡村
- 在页面上布满
- 散布在周围
- 沿海岸分布
- 星罗棋布
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dotted with sth
- dotted across/throughout
- dotted along the coast/road
- dotted around the area

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 点缀着某物
- 遍布，散布在
- 沿着海岸/道路分布
- 在区域附近散布
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The countryside is dotted with small villages and farms. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The report was well-researched but the author forgot to dot the i's and cross the t's on a few minor points. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The islands are dotted across the vast ocean, forming a natural archipelago. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The region is dotted with archaeological sites dating back thousands of years. (Science 2022)
- Common: Make sure to dot all the i's and cross all the t's before submitting the contract.
- 介词搭配: The field was dotted with yellow flowers after the spring rain.
- 介词搭配: Small cafes are dotted along the main street of the old town.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 乡村里散布着一些小村庄和农场。
- 这份报告研究充分，但作者在一些小细节上没有做到一丝不苟。
- 这些岛屿散布在广阔的海洋上，形成了一个天然群岛。
- 该地区遍布着数千年前的考古遗址。
- 提交合同前务必确保所有细节都准确无误。
- 春雨过后，田野里点缀着黄色的小花。
- 古镇的主街道上散布着小咖啡馆。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#toc-133-dot)

### 134. double <a id="134-double"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French dobler, from Latin duplare ('to double'), from duplex ('twofold'), from duo ('two') + plicare ('to fold').

**词源：** 源自古法语 dobler，源自拉丁语 duplare（'加倍'），源自 duplex（'两倍的'），源自 duo（'二'） + plicare（'折叠'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become twice as much or as many; to make something twice as much or as many
- CN: （使）加倍，翻倍

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: double the amount/size/price — 及物：使金额/尺寸/价格加倍
- Intransitive: prices/sales doubled — 不及物：价格/销售额翻倍
- Phrasal: double as (serve a second function), double over (bend forward) — 短语：double as（兼作），double over（弯腰）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- double the size
- double the amount
- double one's efforts
- double the price
- double in value
- double as
- double over
- double the capacity
- double the number

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使尺寸翻倍
- 使数量加倍
- 加倍努力
- 使价格翻倍
- 价值翻倍
- 兼作
- 弯腰
- 使容量翻倍
- 使数量翻倍
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- double in size/value
- double over with pain/laughter
- double as a sth
- double by/from/to

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 尺寸/价值翻倍
- 痛/笑得弯了腰
- 兼作某物
- 增加到/从……翻倍
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company's profits doubled in the last fiscal year. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The population of the city has doubled over the past two decades. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The sofa doubles as a bed, making it perfect for small apartments. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: Global energy demand is projected to double by 2050. (Nature 2023)
- Common: I nearly doubled over with laughter at his joke.
- 介词搭配: The number of students has doubled from 500 to 1000 in five years.
- 介词搭配: This room doubles as a home office during the day.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司利润在上一个财年翻了一番。
- 该市人口在过去二十年中翻了一番。
- 沙发兼作床用，非常适合小公寓。
- 全球能源需求预计到2050年将翻一番。
- 听了他的笑话，我差点笑弯了腰。
- 学生人数在五年内从500人翻倍到了1000人。
- 这个房间在白天兼作家庭办公室。
</details>

### 135. doubt <a id="135-doubt"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French doter ('to doubt, be uncertain'), from Latin dubitare ('to waver, hesitate, be uncertain'), from dubius ('wavering, uncertain').

**词源：** 源自古法语 doter（'怀疑，不确定'），源自拉丁语 dubitare（'摇摆不定，犹豫，不确定'），源自 dubius（'摇摆的，不确定的'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to feel uncertain about something; to not believe or trust something
- CN: 怀疑，不确定；不信任，不信

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Doubting something = not being sure — doubt sth 表示不确定
- "I doubt that" = I think it is unlikely — "I doubt that" = 我认为不太可能
- Be careful: "I doubt if/whether" is common in questions of uncertainty — 注意："I doubt if/whether" 常用于不确定的疑问
- Related noun: doubt; adjective: doubtful — 相关名词：doubt；形容词：doubtful

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- doubt sb
- doubt the truth of
- doubt one's ability
- doubt the validity of
- seriously doubt
- never doubt
- doubt that
- doubt whether/if
- beyond doubt

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 怀疑某人
- 怀疑……的真实性
- 怀疑自己的能力
- 怀疑……的有效性
- 严重怀疑
- 从不怀疑
- 怀疑……
- 怀疑是否……
- 毫无疑问
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- doubt about/as to
- doubt by sb
- beyond doubt
- without doubt

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……怀疑
- 被某人怀疑
- 毫无疑问
- 无疑
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many experts doubt that the plan will be implemented on time. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: I doubt whether the government can meet its targets without additional funding. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Some researchers doubt the validity of the study's conclusions due to methodological flaws. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The findings have been doubted by several independent research groups. (Nature 2021)
- Common: I never doubted your ability to succeed.
- 介词搭配: There is no doubt about her qualifications for the position.
- 介词搭配: His honesty has never been doubted by anyone who knows him.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多专家怀疑该计划能否按时实施。
- 我怀疑政府在没有额外资金的情况下能否实现目标。
- 一些研究人员因方法缺陷而质疑该研究结论的有效性。
- 这些发现受到了几个独立研究小组的质疑。
- 我从未怀疑过你取得成功的能力。
- 她的任职资格毋庸置疑。
- 任何认识他的人都从未怀疑过他的诚实。
</details>

### 136. downgrade <a id="136-downgrade"></a>

**Etymology:** Formed from down + grade (verb), modeled on upgrade. First recorded in the mid-20th century in business and computing contexts.

**词源：** 由 down + grade 构成，仿照 upgrade 形成。最早于20世纪中期记录于商业和计算机领域。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to reduce something to a lower level, rank, or standard
- CN: 降级；降低；贬低

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Job: downgrade a position/employee — 工作：降低职位/降级员工
- Finance: downgrade a credit rating/forecast — 金融：下调信用评级/预测
- Opposite of upgrade — 是 upgrade 的反义词
- Related noun: downgrade — 相关名词：downgrade

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- downgrade a job
- downgrade a rating
- downgrade a forecast
- downgrade the importance of
- downgrade one's status
- downgrade from
- downgrade to
- significantly downgrade

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 降级职位
- 下调评级
- 下调预测
- 降低……的重要性
- 降低地位
- 从……降级
- 降级到……
- 大幅下调
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- downgrade from A to B
- downgrade by a notch/level
- downgrade to a lesser position

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从A降到B
- 降低一个等级
- 降级到较低职位
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company downgraded its profit forecast for the next quarter. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The credit rating agency downgraded the country's debt from stable to negative. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: After the security breach, the employee was downgraded to a less sensitive position. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The report downgrades the role of human activity in recent climate shifts. (Science 2022)
- Common: I had to downgrade my phone plan to save money.
- 介词搭配: The stock was downgraded from 'buy' to 'hold' by analysts.
- 介词搭配: He was downgraded to a junior position after the restructuring.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司下调了下一季度的利润预测。
- 信用评级机构将该国的主权债务评级从稳定下调至负面。
- 安全漏洞发生后，该员工被调至敏感度较低的职位。
- 该报告降低了人类活动在近期气候变化中的作用。
- 我不得不降低手机套餐以省钱。
- 该股票被分析师从'买入'降级为'持有'。
- 重组后他被降级为初级职位。
</details>

### 137. download <a id="137-download"></a>

**Etymology:** Formed from down + load, coined in the 1970s in computing contexts. Modeled on upload.

**词源：** 由 down + load 构成，于20世纪70年代在计算机领域创造，仿照 upload 形成。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to copy data or a program from one computer system to another, especially from the internet to a personal device
- CN: 下载（数据或程序）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Common in computing — 常用于计算机领域
- Transitive: download a file/app/song — 及物：下载文件/应用程序/歌曲
- Intransitive: the download is complete — 不及物：下载完成
- Opposite: upload — 反义词：upload（上传）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- download a file
- download an app
- download software
- download music
- download data
- download from
- download to
- download onto
- download via

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 下载文件
- 下载应用
- 下载软件
- 下载音乐
- 下载数据
- 从……下载
- 下载到……
- 下载到……上
- 通过……下载
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- download from a website/server
- download onto a device
- download to a folder
- download via a link

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从网站/服务器下载
- 下载到设备上
- 下载到文件夹
- 通过链接下载
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: You can download the application form from our official website. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: Users can download digital textbooks onto their tablets for offline reading. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The software can be downloaded free of charge from the university's online portal. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The dataset was downloaded from a public repository and analyzed using statistical methods. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I downloaded the latest episode of the podcast to listen to on my commute.
- 介词搭配: The file was downloaded from a secure server.
- 介词搭配: You can download the document directly onto your phone.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 你可以从我们官网下载申请表。
- 用户可以将电子教材下载到平板电脑上进行离线阅读。
- 该软件可从大学在线门户免费下载。
- 该数据集从公共存储库下载并使用统计方法进行分析。
- 我下载了最新一期的播客，在通勤路上听。
- 文件是从安全服务器下载的。
- 你可以将文档直接下载到手机上。
</details>

### 138. doze <a id="138-doze"></a>

**Etymology:** Of uncertain origin; possibly from Old English *dūsian ('to become dull'). First recorded in the 17th century. Related to dizzy.

**词源：** 词源不确定；可能源自古英语 *dūsian（'变迟钝'）。最早记载于17世纪。与 dizzy 有关联。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to sleep lightly for a short period of time
- CN: 打瞌睡，打盹儿

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Common phrasal verb: doze off = fall into a light sleep — 常见短语动词：doze off = 打瞌睡
- Often used in everyday contexts — 常用于日常语境
- Can also be a noun: have a doze — 也可作名词：have a doze（小睡一会儿）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- doze off
- doze in the sun
- doze peacefully
- doze through the movie
- doze lightly
- doze during the lecture
- doze after lunch
- doze in front of the TV

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 打瞌睡
- 在阳光下打盹
- 安静地打盹
- 看电影时打瞌睡
- 浅睡
- 上课时打瞌睡
- 午饭后打盹
- 在电视前打瞌睡
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- doze off during sth
- doze in/under/on
- doze through the film/class

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在做某事时打瞌睡
- 在……打盹
- 在电影/课上打瞌睡
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I dozed off during the movie and missed the ending. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The old man was dozing peacefully in his armchair by the fireplace. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many students doze off in long lectures, especially after lunch. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The study found that people who doze during the day may have higher cognitive flexibility. (Nature 2020)
- Common: The warm sun made me doze off on the beach.
- 介词搭配: She dozed through the entire two-hour meeting.
- 介词搭配: The cat was dozing in the sunlight on the windowsill.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我看电影时打瞌睡，错过了结尾。
- 老人正在壁炉旁的扶手椅里安静地打盹。
- 许多学生在冗长的讲座上打瞌睡，尤其是午饭后。
- 研究发现，白天打盹的人可能具有更高的认知灵活性。
- 温暖的阳光使我在海滩上打起了瞌睡。
- 她在整个两小时的会议中都在打瞌睡。
- 猫正在窗台上晒太阳打盹。
</details>

### 139. draft <a id="139-draft"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English draught, from Old English *dreaht, *dræht ('something drawn, a pull'), from dragan ('to draw, drag'). The verb 'prepare a preliminary version' dates from the 16th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 draught，源自古英语 *dreaht, *dræht（'被拉的东西，一拉'），源自 dragan（'拉，拖'）。动词'起草'之义可追溯至16世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to write a preliminary version of a document, plan, or letter; to select someone for a special duty
- CN: 起草，草拟；选派，征召

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Writing: draft a contract/letter/report — 写作：起草合同/信函/报告
- Sports/military: draft a player/soldier — 体育/军事：选拔球员/征召士兵
- Related noun: draft (preliminary version); draftee (person drafted) — 相关名词：draft（草稿）；draftee（被征召者）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- draft a document
- draft a contract
- draft a letter
- draft legislation
- draft a plan
- draft a proposal
- draft carefully
- draft for review
- draft a report

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 起草文件
- 起草合同
- 草拟信件
- 起草法规
- 起草计划
- 起草提案
- 认真起草
- 起草供审阅
- 撰写报告
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- draft by a committee
- draft for approval/review
- draft into the army
- draft on behalf of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 由委员会起草
- 起草供批准/审阅
- 征召入伍
- 代表……起草
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The committee drafted a new set of guidelines for the use of social media. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The lawyer carefully drafted the contract to protect her client's interests. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students were asked to draft an outline before writing the full essay. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The research team drafted a proposal for funding from the National Science Foundation. (Nature 2023)
- Common: I need to draft a reply to this email before I send it.
- 介词搭配: The agreement was drafted by a team of international lawyers.
- 介词搭配: He was drafted into the military right after college.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 委员会起草了一套新的社交媒体使用指南。
- 律师仔细起草了合同以保护客户的利益。
- 要求学生在写完整文章之前先起草一个大纲。
- 研究团队起草了一份向国家科学基金会申请资助的提案。
- 在发送这封邮件之前我需要先起草一个回复。
- 该协议由一组国际律师起草。
- 他大学刚毕业就被征召入伍了。
</details>

### 140. drag <a id="140-drag"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English draggen, from Old English dragan ('to draw, drag, pull'), from Proto-Germanic *draganą ('to draw, pull'). Related to draw.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 draggen，源自古英语 dragan（'拉，拖，拽'），源自原始日耳曼语 *draganą（'拉，拽'）。与 draw 有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to pull something or someone along with force, often with difficulty; to continue for too long
- CN: 拖，拉，拽；（使）拖延

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: drag a heavy box across the floor — 具体：在地板上拖重箱子
- Figurative: drag one's feet (delay doing sth) — 比喻：drag one's feet（拖延）
- Phrasal: drag on (continue tediously), drag out (prolong), drag sb into (involve unwillingly) — 短语：drag on（冗长地进行），drag out（拖延），drag sb into（把某人卷入）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drag sb along
- drag a heavy box
- drag one's feet
- drag through the mud
- drag the meeting on
- drag over
- drag across
- drag into
- drag out
- drag behind

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拖着某人走
- 拖重箱子
- 故意拖延
- 拖入泥潭/败坏名声
- 使会议拖延
- 拖过
- 拖过（表面）
- 把……拖入
- 拖延
- 落在后面
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drag sb into a situation
- drag on for hours/days
- drag out of
- drag through
- drag across

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 把某人拖入某种局面
- 拖延数小时/数天
- 从……拖出
- 拖过
- 拖过（表面）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The negotiations dragged on for months without any progress. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The politician was dragged into a corruption scandal that ended his career. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The children dragged their feet when asked to clean their rooms. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The legal dispute dragged out for years, costing both parties millions. (Nature 2022)
- Common: We dragged the heavy sofa across the room to make space.
- 介词搭配: She didn't want to be dragged into their argument.
- 介词搭配: The meeting dragged on for over three hours.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 谈判拖延了数月，毫无进展。
- 这位政客被卷入了一场腐败丑闻，结束了他的政治生涯。
- 被要求打扫房间时，孩子们故意磨蹭。
- 法律纠纷拖延了数年，双方都损失了数百万。
- 我们把沉重的沙发拖到房间的另一边腾出空间。
- 她不想被卷入他们的争吵。
- 会议拖延了三个多小时。
</details>

### 141. drain <a id="141-drain"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English drēahnian, drēhnian ('to drain, strain'), from Proto-Germanic *dreuhnōną. Related to dry.

**词源：** 源自古英语 drēahnian, drēhnian（'排水，过滤'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dreuhnōną。与 dry 有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to remove liquid from something; to make something empty or dry; to use up resources or energy
- CN: 排空，排干；耗尽（资源、精力）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: drain a pipe/sink/swamp — 具体：排空管道/水槽/沼泽
- Figurative: drain resources/energy/the budget — 比喻：耗尽资源/精力/预算
- Phrasal: drain away/off/out — 短语：drain away/off/out（流走/排走）
- Related noun: drainage — 相关名词：drainage（排水系统）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drain water
- drain the sink
- drain resources
- drain one's energy
- drain the budget
- drain away
- drain from
- drain into
- drain off
- drain of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 排水
- 排空水槽
- 耗尽资源
- 耗尽精力
- 耗尽预算
- 流走
- 从……流出
- 流入
- 排掉
- 耗尽……
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drain from the area
- drain into the river/sea
- drain of resources/energy
- drain away/off/out

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从该区域排出
- 流入河流/大海
- 耗尽资源/精力
- 流走/排掉/流出
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The constant stress drained all her energy and enthusiasm. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The war drained the country's financial resources, leading to economic crisis. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Wetlands help drain floodwater and prevent damage to nearby communities. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The project drained the research institute's budget for the next three years. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Please drain the pasta and rinse it with cold water.
- 介词搭配: The river drains into the Atlantic Ocean.
- 介词搭配: After the hike, I felt completely drained of all energy.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 持续的压力耗尽了她所有的精力和热情。
- 战争耗尽了国家的财政资源，导致经济危机。
- 湿地有助于排出洪水，防止附近社区受损。
- 该项目耗尽了该研究机构未来三年的预算。
- 请把意大利面的水沥干并用冷水冲洗。
- 这条河汇入大西洋。
- 徒步之后，我感觉精力完全耗尽了。
</details>

### 142. draw <a id="142-draw"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dragan ('to draw, drag, pull'), from Proto-Germanic *draganą ('to draw, pull'). Cognate with drag.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dragan（'拉，拖，拽'），源自原始日耳曼语 *draganą（'拉，拽'）。与 drag 同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to produce a picture or diagram; to pull or move something; to attract attention or interest; to reach a conclusion
- CN: 画，绘制；拉，拖；吸引；得出（结论）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Highly polysemous verb with many phrasal verbs — 多义词，具有大量短语动词
- Drawing: draw a picture/sketch/map — 绘画：画图/素描/地图
- Attracting: draw attention/interest/a crowd — 吸引：引起注意/兴趣/吸引人群
- Mental: draw a conclusion/distinction/comparison — 思维：得出结论/区分/比较
- Phrasal: draw up (create a document), draw on (use as a resource), draw back (retreat), draw out (prolong or elicit) — 短语：draw up（起草），draw on（利用），draw back（后退），draw out（延长或引出）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- draw a picture
- draw attention to
- draw a conclusion
- draw a map
- draw a crowd
- draw near
- draw back
- draw on
- draw up
- draw from

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 画画
- 引起对……的注意
- 得出结论
- 画地图
- 吸引人群
- 靠近
- 后退
- 利用
- 起草
- 从……中提取
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- draw from experience/sources
- draw on resources
- draw up a plan/contract
- draw attention to
- draw a comparison between
- draw near to

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从经验/来源中获取
- 利用资源
- 起草计划/合同
- 引起对……的注意
- 在……之间进行比较
- 靠近
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The artist drew a beautiful landscape of the countryside. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The report draws attention to the growing inequality in education. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The lecturer drew on years of research experience to explain the complex theory. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The study draws a clear distinction between correlation and causation. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The parade drew a large crowd of spectators.
- 介词搭配: We need to draw up a detailed contract before proceeding.
- 介词搭配: As the exam drew near, she became more anxious.
- 介词搭配: The author drew heavily from personal experience in writing the novel.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这位艺术家画了一幅美丽的乡村风景画。
- 该报告引起人们对教育领域日益加剧的不平等的关注。
- 讲师利用多年的研究经验来解释这个复杂的理论。
- 该研究明确区分了相关性和因果关系。
- 游行吸引了大批观众。
- 在继续之前，我们需要起草一份详细的合同。
- 随着考试临近，她变得越来越焦虑。
- 作者在写这部小说时大量借鉴了个人经历。
</details>

### 143. dread <a id="143-dread"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English ādrǣdan, ondrǣdan ('to fear, be afraid'), from Proto-Germanic *undrēdaną. Related to German fürchten ('to fear').

**词源：** 源自古英语 ādrǣdan, ondrǣdan（'害怕，恐惧'），源自原始日耳曼语 *undrēdaną。与德语 fürchten（'害怕'）有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to feel extremely anxious or frightened about something that is going to happen
- CN: 惧怕，担心；敬畏

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Common pattern: dread doing sth / dread sth — 常见句型：dread doing sth / dread sth
- Stronger than "worry" — expresses deeper fear — 比 "worry" 语气更强，表达更深层的恐惧
- Related noun: dread; adjective: dreadful — 相关名词：dread；形容词：dreadful
- Idiom: live in dread of — 习语：live in dread of（生活在……的恐惧中）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dread doing sth
- dread the thought of
- dread the exam
- dread going to
- fill with dread
- live in dread of
- dread that
- dread the prospect of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 害怕做某事
- 一想到……就害怕
- 害怕考试
- 害怕去……
- 充满恐惧
- 生活在……的恐惧中
- 担心……
- 害怕……的前景
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dread of doing sth
- dread at the thought of
- dread by all
- in dread of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 害怕做某事
- 一想到……就害怕
- 被所有人害怕
- 害怕……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She dreaded the thought of giving a speech in front of the whole school. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Many college graduates dread the job interview process. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students often dread the speaking section of the test more than any other part. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The researchers dreaded the possibility that their funding would be cut. (Nature 2021)
- Common: I dread going to the dentist every time I have a toothache.
- 介词搭配: He lived in constant dread of being discovered.
- 介词搭配: The soldiers dreaded the prospect of another long winter in the field.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她一想到要在全校师生面前演讲就害怕。
- 许多大学毕业生害怕求职面试的过程。

- 研究人员担心他们的研究经费可能被削减。
- 每次牙疼我都害怕去看牙医。
- 他时刻生活在害怕被发现的恐惧中。
- 士兵们害怕在野外再过一个漫长的冬天。
</details>

### 144. dream <a id="144-dream"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English dremen, from Old English drēman, drȳman ('to rejoice, sing, make music'), influenced by the noun dream from Old Norse draumr ('dream').

**词源：** 源自中古英语 dremen，源自古英语 drēman, drȳman（'欢欣，歌唱，奏乐'），受古诺尔斯语 draumr（'梦'）的名词 dream 影响。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have a series of thoughts, images, or emotions while sleeping; to imagine or hope for something desirable
- CN: 做梦；梦想，向往

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Sleeping: dream about/of sb/sth — 睡眠：梦见某人/某事
- Aspiration: dream of becoming / doing sth — 志向：梦想成为/做某事
- Phrasal: dream up (invent, imagine) — 短语：dream up（凭空想出，虚构）
- Related noun: dream; adjective: dreamy — 相关名词：dream；形容词：dreamy

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dream of/about
- dream that
- dream a dream
- dream big
- dream up
- dream of becoming
- dream about the future
- never dreamed that
- always dreamed of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 梦见；梦想
- 梦见……
- 做梦
- 有远大梦想
- 凭空想出
- 梦想成为
- 憧憬未来
- 从未想过
- 一直梦想
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dream of doing sth
- dream about sb/sth
- dream up a plan/scheme
- dream on (informal)

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 梦想做某事
- 梦见某人/某事
- 想出计划/方案
- 继续做梦吧（别妄想）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I dreamed that I was flying over a beautiful city. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Many young entrepreneurs dream of starting their own tech company. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: She dreamed of studying abroad ever since she was a child. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The team dreamed up an innovative solution to the energy storage problem. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I never dreamed that I would win the competition.
- 介词搭配: He dreamed of becoming a professional musician from a very young age.
- 介词搭配: Stop dreaming and focus on your current responsibilities.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她从小就梦想出国留学。
- 该团队想出了一个创新的能量储存解决方案。
- 我从没想过会赢得比赛。

### 145. dress <a id="145-dress"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French drecier, dreichier ('to arrange, set right'), from Latin dīrigere ('to direct, guide'). The meaning 'put on clothing' evolved in Middle English.

**词源：** 源自古法语 drecier, dreichier（'安排，整理'），源自拉丁语 dīrigere（'指导，引导'）。'穿上衣服'之义在中古英语中演变而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to put on clothes; to put clothes on someone else; to decorate or arrange; to prepare food for cooking
- CN: 穿衣服；给……穿衣；装饰；调制（食物）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Reflexive: dress oneself — 反身：自己穿衣服
- Other people: dress a child/baby/patient — 给别人穿衣：给小孩/婴儿/病人穿衣
- Medical: dress a wound (clean and bandage) — 医疗：包扎伤口
- Food: dress a salad (add dressing) — 烹饪：给沙拉调味
- Phrasal: dress up (wear formal or special clothes), dress down (wear casual clothes) — 短语：dress up（盛装），dress down（穿着随意）
- Related noun: dress, dressing — 相关名词：dress, dressing

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dress oneself
- dress a child
- dress a wound
- dress up
- dress down
- dress casually
- dress for dinner
- dress in
- dress as
- dress with style

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 自己穿衣服
- 给孩子穿衣
- 包扎伤口

### 146. drift <a id="146-drift"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English driften, from Old English drifan ('to drive'), from Proto-Germanic *drībaną ('to drive, push'). The sense 'move aimlessly' developed in the 17th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 driften，源自古英语 drifan（'驱动'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drībaną（'驱动，推'）。'漂流，漂泊'之义于17世纪发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move slowly, especially without control or direction; to change gradually from one state to another
- CN: 漂流，漂移；趋势，逐渐变化

**Part of Speech:** vi. & vt. (intransitive & transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: drift on water/air — 具体：在水上/空气中漂流
- Relationships: drift apart (become less close) — 关系：drift apart（逐渐疏远）
- Sleep: drift off (gradually fall asleep) — 睡眠：drift off（渐渐入睡）
- Phrasal: drift along (live without purpose), drift into (enter gradually) — 短语：drift along（随波逐流），drift into（逐渐进入）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drift apart
- drift away
- drift off to sleep
- drift through life
- drift into
- drift along
- drift toward
- drift from
- drift across
- drift with

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 逐渐疏远
- 漂走
- 渐渐入睡
- 浑浑噩噩地生活
- 逐渐进入
- 随波逐流
- 漂向
- 从……漂离
- 漂过
- 随……漂流
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drift from A to B
- drift into a habit/situation
- drift apart from sb
- drift off to sleep
- drift along the river

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从A漂到B
- 逐渐养成习惯/陷入某种局面
- 与某人逐渐疏远
- 渐渐入睡
- 沿河漂流
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: As they grew older, the two friends gradually drifted apart. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Many young people drift into big cities without a clear plan for their future. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The boat drifted aimlessly on the open sea for three days. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: Continental plates drift slowly across the Earth's surface over millions of years. (Nature 2023)
- Common: I drifted off to sleep while watching the movie.
- 介词搭配: The conversation drifted from politics to sports and then back again.
- 介词搭配: The snow drifted against the door, blocking the entrance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 随着年龄增长，这两个朋友逐渐疏远了。
- 许多年轻人对未来没有明确计划就盲目流入大城市。
- 小船在公海上漂流了三天。
- 大陆板块在数百万年间缓慢地漂移过地球表面。
- 我看电影时渐渐睡着了。
- 话题从政治转到体育，然后又转了回来。
- 雪在门边堆积起来，堵住了入口。
</details>

### 147. drill <a id="147-drill"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle Dutch drillen ('to bore, drill, turn'), from Proto-Germanic *þrillijaną. The military sense 'train by repetition' dates from the 18th century.

**词源：** 源自中古荷兰语 drillen（'钻孔，钻，转动'），源自原始日耳曼语 *þrillijaną。军事意义'反复训练'可追溯至18世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a hole in something using a tool; to train someone by repeating instructions; to perform a repetitive exercise
- CN: 钻（孔）；训练，操练；反复练习

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Construction: drill a hole into the wall — 建筑：在墙上钻孔
- Military/education: drill soldiers/students — 军事/教育：训练士兵/学生
- Sports: drill a movement/technique — 体育：反复练习动作/技巧
- Resource extraction: drill for oil/gas/water — 资源开采：钻井寻找石油/天然气/水

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drill a hole
- drill into
- drill through
- drill for oil
- drill soldiers
- drill a team
- drill on
- drill with precision
- drill in pronunciation

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 钻孔
- 钻入
- 钻穿
- 钻井找油
- 训练士兵
- 训练队伍
- 训练/操练
- 精确地钻
- 发音练习
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drill into a surface
- drill through metal/wood
- drill for oil/water
- drill sb in a subject

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 钻入表面
- 钻穿金属/木头
- 钻井找油/水
- 在某科目上训练某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: You need to drill a hole in the wall to hang the shelf. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The teacher drilled the students in English grammar every morning. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The team drilled the emergency evacuation procedure until it became second nature. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The company plans to drill for oil in the Arctic region despite environmental concerns. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The soldiers spent the morning drilling on the parade ground.
- 介词搭配: He drilled a small hole through the wall to pass the cable through.
- 介词搭配: The coach drilled the team in the new formation repeatedly.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他在墙上钻了一个小孔以便穿过电缆。
- 教练反复训练队伍掌握新的阵型。
</details>

### 148. drink <a id="148-drink"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English drincan ('to drink'), from Proto-Germanic *drinkaną. Cognate with German trinken, Dutch drinken.

**词源：** 源自古英语 drincan（'喝'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drinkaną。与德语 trinken、荷兰语 drinken 同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take a liquid into the mouth and swallow it; to consume alcohol
- CN: 喝，饮；喝酒

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: drink water/coffee/tea — 及物：喝水/咖啡/茶
- Intransitive: drink heavily (consume large amounts of alcohol) — 不及物：drink heavily（酗酒）
- Phrasal: drink to (toast), drink up (finish drinking), drink in (absorb eagerly) — 短语：drink to（为……干杯），drink up（喝完），drink in（陶醉于）
- Related noun: drink; adjective: drinkable — 相关名词：drink；形容词：drinkable

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drink water
- drink alcohol
- drink tea/coffee
- drink to
- drink up
- drink in
- drink heavily
- drink moderately
- drink from
- drink with friends

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 喝完
- 陶醉于
- 酗酒
- 适量饮酒
- 从……喝
- 与朋友喝酒
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drink from a glass/bottle

- drink through a straw

- 陶醉于美景/氛围
- 用吸管喝
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: They drank a toast to the newly married couple. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The study found that people who drink moderate amounts of red wine may have better heart health. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The subject drank 500 mL of the solution and was monitored for two hours. (Nature 2021)

- 介词搭配: Let's drink to the success of the project!
- 介词搭配: He drank his coffee through a straw because it was too hot.

### 149. drip <a id="149-drip"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English drippen, from Old English dryppan ('to drop, fall in drops'), from Proto-Germanic *drupjaną. Related to drop.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 drippen，源自古英语 dryppan（'滴下，滴落'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drupjaną。与 drop 有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to fall or let fall in small drops; to produce liquid in drops
- CN: （使）滴下；滴水

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: water drips from the tap — 不及物：水从水龙头滴下
- Transitive: drip paint onto the canvas — 及物：往画布上滴颜料
- Medical: intravenous drip — 医疗：静脉滴注
- Figurative: drip with sth (be full of a quality) — 比喻：drip with sth（充满某种特质）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drip from
- drip with
- drip down
- drip onto
- drip off
- drip into
- drip through
- drip slowly
- drip-dry

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……滴下
- 充满，滴着
- 滴下
- 滴到……上
- 滴落
- 滴入
- 滴穿
- 慢慢滴
- 滴干晾干
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drip from a surface
- drip with sweat/jewels/condescension
- drip down the wall
- drip onto the floor

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从表面滴下
- 滴着汗/珠光宝气/充满优越感
- 沿墙壁滴下
- 滴到地板上
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The tap is dripping — you need to fix it. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The cave ceiling dripped with water that had been seeping through the rock for centuries. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The patient was hooked up to an intravenous drip in the hospital. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The solution was allowed to drip slowly through the filter paper. (Nature 2022)
- Common: His forehead was dripping with sweat after the long run.
- 介词搭配: Paint dripped from the brush onto the floor.
- 介词搭配: The old faucet drips water continuously throughout the night.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

</details>

### 150. drive <a id="150-drive"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English drīfan ('to drive, push, force'), from Proto-Germanic *drībaną ('to drive, push'). Cognate with German treiben, Dutch drijven.

**词源：** 源自古英语 drīfan（'驱动，推动，迫使'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drībaną（'驱动，推'）。与德语 treiben、荷兰语 drijven 同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to operate a vehicle; to force someone or something to move; to provide the power for something; to motivate someone
- CN: 驾驶；驱赶；驱动；激发，促使

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Vehicle: drive a car/truck/bus — 车辆：驾驶汽车/卡车/公交车
- Psychological: drive sb crazy/mad/wild — 心理：逼疯某人/使某人发狂
- Business: drive growth/sales/innovation — 商业：推动增长/销售/创新
- Phrasal: drive away (repel), drive off (leave), drive through (pass through), drive at (imply) — 短语：drive away（驱赶），drive off（离开），drive through（驾车穿过），drive at（意指）
- Related noun: driver — 相关名词：driver（司机；驱动因素）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drive a car
- drive to work
- drive sb crazy/mad
- drive change
- drive growth
- drive home
- drive away
- drive through
- drive off
- drive by

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 开车
- 开车上班
- 把某人逼疯
- 推动变革
- 推动增长

- 驱赶；驾车离开
- 驾车穿过
- 驶离
- 开车经过
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drive sb to do sth
- drive along/across/through
- drive into the city/country

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 开车进城/下乡

- 小心驾驶
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: Technological innovation drives economic growth in the modern world. (TOEFL TPO 48)

- 介词搭配: He drove home the importance of teamwork in his final speech.

</details>

### 151. drop <a id="151-drop"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dropian ('to fall in drops'), from Proto-Germanic *drupōną ('to drop, fall'). Related to drip and droop.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dropian（'滴落'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drupōną（'滴落，落下'）。与 drip 和 droop 有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to fall or allow to fall; to become lower or weaker; to stop doing or discussing something
- CN: 落下，掉下；降低，下降；放弃，停止

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: drop a glass/pen/ball — 具体：掉下玻璃杯/笔/球
- Prices/temperature: prices/temperatures dropped — 价格/温度：价格/温度下降
- Phrasal: drop by/in (visit casually), drop out (withdraw from a course), drop off (fall asleep or deliver), drop behind (fall behind) — 短语：drop by/in（顺便拜访），drop out（退学），drop off（睡着或放下），drop behind（落后）
- Idiom: drop a hint (give a subtle clue) — 习语：drop a hint（暗示）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drop the ball
- drop a habit
- drop a course
- drop the price
- drop a hint
- drop a line
- drop by/in
- drop off
- drop out
- drop behind
- drop from

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 掉球；犯错
- 戒掉习惯
- 退课
- 降价
- 暗示
- 写信
- 顺便拜访
- 睡着；放下
- 退学；退出
- 落后
- 从……落下
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drop from sth
- drop onto a surface
- drop to the ground/floor
- drop out of school/society
- drop by the house/office

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……掉下
- 掉到表面上
- 掉到地上
- 退学/脱离社会
- 顺便到家里/办公室
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The price of electronic products has dropped significantly in recent years. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Many students drop out of college due to financial difficulties. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The temperature dropped below freezing overnight. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The study found that air pollution levels dropped dramatically during the lockdown. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I dropped by her office to say hello.
- 介词搭配: The book dropped from his hands as he fell asleep.
- 介词搭配: She dropped out of the competition due to an injury.
</details>

### 152. drown <a id="152-drown"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English drounen, probably from Old English druncnian ('to be swallowed up, drown'), related to drincan ('to drink').

**词源：** 源自中古英语 drounen，可能源自古英语 druncnian（'被吞没，淹死'），与 drincan（'喝'）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to die or cause to die by being underwater and unable to breathe; to make a sound impossible to hear by making a louder noise
- CN: 淹死，溺死；淹没（声音）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: drown in the river/ocean — 不及物：在河流/海洋中淹死
- Transitive: drown kittens/babies (intentionally kill) — 及物：淹死小猫/婴儿（故意杀死）
- Figurative: drown in debt/work — 比喻：负债累累/忙于工作
- Phrasal: drown out (overwhelm a sound) — 短语：drown out（淹没声音）
- Idiom: drown one's sorrows (drink alcohol to forget problems) — 习语：drown one's sorrows（借酒浇愁）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drown in
- drown out
- drown one's sorrows
- drown the sound
- drown in debt
- drown in work
- drown by
- drown beneath
- drown under

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 淹死在……
- 淹没（声音）
- 借酒浇愁
- 掩盖声音
- 债台高筑
- 忙于工作
- 被……淹死
- 淹没在……之下
- 在……之下淹没
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drown in the sea/river
- drown out the noise/music
- drown beneath the waves
- drown under the weight of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在海洋/河流中淹死
- 淹没噪音/音乐
- 淹没在波涛之下
- 在……的重压下淹没
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The loud music from the party drowned out our conversation. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Many small businesses are drowning in debt due to the economic downturn. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The lifeguard rescued the child who was drowning in the deep end of the pool. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The report warns that low-lying islands could drown beneath rising sea levels. (Nature 2023)
- Common: After a long week at work, he went to the bar to drown his sorrows.
- 介词搭配: The sound of the waterfall drowned out all other noise.
- 介词搭配: She felt like she was drowning in a sea of paperwork.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 派对上震耳的音乐淹没了我们的谈话声。
- 由于经济低迷，许多小企业债台高筑。
- 救生员救起了那个在游泳池深水区溺水的孩子。
- 报告警告说低洼岛屿可能会被上升的海平面淹没。
- 工作了一周后，他去酒吧借酒浇愁。
- 瀑布的水声淹没了所有其他声音。
- 她感觉自己被淹没在文山会海之中。
</details>

### 153. drug <a id="153-drug"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English drogge, from Old French drogue ('drug, medicine'), possibly from Middle Dutch droge vate ('dry barrels'). The verb 'administer a drug' dates from the late 16th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 drogge，源自古法语 drogue（'药物'），可能来自中古荷兰语 droge vate（'干桶'）。动词'用药麻醉'可追溯至16世纪末。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give someone a substance that affects their mind or body, especially in order to make them unconscious or to improve their performance
- CN: 给……服药；用药麻醉；在……中掺麻醉药

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Medical: drug a patient (for surgery) — 医疗：给病人用药（手术前）
- Criminal: drug sb's drink (put a drug in it) — 犯罪：在某人饮料中下药
- Sports: drug a horse (illegally) — 体育：给马下药（非法）
- Related noun: drug; adjective: drugged — 相关名词：drug；形容词：drugged（被下药的）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drug a patient
- drug sb
- drug a drink
- drug with sedatives
- be drugged
- heavily drugged
- drug by force
- drug without consent

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 给病人用药
- 给某人下药
- 在饮料中下药
- 用镇静剂麻醉
- 被下药
- 被大量用药
- 被迫服药
- 未经同意下药
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drug sb with sth
- drug sb into unconsciousness
- drug without consent
- drug for the purpose of

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某药给某人下药
- 把人麻醉昏迷
- 未经同意下药
- 为了……而下药
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The kidnappers drugged the victim before taking him away. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The athlete was disqualified after being found to have drugged himself before the race. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In some countries, it is illegal to drug animals for sporting purposes. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The study involved drugging the subjects with a mild sedative and monitoring brain activity. (Nature 2021)
- Common: Someone must have drugged my drink — I don't remember anything after the first sip.
- 介词搭配: The patient was drugged with a powerful anesthetic before the operation.
- 介词搭配: She was drugged into submission by her captors.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 绑架者在带走受害者之前给他下了药。
- 该运动员被发现赛前服用药物后被取消资格。
- 在一些国家，为体育目的给动物下药是非法的。
- 该研究包括给受试者服用温和镇静剂并监测大脑活动。
- 肯定有人在饮料里给我下了药——喝第一口之后的事我都不记得了。
- 病人在手术前被用强力麻醉剂麻醉。
- 她被劫持者用药弄得顺从。
</details>
