### 154. drum <a id="154-drum"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English drum(m)e, from Middle Dutch tromme ('drum'), from Proto-Germanic *trumbō. The verb meaning 'play the drum' or 'make rhythmic sounds' dates from the 16th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 drum(m)e，源自中古荷兰语 tromme（'鼓'），源自原始日耳曼语 *trumbō。动词'打鼓'或'发出有节奏的声音'可追溯至16世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to play a drum or make a rhythmic sound; to tap fingers repeatedly on a surface; to force something into someone's mind by repetition
- CN: 打鼓；敲击，有节奏地击打；反复灌输

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Music: drum a rhythm/beat — 音乐：敲击节奏/节拍
- Nervous habit: drum one's fingers on the table — 紧张习惯：用手指敲桌子
- Phrasal: drum up (promote, generate), drum into (teach by repetition) — 短语：drum up（招揽，争取），drum into（反复灌输）
- Related noun: drummer — 相关名词：drummer（鼓手）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- drum one's fingers
- drum on the table
- drum up support
- drum into
- drum a rhythm
- drum loudly
- drum for business
- drum out of

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 用手指敲击
- 敲桌子
- 争取支持
- 反复灌输
- 敲击节奏
- 大声敲击
- 招揽生意
- 逐出
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- drum on a surface
- drum into sb's head
- drum up business/support
- drum out of the army/school

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在表面上敲击
- 反复灌输给某人
- 招揽生意/争取支持
- 从军队/学校开除
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He drummed his fingers impatiently on the desk while waiting. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The teacher drummed the multiplication tables into the students' heads. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The sales team is working hard to drum up new business opportunities. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The candidate tried to drum up support among undecided voters. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I could hear the rain drumming against the window all night.
- 介词搭配: The sergeant was drummed out of the army for misconduct.
- 介词搭配: She drummed her fingers on the steering wheel while stuck in traffic.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他一边等待一边不耐烦地用手指敲着桌子。
- 老师把乘法表反复灌输到学生的脑子里。
- 销售团队正在努力争取新的商机。
- 该候选人试图在犹豫不决的选民中争取支持。
- 我整夜都能听到雨点敲打窗户的声音。
- 该中士因行为不端被开除出军队。
- 她在塞车时用手指敲着方向盘。
</details>

### 155. dry <a id="155-dry"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English drȳgan ('to dry'), from Proto-Germanic *drūgiz ('dry'). Related to drought and drain.

**词源：** 源自古英语 drȳgan（'使干燥'），源自原始日耳曼语 *drūgiz（'干燥的'）。与 drought 和 drain 有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to become or make something dry; to remove moisture from something
- CN: （使）变干，把……弄干

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: dry one's hands/clothes/dishes — 及物：擦干手/晾干衣服/擦干盘子
- Intransitive: the paint dries quickly — 不及物：油漆干得快
- Phrasal: dry up (disappear, no longer available), dry out (become completely dry), dry off (make or become dry) — 短语：dry up（干涸，枯竭），dry out（变干），dry off（弄干）
- Related adjective: dry — 相关形容词：dry

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dry one's hands
- dry clothes
- dry the dishes
- dry one's eyes/tears
- dry out
- dry up
- dry off
- dry by the sun
- dry with a towel
- dry naturally

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 擦干手
- 晾干衣服
- 擦干盘子
- 擦干眼泪
- 变干
- 干涸；枯竭
- 弄干
- 晒干
- 用毛巾擦干
- 自然晾干
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dry with a cloth/towel
- dry by the fire/sun
- dry in the open air
- dry on a line/rack

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用布/毛巾擦干
- 用火/太阳烤干/晒干
- 在露天晾干
- 挂在绳子/架子上晾干
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: After washing the dishes, she dried them with a clean towel. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The river dried up completely during the summer drought. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The paint should be allowed to dry for at least 24 hours before applying a second coat. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The lake dried out over centuries, leaving behind vast salt flats. (Nature 2022)
- Common: He dried off with a towel after swimming in the pool.
- 介词搭配: Let the dishes dry on the rack before putting them away.
- 介词搭配: Her creativity seemed to have dried up after years of working in the same style.
</details>

- 这个湖泊经过几个世纪干涸了，留下广阔的盐滩。
- 他在游泳池游泳后用毛巾擦干了身体。
- 让盘子在架上晾干后再收起来。

</details>

### 156. dub <a id="156-dub"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English dubben ('to make a knight'), from Old French adouber ('to equip, arm, knight'). The film sense 'provide alternative soundtrack' dates from the 1930s.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 dubben（'封为骑士'），源自古法语 adouber（'装备，武装，封为骑士'）。电影意义'配音'可追溯至20世纪30年代。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give someone a name or nickname; to knight someone; to provide a film or TV show with a soundtrack in a different language
- CN: 给……起绰号；封……为爵士；为（电影、电视节目）配音

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Naming: dub sb a nickname / dub sb as sth — 命名：给某人起绰号/称某人为……
- Royalty: dub sb a knight — 皇室：封某人为爵士
- Film: dub a movie into another language — 电影：为电影配音成另一种语言
- Past participle: dubbed — 过去分词：dubbed

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dub a movie
- dub into
- dub a knight
- dub sb the winner
- dub sb as
- dubbed by critics
- dubbed from the original
- dub in several languages

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 为电影配音
- 配音成
- 封某人为爵士
- 称某人为赢家
- 把某人称为
- 被评论家称为
- 从原版配音
- 用多种语言配音
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dub sb/sth sth
- dub a film into French/Spanish
- dub as a nickname
- dub by the media

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 称某人/某物为……
- 把电影配音成法语/西班牙语
- 作为绰号
- 被媒体称为
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The film was dubbed into several languages for international release. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The media dubbed the young actress the 'next big star' of Hollywood. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many foreign films are dubbed rather than subtitled for the local audience. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The economist was dubbed the 'prophet of the financial crisis' by the press. (Nature 2022)
- Common: The king dubbed him a knight for his bravery in battle.
- 介词搭配: He was dubbed 'the genius' by his classmates.
- 介词搭配: The anime was dubbed from Japanese into English.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这部电影被配音成多种语言以便在国际发行。
- 媒体称这位年轻女演员为好莱坞'下一个巨星'。
- 许多外国电影为当地观众配音而非加字幕。
- 这位经济学家被媒体称为金融危机的'预言家'。
- 国王因他在战斗中的英勇表现封他为骑士。
- 他被同学们称为'天才'。
- 这部动漫从日语配音成了英语。
</details>

### 157. duck <a id="157-duck"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English douken ('to dive, duck'), from Old English *dūcan ('to dive, bend down'), from Proto-Germanic *dūkaną. Related to German tauchen ('to dive').

**词源：** 源自中古英语 douken（'潜入，闪避'），源自古英语 *dūcan（'潜入，弯腰'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dūkaną。与德语 tauchen（'潜水'）有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to lower the head or body quickly to avoid something; to avoid a responsibility or difficult situation
- CN: 低下头，弯下腰躲闪；回避，逃避（责任等）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: duck behind/under/into — 具体：躲到后面/下面/里面
- Figurative: duck a question/duty/issue — 比喻：回避问题/责任/议题
- Sports: duck a punch — 体育：躲闪一拳
- Phrasal: duck out of (avoid doing) — 短语：duck out of（逃避）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- duck the question
- duck behind
- duck under
- duck down
- duck out of
- duck a duty
- duck one's head
- duck from
- duck into

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 回避问题
- 躲到后面
- 躲到下面
- 蹲下
- 逃避
- 逃避责任
- 低下头
- 躲避
- 躲进
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- duck behind a wall/tree
- duck under a table
- duck out of a commitment
- duck into a shop/doorway
- duck from a blow

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 躲到墙/树后
- 躲到桌子下
- 逃避承诺
- 躲进商店/门口
- 躲开一击
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He ducked into a shop to avoid the sudden rain. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Politicians who duck difficult questions often lose public trust. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The player ducked to avoid the ball that was flying toward his head. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The report criticizes companies that duck their environmental responsibilities. (Nature 2022)
- Common: She ducked behind the sofa so her brother wouldn't see her.
- 介词搭配: He ducked out of the meeting early claiming he had an emergency.
- 介词搭配: I had to duck my head to get through the low doorway.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 回避棘手问题的政客往往会失去公众信任。
- 选手低下头躲开飞向头部的球。
- 报告批评了逃避环境责任的公司。
- 她躲到沙发后面，这样她哥哥就看不到她了。
- 他借口有急事提前溜出了会议。
- 我不得不低下头才能穿过低矮的门洞。
</details>

### 158. dump <a id="158-dump"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English dumpen, dompen ('to fall, throw down'), from Old Norse dumpa ('to thump, strike'). The sense 'discard waste' developed in the 19th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 dumpen, dompen（'落下，扔下'），源自古诺尔斯语 dumpa（'重击，撞击'）。'倾倒垃圾'之义于19世纪发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to get rid of something you no longer want; to put something down carelessly; to end a romantic relationship with someone
- CN: 倾倒，丢弃；乱放；抛弃（恋人）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Waste: dump garbage/rubbish/chemicals — 垃圾：倾倒垃圾/废物/化学品
- Informal: dump sb (end a relationship) — 非正式：甩了某人（结束恋情）
- Business: dump goods/products (sell at very low prices) — 商业：倾销商品
- Computing: dump data/files — 计算机：转储数据/文件
- Related noun: dump (landfill); dumping — 相关名词：dump（垃圾场）；dumping（倾销）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dump garbage
- dump waste
- dump chemicals
- dump data
- dump sb
- dump a project
- dump into
- dump on
- dump from
- dump in a landfill

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 倾倒垃圾
- 倾倒废物
- 倾倒化学品
- 转储数据
- 甩了某人
- 放弃项目
- 倾倒入
- 倾倒到……上
- 从……倾倒
- 填埋
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dump sth in/on/into
- dump on the ground
- dump into the river/sea
- dump from a truck

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 把某物倒在/扔在……里/上
- 倒在地面上
- 倾倒入河流/大海
- 从卡车上倾倒
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Factory waste should not be dumped directly into rivers. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The company was fined for illegally dumping toxic waste. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many developed countries dump their electronic waste in developing nations. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The study reveals that millions of tons of plastic are dumped into the ocean each year. (Nature 2023)
- Common: He dumped his old clothes at the charity donation bin.
- 介词搭配: She dumped all the papers on the desk and left.
- 介词搭配: The truck dumped its load of sand at the construction site.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 工厂废物不应直接倾倒入河流。
- 该公司因非法倾倒有毒废物而被罚款。
- 许多发达国家将电子垃圾倾倒在发展中国家。
- 研究显示每年有数百万吨塑料被倾倒进海洋。
- 他把旧衣服扔进了慈善捐赠箱。
- 她把所有文件都扔在桌子上就离开了。
- 卡车在建筑工地倾倒了沙子。
</details>

### 159. duplicate <a id="159-duplicate"></a>

**Etymology:** From Latin duplicatus, past participle of duplicare ('to double'), from duplic- ('twofold'), from duo ('two') + plicare ('to fold').

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 duplicatus，duplicare（'加倍，重复'）的过去分词，源自 duplic-（'两倍的'），源自 duo（'二'） + plicare（'折叠'）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to copy something exactly; to do something again, often unnecessarily
- CN: 复制，复印；重复（尤指不必要地）

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical: duplicate a key/document — 具体：复制钥匙/复印文件
- Business: duplicate an effort (waste effort by repeating) — 商业：重复努力（浪费精力重复做某事）
- Science: duplicate an experiment/result — 科学：重复实验/重现结果
- Related noun: duplicate; duplication — 相关名词：duplicate（副本）；duplication（重复，复制）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- duplicate a key
- duplicate a document
- duplicate an effort
- duplicate a result
- duplicate data
- duplicate the success of
- duplicate exactly
- duplicate by
- duplicate from

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 配钥匙
- 复印文件
- 重复劳动
- 重现结果
- 复制数据
- 复制……的成功
- 精确复制
- 通过……复制
- 从……复制而来
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- duplicate from an original
- duplicate by photocopying
- duplicate in triplicate
- duplicate without permission

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从原件复制
- 通过影印复制
- 一式三份地复印
- 未经许可复制
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Please duplicate this document and distribute it to all team members. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The research team was able to duplicate the experimental results in a second trial. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students are warned not to duplicate other people's work without proper citation. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The study's findings could not be duplicated by independent laboratories. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I need to duplicate the house key for my roommate.
- 介词搭配: The report was duplicated from an earlier version without any updates.
- 介词搭配: We should avoid duplicating efforts across different departments.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 请复印这份文件并分发给所有团队成员。
- 研究团队在第二次试验中成功重现了实验结果。
- 警告学生不要在没有适当引用的情况下抄袭他人的作品。
- 该研究的发现无法被独立实验室重现。
- 我需要给室友配一把房子钥匙。
- 这份报告是从早期版本复制而来，没有任何更新。
- 各部门之间应避免重复劳动。
</details>

### 160. dwarf <a id="160-dwarf"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dweorg, dwerh ('dwarf'), from Proto-Germanic *dwergaz. The verb meaning 'make look small by comparison' dates from the late 16th century.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dweorg, dwerh（'侏儒'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dwergaz。动词'使相形见绌'可追溯至16世纪末。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something seem small or unimportant by comparison; to cause to appear tiny
- CN: 使相形见绌；使……显得矮小

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Physical size: the skyscraper dwarfs all other buildings — 物理尺寸：摩天大楼使所有其他建筑相形见绌
- Figurative: the success dwarfs previous achievements — 比喻：成功使之前的成就相形见绌
- Often used in passive: be dwarfed by — 常用于被动语态：be dwarfed by

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dwarf the competition
- dwarf other buildings
- be dwarfed by
- dwarf in comparison
- dwarf everything else
- dwarf with respect to

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使竞争对手相形见绌
- 使其他建筑显得矮小
- 相形见绌
- 相比之下显得矮小
- 使其他一切相形见绌
- 在……方面相形见绌
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dwarf sb/sth by comparison
- be dwarfed by sth
- dwarf in comparison to/with

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 相比之下使某人/某物相形见绌
- 被某物比下去
- 与……相比相形见绌
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The new skyscraper dwarfs all the surrounding buildings. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: China's economic growth dwarfs that of most other nations. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The Grand Canyon dwarfs every other canyon in terms of sheer scale. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The magnitude of the climate crisis dwarfs all other environmental challenges. (Nature 2023)
- Common: When you stand next to him, you feel dwarfed by his height.
- 介词搭配: The problems of developed countries are dwarfed by those facing the developing world.
- 介词搭配: The old church is dwarfed by the massive modern office buildings around it.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 新建的摩天大楼使周围所有建筑相形见绌。
- 中国的经济增长使大多数其他国家相形见绌。
- 就规模而言，大峡谷使所有其他峡谷相形见绌。
- 气候危机的规模使所有其他环境挑战相形见绌。
- 站在他旁边时，你会因他的身高而觉得自己矮小。
- 发达国家的问题与发展中国家面临的问题相比微不足道。
- 古老的教堂被周围现代化的巨型办公大楼显得非常矮小。
</details>

### 161. dwell <a id="161-dwell"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English dwellan ('to mislead, deceive, hinder'), from Proto-Germanic *dwaljaną ('to delay, hesitate'). The meaning 'live in a place' developed in Middle English.

**词源：** 源自古英语 dwellan（'误导，欺骗，阻碍'），源自原始日耳曼语 *dwaljaną（'延迟，犹豫'）。'居住'之义在中古英语中发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to live in a particular place (formal); to think or speak about something for a long time (usually with 'on/upon')
- CN: 居住（正式用语）；细想，详述（常与 'on/upon' 连用）

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal: dwell in a place — 正式：居住在某个地方
- Common phrase: dwell on/upon sth (think or talk about at length) — 常见短语：dwell on/upon sth（细想，反复谈论）
- Negative connotation when dwelling on past: don't dwell on the past — 纠结过去时含负面意味：不要沉湎于过去
- Related noun: dweller (inhabitant), dwelling (home) — 相关名词：dweller（居民），dwelling（住所）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dwell on/upon
- dwell in
- dwell in the past
- dwell at length
- dwell on a topic
- dwell among
- dwell in a place
- dwell in the mind

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 细想；详述
- 居住在
- 沉湎于过去
- 详细讨论
- 纠结某个话题
- 生活在……中
- 居住在某个地方
- 萦绕在心头
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dwell on the details/past
- dwell in a city/forest
- dwell upon a subject
- dwell among the people

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 纠结于细节/过去
- 居住在城市/森林里
- 详述一个话题
- 生活在人们中间
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Try not to dwell too much on past failures — focus on the future instead. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The speaker dwelled at length on the importance of education reform. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The indigenous people who dwell in this region have a rich cultural heritage. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The article dwells on the ethical implications of artificial intelligence. (Nature 2022)
- Common: There's no point dwelling on things you can't change.
- 介词搭配: She dwells in a small village in the mountains.
- 介词搭配: Don't dwell on the mistake — learn from it and move on.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 尽量不要过多纠结于过去的失败——要着眼于未来。
- 演讲者详细阐述了教育改革的重要性。
- 居住在这个地区的土著人民拥有丰富的文化遗产。
- 文章详细讨论了人工智能的伦理影响。
- 纠结于无法改变的事情毫无意义。
- 她居住在山中的一个小村庄里。
- 不要纠结于错误——从中学习然后继续前进。
</details>

### 162. dye <a id="162-dye"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English deien, from Old English dēagian, dēgan ('to dye, color'), from Proto-Germanic *daugijaną. Related to Old High German tougen ('to color').

**词源：** 源自中古英语 deien，源自古英语 dēagian, dēgan（'染色，着色'），源自原始日耳曼语 *daugijaną。与古高地德语 tougen（'着色'）有关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to change the color of something, especially by using a special liquid or substance
- CN: 给……染色，着色

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Hair: dye one's hair blonde/black/red — 头发：把头发染成金色/黑色/红色
- Fabric: dye cloth/wool/fabric — 织物：染布/羊毛/织物
- Note: "die" (死亡) is a different word — 注意："die"（死亡）是不同的单词，拼写不同
- Related noun: dye; dyer — 相关名词：dye（染料）；dyer（染工）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- dye one's hair
- dye a fabric
- dye cloth
- dye with
- dye a dress
- dye the wool
- dye by hand
- dye in colors
- dye to a shade

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 染头发
- 染布料
- 染布
- 用……染色
- 染裙子
- 染羊毛
- 手工染色
- 染成某种颜色
- 染成某种色调
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- dye sth a color
- dye with natural pigments
- dye by hand/machine
- dye to a specific shade

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 把某物染成某种颜色
- 用天然颜料染色
- 手工/机器染色
- 染成特定色调
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She decided to dye her hair brown for a change. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Natural materials were used to dye fabrics in ancient times. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The traditional method of dyeing cloth with indigo has been practiced for centuries. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The researchers developed a new technique to dye cotton without using toxic chemicals. (Science 2022)
- Common: I need to dye this old curtain — it's faded from the sun.
- 介词搭配: The fabric was dyed with natural plant extracts.
- 介词搭配: She dyed her dress a deep shade of blue.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她决定把头发染成棕色换个风格。
- 古代使用天然材料给织物染色。
- 用靛蓝染布的传统方法已经实践了几个世纪。
- 研究人员开发了一种不使用有毒化学物质染棉的新技术。
- 我需要染一下这旧窗帘——它被太阳晒褪色了。
- 这块布料是用天然植物提取物染色的。
- 她把裙子染成了深蓝色。
</details>
